# Comprehensive Local Repository Architecture and Safety Audit

_This report was produced by a multi-agent audit workflow driven by `docs/milestones/review.md`.
The workflow ran the Inventory, Find, and Verify phases to completion and was intentionally stopped before the Critic and full Synthesis phases; findings below are the machine-generated, adversarially verified output. Every survived finding was reviewed by 3 independent verifiers (code-refute / reachability / severity lenses) with majority voting; findings refuted by >=2 verifiers were dropped._

## 1. Executive summary

**Overall architecture assessment.** The repository is a research + paper-trading system with a deliberately narrow "domain owns everything" architecture: a Python trading-research package (`src/trading_research/`) that owns eligibility, risk, cash, positions, evidence, orchestration, scheduling, and the external Alpaca-paper boundary; an isolated LumiBot/Alpaca child process (`paper_runtime/`) accessed only over stdin/stdout JSON Lines; and a read-only Streamlit dashboard. Live trading is structurally unavailable (single `Disabled` gateway + hard-coded `live_trading_enabled=false`, `allow_live_promotion=false`, `allow_enhanced_submission=false`).

**Strongest design decisions.**

- Provider-integration isolation via a subprocess boundary (`paper_runtime` speaks JSON Lines over stdio).
- Multiple invariants defended by both configuration defaults *and* domain-boundary checks (e.g. limit-only order type, no-short/no-margin, $50 single-trade limit).
- Explicit UTC-day accounting for external-paper reservations with recent hardening (external-paper reservation upgrade + recovery merged just before this audit).
- Point-in-time discipline: recent commits align event timestamps to exchange-session boundaries and separate strategy content identity from evaluation identity.
- Deterministic Python scoring scripts and read-only dashboard.

**Most serious residual risks (from verified findings, ranked).**

- Several **High** severity findings involve failure paths in reservation/settlement, migration-safety of active state, and pre-research vs final-execution risk enforcement asymmetries. See findings F-001..F-011.
- Broad **Medium** cluster around: concurrency races on SQLite when multiple workers share a connection, INSERT OR IGNORE hiding conflicts, and CI/lint/security-check coverage gaps.
- Documentation drift: several claims in README / milestone docs reference behaviors that either changed or are enforced only in specific entry points.

**Current readiness level.** Deterministic offline strategies, historical backtesting, daily read-only candidates, and the local paper book are READY subject to noted follow-ups. Supervised external paper submission is ALLOWED but with High-severity items to address. Automated paper scheduling and live trading remain (correctly) DISABLED.

**Recommended engineering focus.**

1. Close the High-severity findings first (see remediation sequence).
2. Add crash-point and cross-day regression tests for external-paper reservation & settlement flows.
3. Reconcile documentation with code (README claims vs. current enforcement locations).
4. Tighten CI: pinned actions, required security checks, explicit exclusion of broker/provider markers.

## 3. Architecture map

**Summary.** Local-first research + paper-trading Python system. Two Python packages: (a) `src/trading_research/` (main desk; deterministic Python that owns eligibility, risk, cash, positions, evidence, research orchestration, scheduling, shadow ops, external Alpaca-paper boundary) and (b) `paper_runtime/src/trading_paper_runtime/` (an isolated LumiBot/Alpaca child process spoken to via paper-runtime.v2 JSON Lines over stdio - the only place that imports LumiBot/alpaca-py). A `src/dashboard/` read-only Streamlit app renders SQLite state. Live trading is structurally unavailable (single Disabled gateway; hard-coded `live_trading_enabled: false`, `allow_live_promotion: false`, `allow_enhanced_submission: false`; every capability defaults disabled and no env var can flip a capability).

**Entry points.**

- src/trading_research/cli.py:2542 main() - argparse dispatcher; subcommands include analyze, paper-status, execute-paper, paper-runtime-health, sync-paper-orders, reconcile-paper, evaluate, build-evidence, run-research, replay-research, run-research-cycle, resume-research-cycle, evaluate-research-cycle, compare-research-cycles, research-promotion-status, run-due-shadow-cycle, shadow-status/-readiness/-pause/-resume/-kill/-alerts, paper-book-list/-show/-snapshot/-run-cycle/-reconcile, paper-experiment-compare, paper-promotion-status, paper-book-integrate-cycle, paper-book-lifecycle-run, paper-book-exit-request, paper-soak-*, paper-recurring-*, external-paper-account-check/-preview/-submit/-reconcile/-cancel/-retry-submit/-order-show/-queue-show, corporate-status, retention-plan/-apply, claude-code-provider-preflight, codex-provider-preflight, research-usage/-failures/-failure-metrics, provider-health, evidence-provider-usage, fetch-evidence, compare-research-arms
- src/dashboard/streamlit_app.py - Streamlit navigation over pages: overview, decisions, research_cycles, portfolio, system_health
- scripts/run_dashboard.sh / stop_dashboard.sh / tailscale_serve_dashboard.sh - dashboard lifecycle wrappers binding to 127.0.0.1
- scripts/indicators.py, scripts/score.py, scripts/macro_pillar.py - deterministic scoring scripts (CLAUDE.md-mandated computation path)
- scripts/submit_batch.py, scripts/download_batch.py, scripts/check_batch.py, scripts/milestone_batch.py - Anthropic Message Batch submission helpers using `anthropic.Anthropic()`
- scripts/inventory_mcp_tools.py, scripts/investigate_reddit_free.py - MCP inventory and Reddit RSS smoke
- paper_runtime/src/trading_paper_runtime/__main__.py -> main.py:run() - stdin/stdout JSON Lines child process runnable via `python -m trading_paper_runtime`; loaded from config/paper_runtime.yaml (command: python3 -m trading_paper_runtime)
- deploy/launchd/*.example - macOS launchd job artifacts for run-due-shadow-cycle and paper-recurring; all .example suffix, StartCalendarInterval commented out, RunAtLoad=false
- .github/workflows/ci.yml - single CI workflow

**Domain boundaries.**

- research/  - LLM-facing research orchestration; only place that imports `anthropic` (research/anthropic_provider.py:22) and drives subprocess Claude Code/Codex; provider_protocol.ResearchModelProvider abstracts, everything else depends on the protocol not the SDK (research/anthropic_provider.py header)
- evidence_providers/ - real point-in-time data ingestion (SEC, Alpaca market_data, Alpaca News, sentiment/Reddit paid, reddit_free RSS, economic_calendar, fundamentals, corporate_actions/status, filing_documents); http_client + rate_limits + cache; disabled-by-default via config/evidence_providers.yaml
- execution/ - paper-execution boundary; live_gateway.py ships only DisabledLiveExecutionGateway (raises LiveTradingDisabledError unconditionally); eligibility, intent_builder, reconciliation, account_reconciliation, adapter_protocol, broker_snapshots, ledger_events
- paper/ - simulated paper ledger (paper/ledger.py: T+1 settled cash, spread+slippage fills, idempotent orders)
- paper_books/ - isolated multi-book paper subsystem (Milestone 8+): cash_ledger, positions, valuation, risk, order_intent, execution, reconciliation, exit_policy, safety_pause, daily_risk, corporate_actions, external_broker (Alpaca paper coordinator), experiment_assignment, promotion_evidence, lifecycle/lifecycle_state, soak_campaign, controlled_soak_readiness, cross_book_verification, recurring_scheduler, scheduled_integration
- backtesting/ - engine.py + configuration + data_provider + reports (deterministic offline backtests)
- evaluation/ - forward-performance evaluation of persisted recommendations vs market_calendar/price_provider; metrics, calibration, time_to_fill, turnover, research_comparison
- shadow/ - shadow-operations single-invocation scheduler entry (scheduler.run_due_shadow_cycle); pause/kill, budget, role_budget, lease, readiness, health, health_hysteresis, retention, alerts, attempt_controller, model_provider_health
- mcp/ - MCP capability inventory and mock adapters (mock_adapters.py used by CLI analyze default)
- strategies/ - deterministic strategy scanner (momentum_breakout, mean_reversion, event_catalyst); safety_gates, selector, execution_boundary, backtest_adapter
- analysis/ - indicators, scorer, screener, sentiment (VADER), ticker_extractor
- recommendations/ - builder for frozen recommendation records
- risk/ - position_sizing (RiskInputs, compute_position_plan)
- universe/ - static ticker universe
- collection/ - prompt_injection_filter for LLM-facing inputs
- runtime/ - client + lumibot in-process adapter + deterministic_adapter; runtime/client/process_client.py talks to the isolated paper_runtime child; runtime/lumibot/ is the legacy in-process LumiBot adapter (isolated behind optional `paper` extra per pyproject)
- services/ - application-layer orchestrators: analyze_candidate, execute_paper_recommendation, submit_credentialed_paper_order, reconcile_paper, sync_paper_orders
- storage/ - SQLite schemas and repositories (see persistence below)
- paper_runtime (separate package `trading-paper-runtime`) - the only process that imports lumibot/alpaca-py; dispatcher.py routes RequestEnvelope to BrokerGateway (broker_gateway.py Protocol) implemented by lumibot_gateway.py (LumiBotAlpacaPaperGateway) or deterministic_gateway.py

**Persistence.**

- Single SQLite DB opened via src/trading_research/storage/database.py:connect - isolation_level=None, WAL, foreign_keys=ON, busy_timeout=5000ms; forward-schema check via schema_version.check_schema_not_forward_versioned; idempotent `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` (no formal migration tool)
- storage/trading_schema.py: securities, price_bars, fundamentals, corporate_events, earnings_calendar, sec_filings, news_items, reddit_posts, reddit_comments, reddit_ticker_mentions, screening_runs, candidate_scores, recommendations, recommendation_factors, model_versions, prompt_versions, simulated_orders, simulated_fills, simulated_positions, simulated_portfolio_snapshots, approvals, real_orders, evaluation_results, benchmark_results, agent_runs, tool_calls, errors
- storage/execution_schema.py: paper_execution_intents, paper_execution_events, paper_execution_results, paper_execution_reconciliations, paper_execution_failures, paper_broker_submissions, paper_account_reconciliations, paper_position_reconciliations
- storage/research_schema.py: research_evidence_snapshots, research_committee_runs, research_attempts, research_role_reports, research_decisions, research_overlay_decisions, research_experiment_assignments, research_failures, research_attempt_failures
- storage/research_cycle_schema.py: research_cycles, research_cycle_symbol_results, research_cycle_symbol_evidence_status, research_cycle_provider_provenance(_links)

- storage/shadow_alerts_schema.py: shadow-alert tables (22 CREATE TABLE/INDEX statements)
- storage/paper_books_schema.py (largest, 91 CREATE statements): paper_books, paper_book_cash_ledger, paper_book_risk_decisions, paper_book_orders, paper_book_fills, paper_book_positions, paper_book_position_lots, paper_book_snapshots(+positions), paper_book_reconciliations, paper_book_daily_metrics, paper_book_corporate_actions_applied, paper_book_experiment_assignments, paper_book_experiment_comparisons, paper_book_promotion_evidence, paper_book_exit_decisions, paper_book_manual_exit_requests, paper_book_lifecycle_runs(+symbol_results), paper_soak_operator_runs, paper_book_cross_book_verifications(+checks), paper_soak_campaigns(+days,+definition_dates,+attempts,+attempt_days), paper_soak_activation_reviews, paper_recurring_activation_events, paper_recurring_cycle_queue, paper_recurring_scheduler_leases/runs, paper_external_order_previews, paper_external_order_events, paper_external_broker_fills, paper_external_order_lookups, paper_external_reconciliations(+baseline), paper_external_submission_queue, paper_external_position_reservation_events, paper_external_order_leases, paper_order_execution_claims, paper_book_daily_risk_states, paper_book_position_lifecycle_states, paper_book_lifecycle_state_events, paper_book_partial_exit_stages, economic_calendar_events, economic_blackout_decisions, paper_book_safety_events, paper_external_daily_reservations, paper_external_attempt_reservations, backtest_runs, backtest_daily_states, backtest_orders, backtest_fills, backtest_metrics, paper_books_trigger_versions
- storage/migrations.py (bootstrap): research_runs, capability_inventories, source_records, batch_jobs, batch_requests, batch_results, claims, research_recommendations, conflicts, errors, generated_artifacts
- storage/schema_version.py: schema_version table + apply_pending_schema_migrations
- storage/transactions.py: begin_immediate / transaction context managers - explicit BEGIN IMMEDIATE only

**Model-call boundaries.**

- src/trading_research/research/anthropic_provider.py - the ONLY file importing the `anthropic` SDK inside the research layer (module docstring line 2-6); uses forced tool_use for structured JSON; PROVIDER_NAME implicitly via provider protocol
- src/trading_research/research/claude_code_provider.py - subscription-OAuth Claude Code CLI provider; invokes absolute /opt/homebrew/bin/claude via BoundedProcessRunner with shell=False, sanitized env allowlist (HOME/USER/LOGNAME/TMPDIR/LANG/LC_ALL), stdin-only prompt, no tools, MINIMUM_SUPPORTED_VERSION=(2,1,205); revalidates output against schema
- src/trading_research/research/codex_provider.py - locked-down Codex CLI provider (cached ChatGPT auth via `codex login status`; never uses OPENAI_API_KEY); reasoning_output_tokens Policy A
- src/trading_research/research/deterministic_provider.py + openai_compatible_provider.py + fixtures.py - offline/deterministic providers used by default and in tests
- src/trading_research/research/provider_protocol.py - ResearchModelProvider Protocol every other research module depends on
- src/trading_research/research/bounded_subprocess.py - subprocess sandbox for claude_code/codex
- src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py, scheduled_cycle.py - sequence provider calls per role/symbol
- src/trading_research/research/model_provider_health_policy.py + shadow/model_provider_health.py + failure_metrics/failure_taxonomy - failure classification and retry policy
- scripts/submit_batch.py, download_batch.py, check_batch.py, milestone_batch.py - Anthropic Message Batch API (`anthropic.Anthropic()`); note: research layer is separate from these batch scripts

**Broker-call boundaries.**

- paper_runtime/src/trading_paper_runtime/lumibot_gateway.py:LumiBotAlpacaPaperGateway - the ONLY module that constructs `lumibot.brokers.Alpaca` and drives alpaca-py `TradingClient` (broker.api); every mutating op requires is_paper_mode_verified()=True or fails with NOT_PAPER_MODE (dispatcher.py header)
- paper_runtime/src/trading_paper_runtime/broker_gateway.py - BrokerGateway Protocol; two implementations: LumiBotAlpacaPaperGateway (real) and DeterministicBrokerGateway (deterministic_gateway.py, offline in-memory)
- paper_runtime/src/trading_paper_runtime/dispatcher.py - routes RequestEnvelope; health/capabilities do not require paper-mode; everything else fails NOT_PAPER_MODE if unverified
- src/trading_research/runtime/client/process_client.py + protocol.py - the main-process side of the paper-runtime.v2 stdio JSON Lines protocol; talks to the isolated child (no LumiBot import; runtime_client_no_lumibot_import test guards this)
- src/trading_research/runtime/lumibot/adapter.py + event_mapper.py + configuration.py - legacy in-process LumiBot adapter (guarded by optional `paper` extra in pyproject.toml)
- src/trading_research/execution/live_gateway.py - `LiveExecutionGateway` Protocol; only shipped impl is `DisabledLiveExecutionGateway` whose every method raises LiveTradingDisabledError; module comment states no live implementation exists in the codebase
- src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py - Manual coordinator for the isolated Alpaca PAPER account (external-paper boundary); enforces provider=='alpaca_paper' and environment=='paper' on every runtime result (lines 616-617, 704, 960, 1129, 2209); POLICY_VERSION='external-alpaca-paper-v1'; never imports Alpaca directly - only speaks to the runtime child
- src/trading_research/services/submit_credentialed_paper_order.py - service that owns the async credentialed paper-broker submission acknowledgement (creates PENDING_SUBMISSION row before contacting runtime; idempotent get_order check)
- src/trading_research/services/execute_paper_recommendation.py - deterministic in-process/paper-adapter orchestrator (Milestone 3)
- src/trading_research/services/sync_paper_orders.py, reconcile_paper.py - polling for fills and account reconciliation

**External-data boundaries.**

- src/trading_research/evidence_providers/sec_provider.py - SEC EDGAR (config/evidence_providers.yaml providers.sec.enabled=true; user-agent required; no credentials)
- src/trading_research/evidence_providers/market_data_provider.py - Alpaca market data (provider='alpaca', disabled by default; uses ALPACA_MARKET_DATA_API_KEY/SECRET, deliberately distinct from ALPACA_API_KEY/SECRET which are read only by the isolated paper_runtime - config.py:43-47)
- src/trading_research/evidence_providers/alpaca_news_provider.py + news_provider.py - Alpaca News API (disabled default; requires ALPACA_MARKET_DATA_API_KEY/SECRET; providers.news.enabled=false)
- src/trading_research/evidence_providers/sentiment_provider.py - paid Reddit sentiment (requires REDDIT_CLIENT_ID/SECRET; providers.sentiment.enabled=false)
- src/trading_research/evidence_providers/reddit_free.py + reddit_fetch.py - credential-free Reddit Atom RSS (providers.reddit_free.enabled=true; subreddits wallstreetbets/stocks/investing)
- src/trading_research/evidence_providers/economic_calendar.py - economic events; drives economic_blackout_decisions in paper_books/daily_risk.py
- src/trading_research/evidence_providers/corporate_actions.py + corporate_status.py + corporate_status_adapters.py - SEC-derived corporate events/status
- src/trading_research/evidence_providers/fundamentals.py, operating_history.py, filing_documents.py, disclosure_extraction.py - fundamentals + filing extraction
- src/trading_research/evidence_providers/http_client.py + rate_limits.py + cache.py + health.py + errors.py + fixture_clients.py - shared http infra (httpx-based) with per-provider rate limiting, in-memory cache, health snapshotting, fail-closed errors, fixture clients for offline tests
- src/trading_research/evidence_providers/evidence_adapters.py + persistence.py + portfolio_context.py - normalize provider output into research pipeline
- src/trading_research/mcp/reddit_adapter.py + robinhood_inventory.py + mock_adapters.py - MCP client wrappers; robinhood_tools_snapshot.json is the frozen capability inventory; CLI `analyze` defaults to MockRedditAdapter+MockRobinhoodAdapter (cli.py:29)

**Feature flags.**

- config/execution.yaml: trading_mode='paper' (only paper implemented), live_trading_enabled=false (hard-coded, header comment), human_approval_required=true, kill_switch_enabled=false (global paper-execution kill), recommendation_ttl_minutes=60, max_price_staleness_seconds=900
- config/research.yaml: research.enabled=false, provider='deterministic' (deterministic|scripted|anthropic|claude_code|codex), model=null; require_ready fails closed if provider=anthropic and model unset; presence of ANTHROPIC_API_KEY never flips enabled (docstring)
- config/evidence_providers.yaml: every provider disabled by default; sec enabled=true (no key); news provider=null; sentiment disabled; reddit_free enabled=true
- config/shadow_operations.yaml: shadow_operations.enabled=false, schedule.enabled=false, allow_baseline_paper_submission=false, allow_enhanced_submission=false (structurally impossible to be true per shadow/config.py __post_init__)
- config/paper_books.yaml: paper_books.enabled=false, scheduled_integration.enabled=false, soak_campaign.enabled=false, recurring.enabled=false; execution.allow_live_broker structurally impossible
- config/paper_runtime.yaml: paper_broker.mode='paper' only, real_money_enabled=false, base_url=https://paper-api.alpaca.markets, allow_shorting/allow_margin/allow_fractional/allow_extended_hours=false, allowed_sides=[BUY,SELL], allowed_order_types=[LIMIT] only
- config/scheduled_research.yaml: scheduled_research.enabled=false, submit_paper_orders=false, promotion.enabled=false, promotion.allow_live_promotion=false (research/promotion.py::PromotionGateConfig raises if true)
- config/strategies.yaml: strategy_candidate_selection.enabled=false, activation_stage=STAGE_2_HISTORICAL_BACKTEST (7-stage rollout; enabled cannot be true at stage 1/2 - loader enforced)
- config/production/ - production overlays for research, research-codex, scheduled_research, shadow_operations
- No environment variable can flip any capability (all yaml headers assert this); env vars supply credentials only (see .env.example convention)

**Activation controls.**

- 7-stage strategy activation ladder in config/strategies.yaml (STAGE_1_OFFLINE_FIXTURES..STAGE_7_MULTI_DAY_PAPER_SOAK)
- paper_books recurring lifecycle requires: config gate + immutable ready review + explicit activation request + separate activation event (config/paper_books.yaml:36-40)
- External Alpaca-paper submission requires explicit operator sequence: external-paper-account-check -> external-paper-activate-baseline -> external-paper-preview -> external-paper-submit (cli.py subcommands + paper_books/external_broker.py enforcement of provider=='alpaca_paper', environment=='paper')
- Shadow launchd artifact deploy/launchd/*.example - .example suffix, RunAtLoad=false, StartCalendarInterval commented out; explicit operator step required
- Research provider selection requires research.provider=<name> AND research.model set (require_ready in research/configuration.py per config header)
- Promotion gate: research/promotion.py::PromotionGateConfig raises if allow_live_promotion=true; scheduled_research.yaml keeps it hard-coded false

**Authoritative risk checks.**

- src/trading_research/execution/eligibility.py:evaluate_eligibility - single gate before intent_builder.build_paper_order_intent; INELIGIBLE_SIDES=('watch','no_action','analysis_incomplete','screened_out'); no live-portfolio guardrail wired yet (_null_portfolio_guardrail is default)
- src/trading_research/execution/intent_builder.build_paper_order_intent - deterministic intent from frozen recommendation
- src/trading_research/execution/live_gateway.py:LiveTradingDisabledError - every live method raises unconditionally
- src/trading_research/paper_books/risk.py:evaluate_risk - deterministic per-book risk decisions; reason codes: RISK_REJECTED_BOOK_PAUSED, RISK_REJECTED_ARM_MISMATCH, RISK_REJECTED_DAILY_NOTIONAL_LIMIT, RISK_REJECTED_INSUFFICIENT_CASH, RISK_REJECTED_INVALID_RECOMMENDATION, RISK_REJECTED_MAX_OPEN_POSITIONS, RISK_REJECTED_MAX_POSITION_WEIGHT, RISK_REJECTED_MAX_SYMBOL_CONCENTRATION, RISK_REJECTED_MISSING_PRICE, RISK_REJECTED_STALE_PRICE, RISK_REJECTED_DAILY_LOSS_LIMIT, RISK_REJECTED_DRAWDOWN_LIMIT, RISK_REJECTED_RISK_STATE_UNAVAILABLE/STALE/UNRECONCILED, RISK_REJECTED_ECONOMIC_EVENT_BLACKOUT; policy_version constant enforced
- src/trading_research/paper_books/safety_pause.py:pause_for_risk_state / resume - append-only paper_book_safety_events; is_paused blocks execution while latest state==PAUSED
- src/trading_research/paper_books/daily_risk.py + economic_blackout - consumed by risk.evaluate; drives blackout_decisions
- src/trading_research/paper_books/exit_policy.py - deterministic exit decisions; paper_book_exit_decisions + manual_exit_requests
- src/trading_research/strategies/safety_gates.py + execution_boundary.py - deterministic strategy safety gates
- src/trading_research/research/promotion.py:PromotionGateConfig - raises if allow_live_promotion true; policy_version 'research-promotion.v1'; minimum_completed_evaluations=100, market_regimes>=2, max_provider_failure_rate=0.2, max_unsupported_claim_rate=0.05, etc.
- src/trading_research/shadow/config.py:__post_init__ - raises if allow_enhanced_submission=true (structurally impossible)
- src/trading_research/shadow/pause.py + budget.py + lease.py + role_budget.py + readiness.py - shadow scheduler gates (pause/kill/lease/budget acquired before any Claude call)
- src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py - fails closed with NOT_PAPER_ENDPOINT if runtime does not prove provider='alpaca_paper' AND environment='paper' (lines 616-617); POLICY_VERSION='external-alpaca-paper-v1'
- paper_runtime/dispatcher.py - every mutating op requires gateway.is_paper_mode_verified()=True or NOT_PAPER_MODE
- paper_runtime/lumibot_gateway.py: _ALPACA_STATUS_MAP fails closed on unmapped statuses
- src/trading_research/collection/prompt_injection_filter.py - filters LLM-facing inputs
- src/trading_research/research/output_validation.py + evidence_completeness.py + evidence_validation.py + claim_validation.py - schema-revalidate provider output; require_evidence_for_material_claims per config/research.yaml
- src/trading_research/research/token_budget.py + usage.py + failure_taxonomy.py + failure_metrics.py - bounded token/latency/cost per cycle per config/shadow_operations.yaml budgets block

## 4. Findings summary

_Total verified findings: 96 (severity distribution: {"High": 11, "Medium": 46, "Low": 38, "NeedsVerification": 1}; decisions: {"CONFIRMED": 75, "PLAUSIBLE": 21})._

| ID | Severity | Decision | Area / Dimension | Finding | Capability blocked |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| F-001 | High | CONFIRMED | ARCHITECTURE | Daily research-budget helpers and PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController are implemented and unit-tested but never wired into any production caller | Daily research spend cap enforcement |
| F-002 | High | CONFIRMED | ARCHITECTURE | Required-approval-phrase invariant for paper submission is documented but has no enforcement in code | Documented typed-phrase approval gate |
| F-003 | High | CONFIRMED | BACKTEST-CORRECTNESS | max_daily_loss_fraction gate ignores intraday gap-down losses on held positions | Daily loss-limit enforcement |
| F-004 | High | CONFIRMED | CONCURRENCY-TX | External-paper retry: supersede+new-reservation commit is atomic, but SUBMISSION_REQUESTED commit is a separate transaction — a crash between them strands the order with no recovery path |  |
| F-005 | High | CONFIRMED | CRASH-RECOVERY | Cross-day retry can strand a supersede-committed reservation with no forward path | External-paper retry after crash |
| F-006 | High | CONFIRMED | DOCS-ACCURACY | Documented required approval phrase for paper submission is not enforced in code |  |
| F-007 | High | CONFIRMED | DOCS-ACCURACY | Alpaca-paper runbook and README omit the required external-paper-activate-baseline step; submissions fail closed without it |  |
| F-008 | High | CONFIRMED | MODEL-TOKEN-COST | Persistent daily token-budget subsystem is inert in production — no reservation on any real provider call |  |
| F-009 | High | CONFIRMED | OPERATIONAL-WIRING | PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController is fully implemented but never instantiated by any production caller; the daily research-token cap is only enforced from tests | persistent daily research-token cap |
| F-010 | High | CONFIRMED | PORTFOLIO-CONCENTRATION | max_daily_new_notional_usd is never accumulated across orders (local paper books) |  |
| F-011 | High | CONFIRMED | STRATEGY-CORRECTNESS | event_catalyst content_id changes on every re-evaluation, breaking dedup/reuse |  |
| F-012 | Medium | CONFIRMED | ARCHITECTURE | Two competing paper-intent identity/type systems: `execution/intent_builder.py` still constructs `ORDER_TYPE_MARKET` intents while `paper_books/order_intent.py` builds `ORDER_TYPE_LIMIT` only | Single authoritative intent-building rule |

## 5. Detailed findings

### F-001 [High / CONFIRMED] Daily research-budget helpers and PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController are implemented and unit-tested but never wired into any production caller

- **Area / Dimension.** ARCHITECTURE
- **Affected workflow.** Research reuse / token budget
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/strategies/research_budget.py`, `src/trading_research/research/token_budget.py`, `src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py`, `src/trading_research/research/scheduled_cycle.py`
- **Line ranges.** strategies/research_budget.py:62-98; research/token_budget.py:22-97; research/orchestration.py:445-449, 691-762
- **Observed behavior.**
  Grep across src/ shows `decide_research_action`, `record_research_outcome`, and `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` are only referenced by their own defining modules and tests — no production caller instantiates the token-budget controller or invokes the pre-flight shortlist gate. `run_scheduled_research_cycle` (scheduled_cycle.py:318) and the CLI `run-research` path both call `analyze_with_research_committee` directly, and inside `_run_role_with_retries` the controller invocation is gated on `token_budget_controller is not None` (orchestration.py:447-449), which is always None in production. The docstring of research_budget.py:1-10 asserts a caller that does not exist.
- **Failure scenario.**
  A production scheduled research cycle runs 200 provider calls in a single UTC day; nothing enforces `maximum_symbols_to_research_per_day` / `maximum_fresh_research_cycles_per_day` from config/strategies.yaml, and nothing writes to `shadow_budget_reservations` for research tokens. The persistent daily token cap is therefore only enforced under test.
- **Impact.** The two ostensible daily-spend guardrails on real-money-adjacent LLM calls (candidate cap, token cap) are not actually enforced in production, contrary to the module docstrings and the invariant audit's `strategy_candidate_selection` gating story. Config values give a false sense of enforcement.
- **Why existing tests do not prevent it.** Existing tests directly construct `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` in-line (e.g., tests/unit/test_research_orchestration.py:401) rather than exercising the production wiring, so absence-of-wiring is invisible to unit tests.
- **Recommended correction.** Either wire `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` into `run_scheduled_research_cycle` / `run_due_shadow_cycle` and add a `decide_research_action` pre-flight in the scheduled cycle before calling `analyze_with_research_committee`, or remove the dead modules and document that daily token limits are enforced only by shadow-role budget checks.
- **Recommended regression tests.** Add an integration test that exercises a scheduled cycle end-to-end and asserts that the count of `shadow_budget_reservations` rows with `reservation_kind='RESEARCH_TOKENS'` matches the number of provider invocations.
- **Capability blocked.** Daily research spend cap enforcement
- **Finder confidence.** high
- **Verification.** 3 votes (3 confirmed, 0 plausible, 0 refuted).
  - _severity_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Confirmed the mechanical claim: `decide_research_action`, `record_research_outcome`, and `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` have zero production callers under `src/`; only tests instantiate them. All three production callers of `analyze_with_research_committee` (scheduled_cycle.py:516, cli.py:833, shadow/scheduler.py path) omit `token_budget_controller`, so the `if token_budget_controller i
  - _reachability_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Reachability lens: the finding is about DEAD (not-invoked) production code paths. Verified directly:  1) The two production callers of `analyze_with_research_committee` — `scheduled_cycle.py:516` and `cli.py:833` — omit the `token_budget_controller` kwarg, so at `orchestration.py:447-449` the reservation branch is always short-circuited by `token_budget_controller is not None` (always False in pro
  - _code-refute_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Re-reading the cited files reproduces every part of the finding.  1) `src/trading_research/strategies/research_budget.py:62-98` defines `decide_research_action` and `record_research_outcome`. `grep -R` across the tree shows they are referenced only by `tests/unit/test_research_budget.py` and the defining module itself — no production caller invokes the pre-flight shortlist gate.  2) `src/trading_r

### F-002 [High / CONFIRMED] Required-approval-phrase invariant for paper submission is documented but has no enforcement in code

- **Area / Dimension.** ARCHITECTURE
- **Affected workflow.** External paper submission
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/cli.py`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py`
- **Line ranges.** cli.py:361-372; external_broker.py:1319-1324, 115-119
- **Observed behavior.**
  The invariant audit already lists this as documented-not-enforced. `external_paper_submit_cli` forwards `operator` and `reason` verbatim to `submit_external_paper_order`, which only calls `_bounded(operator, 'operator', 128)` and `_bounded(reason, 'reason', 512)` (external_broker.py:1324, 115-119). No string comparison against the mandated phrase 'Approved, submit this exact paper order.' exists anywhere in src/ (Grep for 'Approved, submit' returns zero hits). The `_bounded` guard accepts any non-empty, length-bounded string.
- **Failure scenario.**
  An operator (or an automation with mistakenly broad permissions) invokes `trading-research external-paper-submit --operator alice --reason foo ...`; the CLI accepts `reason='foo'` and submits the order to the isolated Alpaca-paper account. The intended human-in-the-loop typed-phrase gate is bypassed by any non-empty reason.
- **Impact.** The last defense against accidental submission (a typed phrase specifically chosen to be improbable to type by mistake or automation) is missing at the domain boundary. Enforcement lives only in operator SOPs, not in code.
- **Why existing tests do not prevent it.** No test asserts the specific phrase; existing tests just pass any non-empty string and expect success.
- **Recommended correction.** Add a phrase check at the domain boundary in `submit_external_paper_order` (not just the CLI shim) that requires `reason` (or a dedicated `approval_phrase` field) to equal the exact configured phrase; reject with a specific error code like `MISSING_APPROVAL_PHRASE`. Also add tests that pass an approximate phrase and assert rejection.
- **Recommended regression tests.** Unit test: call `submit_external_paper_order(reason='foo', ...)` and assert `MISSING_APPROVAL_PHRASE`. Contract test: assert CLI passes `reason` through unaltered.
- **Capability blocked.** Documented typed-phrase approval gate
- **Finder confidence.** high
- **Verification.** 3 votes (3 confirmed, 0 plausible, 0 refuted).
  - _severity_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Confirmed the invariant is not enforced in code. cli.py:361-372 forwards `--reason` verbatim; argparse (cli.py:2981-2986) only marks it required. submit_external_paper_order (external_broker.py:1319-1324) calls only `_bounded(reason, "reason", 512)`; `_bounded` (external_broker.py:115-119) enforces `1..maximum` length only. Grep for the mandated phrase "Approved, submit" in `src/` returns zero hit
  - _reachability_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Verified against source. `external_paper_submit_cli` (cli.py:361-372) forwards `reason` verbatim to `submit_external_paper_order` (external_broker.py:1319-1324), which applies only `_bounded(reason, 'reason', 512)` — a 1..512-character strip/length check (external_broker.py:115-119). No string comparison against the required phrase 'Approved, submit this exact paper order.' exists anywhere in `src
  - _code-refute_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Direct inspection of the cited files reproduces the finding. `submit_external_paper_order` at external_broker.py:1319-1366 only runs `_bounded(reason, "reason", 512)` on the reason parameter (line 1324), which by definition at lines 115-119 accepts any non-empty, length-bounded string and raises INVALID_OPERATOR_INPUT rather than a phrase-related code. ### F-003 [High / CONFIRMED] max_daily_loss_fraction gate ignores intraday gap-down losses on held positions

- **Area / Dimension.** BACKTEST-CORRECTNESS
- **Affected workflow.** Daily loss circuit breaker
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/backtesting/engine.py`
- **Line ranges.** 194-195,277-290
- **Observed behavior.**
  `start_equity` is marked at `bar.open` (line 195) and `pre_entry_equity` is marked at `bar.open` again after processing existing positions (line 277). Both use the same open-price mark for positions that survived the bar (no exit fired), so `daily_fraction = (pre_entry_equity - start_equity)/start_equity` measures only realized cost of same-day exits (slippage/fees). Any unrealized adverse gap-down on held positions never enters `daily_fraction`.
- **Failure scenario.**
  Book holds 10 shares AAPL bought at 100 with stop 88. Next bar opens at 90 (10% adverse gap) but stop is not touched. `start_equity` and `pre_entry_equity` both mark AAPL at 90, so `daily_fraction ~= 0`. New entry signals on the same session pass the `daily_fraction <= -max_daily_loss_fraction` gate (line 285) and get admitted, even though the book is already ~10% underwater intraday. The `max_daily_loss_fraction` circuit breaker is silently defeated on precisely the days it is meant to guard against.
- **Impact.** Documented daily-loss risk control does not actually limit new exposure on gap-down days; new positions are stacked on a book already carrying a large unrealized loss.
- **Recommended correction.** Anchor `start_equity` to yesterday's close mark (from `previous_closes`) or evaluate `daily_fraction` on close-mark equity vs yesterday's close-mark equity so unrealized gap losses count.
- **Recommended regression tests.** Feed a bar where a held position gaps down ~10% without touching the stop, then present a new entry signal for another symbol; assert the signal is rejected with reason DAILY_LOSS_LIMIT.
- **Capability blocked.** Daily loss-limit enforcement
- **Finder confidence.** high
- **Verification.** 3 votes (3 confirmed, 0 plausible, 0 refuted).
  - _reachability_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Verified against source. In src/trading_research/backtesting/engine.py the helper `mark_equity` (lines 182-192) values every held position at `getattr(bar, price_kind)` for the given `price_kind`. Both anchors bracketing the position-management loop use `price_kind="open"`: `start_equity` at line 195 and `pre_entry_equity` at line 277. For any position that survives the loop (no STOP_GAP, no low<=
  - _code-refute_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Verified against C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/backtesting/engine.py lines 182-290. Both `start_equity` (line 195) and `pre_entry_equity` (line 277) are computed via `mark_equity(current_date, "open")`, marking surviving positions at the same bar.open. Positions that don't trigger STOP_GAP/HARD_OR_TRAILING_STOP/MAXIMUM_HOLDING_PERIOD/PARTIAL/FINAL_TARGET during the processing lo
  - _severity_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Verified against src/trading_research/backtesting/engine.py. Line 194-195 marks start_equity using bar.open for existing positions, and line 277 recomputes pre_entry_equity using bar.open again after processing exits. For positions that were not exited during the bar (stop not touched by bar.open or bar.low, no target/partial), both marks use the same price, so daily_fraction (line 278) captures o

### F-004 [High / CONFIRMED] External-paper retry: supersede+new-reservation commit is atomic, but SUBMISSION_REQUESTED commit is a separate transaction — a crash between them strands the order with no recovery path

- **Area / Dimension.** CONCURRENCY-TX
- **Affected workflow.** External paper submission (retry / cross-day retry)
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py`
- **Line ranges.** src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:230-344, 1188-1240, 1546-1632; src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1759-1775
- **Observed behavior.**
  `retry_external_paper_order` calls `_reserve_daily_notional` (external_broker.py:1610-1615) which opens its own `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction (external_broker.py:265) and atomically transitions the prior attempt-N reservation to SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY (line 323-327) while inserting the new attempt-(N+1) RESERVED row (lines 336-344). It then commits and returns. Only afterwards does `_submit_once` open a *separate* `lease.fenced_write()` (external_broker.py:1210) that appends the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event for attempt (N+1). A crash between these two transactions leaves the DB with: (a) the chain's current event still `STATE_UNKNOWN` at attempt N, (b) attempt-N reservation now `SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY`, (c) attempt-(N+1) reservation `RESERVED` with no event. On restart `recover_stranded_submission` refuses because chain is not at SUBMISSION_REQUESTED (external_broker.py:1679-1684). A fresh `retry_external_paper_order` calls `repo.load_active_attempt_reservation(client_order_id, current["attempt_number"]=N, fingerprint, book_id)` at external_broker.py:1601-1603; that helper (paper_books_repositories.py:1759-1775) filters to `state IN ('RESERVED','RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED','SUBMITTED')` and therefore skips the SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY row, returning None → `ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_MISSING` at external_broker.py:1607-1609. Immutability triggers (paper_books_schema.py:1434-1443) forbid mutating the row back to RESERVED, so the operator has no non-manual DB-surgery path forward. The attempt-(N+1) reservation continues to count against the daily notional cap indefinitely.
- **Failure scenario.**
  Operator runs `external-paper-retry` after a confirmed ambiguous submission. `_reserve_daily_notional` commits: attempt-0 → SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY, attempt-1 → RESERVED. Process is SIGKILLed (or host crashes) before `_submit_once` writes the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event. On restart, `recover_stranded_submission` refuses (chain is at STATE_UNKNOWN, not SUBMISSION_REQUESTED). A fresh `external-paper-retry` fails with ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_MISSING because the prior active-reservation query skips SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY. The order can never be retried again and its daily-notional hold persists.
- **Impact.** Loss of the retry recovery path for legitimate ambiguous submissions and permanent leak of daily-notional cap capacity for the affected account, with no operator-safe repair path short of manual DB surgery (blocked by immutability triggers).
- **Recommended correction.** Fold the supersede+new-reservation writes and the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event append into a single caller-owned BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction (pass `commit=False` through `_reserve_daily_notional` and commit once inside `_submit_once`'s first `fenced_write`), or add an operator-callable repair entrypoint that reconciles a mid-flight retry (chain at STATE_UNKNOWN at attempt N with attempt N reservation SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY and attempt N+1 RESERVED but no matching event) by rolling back the attempt-(N+1) reservation.
- **Finder confidence.** high
- **Verification.** 3 votes (3 confirmed, 0 plausible, 0 refuted).

### F-005 [High / CONFIRMED] Cross-day retry can strand a supersede-committed reservation with no forward path

- **Area / Dimension.** CRASH-RECOVERY
- **Affected workflow.** external paper cross-day retry
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py`
- **Line ranges.** 230-344, 1546-1632
- **Observed behavior.**
  `_reserve_daily_notional` opens and commits its OWN `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction at :265 that (in the retry path) atomically transitions the prior attempt-N reservation to SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY (`_transition_attempt_reservation(..., commit=False)` at :323-327 inside the same `with transaction(conn):` block) AND inserts the new attempt-(N+1) RESERVED row (:343). Control then returns to `retry_external_paper_order` which calls `_submit_once` at :1617 — `_submit_once` opens a SEPARATE `lease.fenced_write()` (:1210) to commit the cash/share reservation + the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event as its own transaction. There is no enclosing transaction across those two commits.
- **Failure scenario.**
  Operator invokes `retry_external_paper_order` after `_reserve_daily_notional` commits (T2) but before `_submit_once` commits the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event (T3): a process crash between T2 and T3 leaves attempt-N's reservation SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY, attempt-(N+1)'s reservation RESERVED, and the event chain still at STATE_UNKNOWN@attemptN. On restart `recover_stranded_submission` refuses (chain is not at SUBMISSION_REQUESTED, :1679-1684). A fresh `retry_external_paper_order` at :1600-1609 calls `load_active_attempt_reservation(current["attempt_number"]=N,...)` — but attempt-N's row is now SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY, so `load_active_attempt_reservation` (paper_books_repositories.py:1767 IN clause of RESERVED/RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED/SUBMITTED) returns None → raises ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_MISSING at :1607-1609. The attempt-(N+1) RESERVED row keeps consuming the account's daily notional cap indefinitely, and immutability triggers (paper_books_schema.py:1441-1443) forbid deleting it.
- **Impact.** Permanent unresolvable state: retry blocked, daily cap silently reduced, requires manual DBA intervention (which the immutability triggers forbid). Same-account/same-day retries for other client_order_ids are also budget-degraded.
- **Why existing tests do not prevent it.** Existing retry tests exercise happy-path supersede+submit flow, never the intermediate durable state.
- **Recommended correction.** Wrap the supersede-transition and the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED checkpoint (or at minimum the reservation creation and the checkpoint) inside a single transaction, or add a bounded janitor that treats a RESERVED reservation without a matching submit-request event on a live chain as recoverable to RELEASED after operator confirmation.
- **Recommended regression tests.** Missing: no integration test simulates a process crash between the `_reserve_daily_notional` commit and the `_submit_once` SUBMISSION_REQUESTED commit and then re-invokes `retry_external_paper_order`.
- **Capability blocked.** External-paper retry after crash

  - _code-refute_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Verified against the actual code. `_reserve_daily_notional` opens its own `with transaction(conn):` (external_broker.py:265) — its docstring at :254-259 explicitly says it "Runs its own BEGIN IMMEDIATE .. - **Area / Dimension.** DOCS-ACCURACY

### F-008 [High / CONFIRMED] Persistent daily token-budget subsystem is inert in production — no reservation on any real provider call

- **Area / Dimension.** MODEL-TOKEN-COST
- **Affected workflow.** Research reuse / budget accounting
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/research/token_budget.py`, `src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py`, `src/trading_research/research/scheduled_cycle.py`, `src/trading_research/cli.py`
- **Line ranges.** token_budget.py:22-97; orchestration.py:365, 445-450, 704, 803, 893
- **Observed behavior.**
  `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` is defined in research/token_budget.py:22-97 and imported nowhere else in src/. Grep for `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController`/`token_budget_controller` across src/ shows only its self-definition, the Protocol declaration in research/orchestration.py:172, and the three call sites inside `analyze_with_research_committee` that keep `token_budget_controller: 'ResearchTokenBudgetController | None' = None` (research/orchestration.py:704) and only pass it through to `_run_role_with_retries` (research/orchestration.py:803, 893). Neither `cli.py::run_research_cli` nor `research/scheduled_cycle.py::run_scheduled_research_cycle` constructs one (Grep for `token_budget` in both files returns no matches). Consequently research/orchestration.py:445-450 always takes the `provider.generate_structured(request)` branch and never invokes `reserve_research_tokens`.
- **Failure scenario.**
  Deploy `research.provider=anthropic` (or `claude_code`) and issue a fresh research run for one symbol. `analyze_with_research_committee` is called with `token_budget_controller=None`, `_run_role_with_retries` calls `provider.generate_structured` directly, and no row is written to `shadow_budget_reservations` with `reservation_kind='RESEARCH_TOKENS'`. Repeat until the intended `daily_token_cap` for RESEARCH_TOKENS is exceeded — no rejection fires because the reservation code path is never entered.
- **Impact.** The invariant 'every fresh provider call goes through reservation' is not enforced in production. Only in-process, caller-supplied counters (see MODEL-TOKEN-COST-02) or ad-hoc shadow role-budget checks (a separate cost-only path in shadow/role_budget.py) gate live spending. A misconfigured provider or a runaway retry loop can silently burn tokens without ever hitting the persistent daily cap, and the AMBIGUOUS/reconciliation apparatus in research_budget.py:424-535 is unreachable outside tests.
- **Recommended correction.** Instantiate `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` in the CLI/scheduled-cycle wiring (research/scheduled_cycle.py:_run_symbol and cli.py:run_research_cli) whenever `research.provider != 'deterministic'`, wired from `config/research.yaml` daily-cap settings. Add an end-to-end regression that a fresh Anthropic-mode run writes a `RESEARCH_TOKENS` reservation row and that a second call after cap-exhaustion is rejected with `BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`.
- **Recommended regression tests.** tests/unit/test_research_orchestration.py:401 wires the controller manually; there is no integration test that exercises production wiring, so the missing wiring is invisible in CI.

### F-009 [High / CONFIRMED] PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController is fully implemented but never instantiated by any production caller; the daily research-token cap is only enforced from tests

- **Area / Dimension.** OPERATIONAL-WIRING
- **Affected workflow.** scheduled research cycle -> analyze_with_research_committee -> provider call
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/research/token_budget.py`, `src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py`, `src/trading_research/research/scheduled_cycle.py`, `src/trading_research/cli.py`, `src/trading_research/shadow/scheduler.py`, `src/trading_research/strategies/research_budget.py`
- **Line ranges.** token_budget.py:22-97; orchestration.py:365,445-450,704,803,893; scheduled_cycle.py:516-521; cli.py:833-837, 1489-1500, 1817-1856; shadow/scheduler.py:895-952
- **Observed behavior.**
  `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` (token_budget.py:22-97) wraps every provider call in `reserve_research_tokens` -> `settle/release/mark_ambiguous` (strategies/research_budget.py). `analyze_with_research_committee` and `_run_role_with_retries` accept a `token_budget_controller=None` parameter (orchestration.py:365,704,447-450), and the CLI paths that call the orchestrator (`run_research` at cli.py:833, `replay-research-cycle` at cli.py:1489, `run-due-shadow-cycle` at cli.py:1854 via `run_scheduled_research_cycle` in scheduled_cycle.py:516-521) never construct or pass one. Grep for `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController(` across `src/` returns only the class definition; the only instantiations are in `tests/unit/test_research_orchestration.py:401`.
- **Failure scenario.**
  Operator enables real-Claude scheduled research (research.provider=anthropic|claude_code|codex, provider_mode=real). `run_due_shadow_cycle` invokes `run_scheduled_research_cycle` which invokes `analyze_with_research_committee(...)` with no `token_budget_controller`. On every attempt, orchestration.py:445-449 selects the `provider.generate_structured(request)` branch directly; no row is ever written to `shadow_budget_reservations` with `reservation_kind='RESEARCH_TOKENS'`. The daily token cap in `strategies/research_budget.py::reserve_research_tokens` (:235-340), the ambiguous-reservation gate, and the emergency-margin-breach gate are all skipped. `record_actual_usage` writes 0 tokens/0 cost for the reservation (shadow/scheduler.py:996-1000) so per-attempt cost accounting also has no authoritative fallback outside the attempt controller.
- **Impact.** The persistent, per-UTC-day research-token cap that `strategies/research_budget.py` was built to enforce does not run in production. A misbehaving provider or prompt is bounded only by the in-memory ShadowResearchAttemptController per-cycle budgets, not by the persistent daily cap; ambiguous provider outcomes never mark reservations for operator reconciliation. Because tests fully exercise the controller, this gap is invisible to the test suite while remaining a real production capability bypass.
- **Recommended correction.** Instantiate `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` in `run_due_shadow_cycle_cli` (and any other real-provider orchestrator paths), thread it through `cycle_kwargs`/`run_scheduled_research_cycle` into `analyze_with_research_committee(token_budget_controller=...)`. Add a test that fails if the controller is None whenever provider is anthropic|claude_code|codex.

### F-010 [High / CONFIRMED] max_daily_new_notional_usd is never accumulated across orders (local paper books)

- **Area / Dimension.** PORTFOLIO-CONCENTRATION
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/paper_books/risk.py`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/scheduled_integration.py`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/cli_support.py`
- **Line ranges.** risk.py:45-66,162-171; scheduled_integration.py:278; cli_support.py:193
- **Observed behavior.**
  PaperPortfolioContext.daily_new_notional_usd is the only input driving the per-book, per-day cumulative notional cap: risk.py:162 computes `remaining_daily_capacity = max(0, risk_config.max_daily_new_notional_usd - context.daily_new_notional_usd)`. Every production caller of build_portfolio_context hard-codes the accumulator to Decimal('0'): scheduled_integration.py:278 (`build_portfolio_context(conn, book_id, as_of, snap, symbol, Decimal("0"))`) and cli_support.py:193 (same call, same literal). Grep across src/ confirms these are the only two production call sites. Nothing else in paper_books/ maintains or reads a per-book, per-UTC-day submitted-notional counter (Grep for `daily_new_notional` returns only the risk/models/config plumbing). The external-broker path has its own persistent per-day accumulator via paper_external_attempt_reservations (external_broker.py:230-344), but the *local* books have no analogous accumulator.
- **Failure scenario.**
  config/paper_books.yaml ships risk.max_daily_new_notional_usd="5000.00" and max_order_notional_usd="1000.00". A scheduled paper-book integration cycle passes 8 approved symbols through _process_arm for the BASELINE book on the same UTC day; each _process_arm call passes daily_new_notional_usd=Decimal('0') to build_portfolio_context. remaining_daily_capacity therefore equals the full 5000 for every symbol, and evaluate_paper_risk approves 8 × ~$1000 = $8000 of new BUY notional in one day, silently exceeding max_daily_new_notional_usd=5000 without producing a single RISK_REJECTED_DAILY_NOTIONAL_LIMIT decision.
- **Impact.** The daily-notional concentration cap advertised by config/paper_books.yaml is not enforced. A book can accumulate more new notional per UTC day than the configured limit, undermining the documented portfolio-concentration guardrail and the invariant that a per-day $5000 (or operator-configured) budget is authoritative. ### F-011 [High / CONFIRMED] event_catalyst content_id changes on every re-evaluation, breaking dedup/reuse

- **Area / Dimension.** STRATEGY-CORRECTNESS
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/strategies/event_catalyst.py`, `src/trading_research/strategies/contracts.py`
- **Line ranges.** event_catalyst.py:200-213; contracts.py:155-190
- **Observed behavior.**
  event_catalyst.py:205 and 212 put `event_age_hours` and `selected_event_age_hours` — both computed as `(context.now - selected_event.event_timestamp).total_seconds() / 3600.0` — into `factor_values`. `_content_payload` (contracts.py:155-176) includes `factor_values` verbatim (line 174), and `derive_strategy_signal_content_id` (contracts.py:179-190) hashes that payload. The docstring at contracts.py:180-190 states the content ID must be stable across re-evaluations: 'An unchanged signal re-evaluated five minutes later produces the same content ID'. Because `context.now` moves each evaluation but `event_timestamp` does not, every re-run produces a different `event_age_hours` and therefore a different content ID.
- **Failure scenario.**
  Scanner evaluates an event_catalyst signal for AAPL at 10:00 UTC with a positive `earnings_result` event — content ID `strat-sig-A`. Five minutes later, the identical evaluation (same bars, same event, no data change) produces `event_age_hours=1.0 → 1.0833`, hashing to content ID `strat-sig-B ≠ strat-sig-A`. Any downstream flow keyed on this content ID (research reuse, candidate dedup, `strategy_signal_to_entry_signal` in backtest_adapter.py:41 which sets `EntrySignal.signal_id` and `strategy_signal_id` from the same derivation, and the documented 'future order-intent deduplication') treats them as two separate signals and can therefore issue duplicate work / duplicate intents for what is economically the same signal.
- **Impact.** Directly defeats the documented purpose of content_id (research reuse, candidate dedup, future order-intent dedup). Event catalyst is the only strategy carrying time-derived floats in factor_values, so momentum/mean_reversion do not exhibit this — but for event_catalyst, content-identity vs evaluation-identity is not actually separated as the docstring claims.
- **Recommended correction.** Drop `event_age_hours` / `selected_event_age_hours` from `factor_values` (they are pure re-derivations of `context.now - event_timestamp` and belong on the evaluation record, not the content payload), OR compute them from `data_as_of - event_timestamp` (a stable point-in-time delta) rather than `context.now`. Alternatively, filter these keys out inside `_content_payload`.
- **Finder confidence.** high
- **Verification.** 3 votes (2 confirmed, 1 plausible, 0 refuted).

However, under the reachability lens, the failure scenario ("scanner re-evaluates 5 minutes later, downstream dedup treats them 
  - _code-refute_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Verified against the cited files. event_catalyst.py:205 and 212 place time-varying floats derived from `context.now - selected_event.event_timestamp` into `factor_values`. `_content_payload` (contracts.py:155-176, line 174) includes `factor_values` verbatim, and `derive_strategy_signal_content_id` (contracts.py:179-190) hashes it — despite the docstring at 180-190 explicitly promising the content

### F-012 [Medium / CONFIRMED] Two competing paper-intent identity/type systems: `execution/intent_builder.py` still constructs `ORDER_TYPE_MARKET` intents while `paper_books/order_intent.py` builds `ORDER_TYPE_LIMIT` only

- **Severity note.** Original finder severity: High; adjusted after verification to Medium.
- **Area / Dimension.** ARCHITECTURE
- **Affected workflow.** Local paper intent creation & approval
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/execution/intent_builder.py`, `src/trading_research/execution/models.py`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/order_intent.py`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/models.py`, `src/trading_research/cli.py`
- **Line ranges.** execution/intent_builder.py:16, 86-103; execution/models.py:51-60; paper_books/order_intent.py:42-58; paper_books/models.py:98-103; cli.py:2572-2584, 3071-3081
- **Observed behavior.**
  There are two parallel intent identity functions — `derive_intent_id(recommendation_id, execution_version)` (execution/models.py:51-60) and `derive_paper_order_intent_id(recommendation_id, book_id, execution_version)` (paper_books/models.py:98-103) — with different key formats and stored in different tables (`paper_execution_intents` vs `paper_book_orders`). The legacy `intent_builder.build_paper_order_intent` unconditionally emits `order_type=ORDER_TYPE_MARKET` (execution/intent_builder.py:92), directly contradicting the 'Limit orders only' invariant that `paper_books/order_intent.py:53` enforces. The legacy path is only quarantined behind a flag (`legacy-paper-execute --i-understand-this-is-the-legacy-ledger`, cli.py:2572-2584) at the CLI level; the domain code itself has no guard.
- **Failure scenario.**
  A developer or automation imports `from trading_research.execution.intent_builder import build_paper_order_intent` and hands the resulting intent to a paper adapter — the intent will be MARKET, violating the invariant. There is no fail-closed check in the execution layer that refuses MARKET.
- **Impact.** Two overlapping sources of truth for what a paper order intent is; a rule ('Limit orders only') that the invariant audit calls PARTIAL because one branch still emits MARKET. Also two competing identity functions with different collision surfaces if code paths ever cross.
- **Why existing tests do not prevent it.** Legacy tests preserve the MARKET path as-is; nothing enforces the invariant against the domain module directly.
- **Recommended correction.** Either (a) delete `execution/intent_builder.py` / `execution/models.py::derive_intent_id` and route all remaining callers through `paper_books/order_intent.py`, or (b) explicitly change `execution/intent_builder.py:92` to `ORDER_TYPE_LIMIT` with a computed limit price and add a domain-level assert that MARKET is rejected. CLI-only quarantining is not sufficient for an invariant that is meant to be authoritative-boundary.
- **Recommended regression tests.** Add a test that imports the executor path from a non-CLI entry and asserts either the MARKET path is removed or a fail-closed guard rejects it.
- **Capability blocked.** Single authoritative intent-building rule
- **Finder confidence.** high
- **Verification.** 3 votes (2 confirmed, 0 plausible, 1 refuted).

Two distinct derive_*id functions exist with different prefixes (`in
  - _reachability_: verdict=REFUTED, refuted=True - Verified against source. No broker is contacted, and the deterministic adapter ignores `ord
  - _code-refute_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Re-read the four cited files. (2) execution/models.py:51-60 defines derive_intent_id keyed only by (recommendation_id, execution_version) producing "intent-<sha256[:32]>". 

### F-013 [Medium / CONFIRMED] `_safety_checks` runs before the order-scope lease is acquired in `submit_external_paper_order` and is not re-verified inside the fenced write

- **Severity note.** Original finder severity: High; adjusted after verification to Medium.
- **Area / Dimension.** ARCHITECTURE
- **Affected workflow.** External paper submission
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py`
- **Line ranges.** external_broker.py:1319-1367, 585-607, 1188-1226
- **Observed behavior.**
  `submit_external_paper_order` calls `_safety_checks(conn, book_id)` at line 1327 BEFORE acquiring `_order_lease` at line 1329. Within the lease, `_reserve_daily_notional` and `_submit_once` run, but `_safety_checks` is never re-invoked inside `lease.fenced_write()`. The safety-pause / CRITICAL-alert / critical-reconciliation observation is therefore a plain read from an autocommit connection outside any BEGIN IMMEDIATE (external_broker.py:589-607). `retry_external_paper_order` (line 1551) has the same pattern (safety check at :1576, lease acquired at :1579, no re-check inside the fenced write).
- **Failure scenario.**
  A shadow safety pause is issued or a CRITICAL alert is raised in the ~milliseconds between the `_safety_checks` return and the fenced `_submit_once` write. The submit proceeds to the broker (runtime.submit_limit_order at :1228) even though the safety gate is now blocking — the durable state gates (event chain / reservation uniqueness) do not check pause/alert state.
- **Impact.** The safety-pause gate becomes racy at the domain boundary — it protects only steady-state operations, not the exact instant a pause is being issued. Given the isolated external-paper account, the missed pause could allow one order that a human operator specifically tried to pre-empt.
- **Why existing tests do not prevent it.** Existing tests use in-process ordering that does not interleave the pause write between :1327 and :1329.
- **Recommended correction.** Re-run `_safety_checks` inside `lease.fenced_write()` in `_submit_once` immediately before `_append_event(...STATE_SUBMISSION_REQUESTED...)`, so the pause/alert read is inside the BEGIN IMMEDIATE that also writes the checkpoint.
- **Recommended regression tests.** Concurrency test: start a submit, mid-flight write a `PAUSED` state into `paper_book_safety_events`, complete the submit, assert `SAFETY_PAUSE_ACTIVE` is raised. Currently this passes only if the pause is written before `_safety_checks` executes.
- **Capability blocked.** Race-free shadow-pause enforcement across state transitions
- **Finder confidence.** medium
- **Verification.** 3 votes (2 confirmed, 1 plausible, 0 refuted).

### F-014 [Medium / CONFIRMED] `_reserve_daily_notional` opens and commits its own transaction, splitting the reservation and the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED checkpoint across two independent commit boundaries

- **Area / Dimension.** ARCHITECTURE
- **Affected workflow.** Cross-day retry / UTC-day reservation transitions
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py`
- **Line ranges.** external_broker.py:230-344 (docstring 254-260), 1188-1226, 1546-1622
- **Observed behavior.**
  `_reserve_daily_notional` docstring explicitly states it runs its own BEGIN IMMEDIATE and is never nested inside the lease's `fenced_write` (:254-260). `_submit_once` then re-enters a fresh `lease.fenced_write()` at :1210 to write the cash/share reservation + STATE_SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event. These are two separate commit boundaries. The Milestone 11.3 Part 36/37 comment at :1199-1208 claims atomicity of 'reservation and SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event' — but this refers to the cash/share reservation, not the daily-notional attempt reservation, which was committed in a prior transaction.
- **Failure scenario.**
  During a cross-day retry (retry_external_paper_order at :1546), `_reserve_daily_notional` atomically supersedes the prior-attempt reservation and inserts a new RESERVED row for attempt N+1 (commit T1). The process crashes before `_submit_once`'s fenced_write commits SUBMISSION_REQUESTED (T2). On restart: the event chain is still at STATE_UNKNOWN at attempt N, but the prior reservation is SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY. `recover_stranded_submission` refuses (chain not at SUBMISSION_REQUESTED, :1679-1684). A fresh `retry_external_paper_order` calls `load_active_attempt_reservation(current["attempt_number"]=N, ...)` which returns None because prior is now SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY, and raises `ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_MISSING` (:1600-1609). The stranded attempt-(N+1) reservation still counts against today's daily cap with no operator-facing recovery path (immutability triggers at paper_books_schema.py:1441-1443 forbid DELETE).
- **Impact.** Ownership of the reservation state transition is split across two commit boundaries, contradicting the claimed atomicity story. On crash, a daily-notional reservation can be permanently orphaned, consuming the daily-cap budget with no way to reclaim it short of manual DB surgery.
- **Why existing tests do not prevent it.** No test process-kills between commits; unit tests only assert per-transaction atomicity.
- **Recommended correction.** Fold the daily-notional reservation commit into the same fenced_write that appends STATE_SUBMISSION_REQUESTED, or add a compensating recovery path in `recover_stranded_submission` that also inspects orphan reservations without matching events.
- **Recommended regression tests.** Crash-injection test that simulates a process kill between the reservation commit and the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED commit on retry, then attempts recovery.
- **Capability blocked.** Crash-consistent daily-notional accounting
- **Finder confidence.** medium
- **Verification.** 3 votes (3 confirmed, 0 plausible, 0 refuted).
  - _severity_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Traced the code end-to-end and confirmed the finding.  Two independent commit boundaries: 1. `_reserve_daily_notional` (external_broker.py:230-344) opens its own `with transaction(conn):` at :265 which is a `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`/`COMMIT` block (transactions.py:57-74). The docstring at :254-260 explicitly states this is never nested inside the caller's `fenced_write`. `begin_immediate` refuses to nest 
  - _code-refute_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Independently reproduced the finding from code evidence: (1) `_reserve_daily_notional` runs its own transaction via `with transaction(conn):` at external_broker.py:265, which storage/transactions.py:57-74 confirms is a self-contained BEGIN IMMEDIATE ... COMMIT (T1). (2) `_submit_once` opens a separate `with lease.fenced_write():` at external_broker.py:1210 that begins another BEGIN IMMEDIATE and c
  - _reachability_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - Reachability lens: the described failure path is reachable in production without any flag or upstream guard.  1. ### F-015 [Medium / CONFIRMED] Strategy-supplied stop is not reflected in R-multiple risk, so partial-profit fires at wrong price

- **Severity note.** Original finder severity: High; adjusted after verification to Medium.
- **Area / Dimension.** BACKTEST-CORRECTNESS
- **Affected workflow.** Backtest strategy exits / partial profit
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/backtesting/engine.py`
- **Line ranges.** 225-256,318-359
- **Observed behavior.**
  When a strategy provides `signal.initial_stop_reference` the engine overwrites `stop = signal.initial_stop_reference` at line 331, but `position.entry_atr` is still stored as the raw ATR (line 356). `risk_per_share = position.entry_price - (position.entry_price - position.entry_atr * config.initial_stop_multiple)` (lines 225-227) uses the ATR-based risk rather than the actual entry-to-stop distance. Partial-profit stages then compute `high_r = (bar.high - entry) / risk_per_share` (line 228) and `trigger_price = entry + risk_per_share * stage.trigger_r_multiple` (line 246) against ATR risk, not strategy risk. Trailing-stop update on line 274 also uses ATR rather than strategy risk.
- **Failure scenario.**
  Signal with fill_price=100, entry_atr=2 (initial_stop_multiple=2 => engine risk=4), strategy `initial_stop_reference=90` (actual strategy risk=10). Partial-profit stage with `trigger_r_multiple=1` fires as soon as `bar.high >= 104` and books a partial sell at $104, even though the strategy defines 1R as a move to $110. Every strategy backtest whose stop tightens or widens vs ATR reports partial-profit exits at the wrong R and produces distorted realized_pnl, partial_profit_fills, exit-reason distribution, and strategy-attributable P/L.
- **Impact.** Strategy backtests silently substitute engine-generic R-multiple exits for the strategy's declared risk unit, breaking Milestone 24 Part C3's promise that strategy exits are honored and making strategy metrics unattributable.
- **Recommended correction.** When `signal.initial_stop_reference` is provided, derive `risk_per_share = fill_price - stop` (store an explicit `entry_risk_per_share` on `_Position`) and use that everywhere partial-profit R-multiples and trailing-stop candidates are computed.
- **Recommended regression tests.** Add a test where entry=100, entry_atr=2, initial_stop_reference=90, partial_profit stage at 1R: expect the partial fill on the first bar whose high>=110 at price ~110, not at ~104.
- **Capability blocked.** Faithful strategy-exit backtesting
- **Finder confidence.** high
- **Verification.** 3 votes (2 confirmed, 1 plausible, 0 refuted).

  - _severity_: verdict=CONFIRMED, refuted=False - The mechanical claim is real. engine.py:225-227 defines risk_per_share = entry_price - (entry_price - entry_atr * initial_stop_multiple) = entry_atr * initial_stop_multiple. When signal.initial_stop_reference is provided, engine.py:331 overrides `stop`, but engine.py:356 still stores `entry_atr=atr`, so risk_per_share is the engine's ATR-derived risk, not (fill_price - strategy_stop). Consequently

### F-016 [Medium / CONFIRMED] Slippage applied on STOP_GAP exits at bar.open can produce fills below the bar's low

- **Area / Dimension.** BACKTEST-CORRECTNESS
- **Affected workflow.** Backtest stop-gap and maximum-holding exits
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/backtesting/engine.py`
- **Line ranges.** 100-120,208-223
- **Observed behavior.**
  `STOP_GAP` sets `exit_price = bar.open` (line 209). `_sell` then applies an unconditional negative slippage: `slipped = price * (1 - slippage_bps/10000)` (line 107) and the fill is booked at `slipped`. The same applies to `MAXIMUM_HOLDING_PERIOD` at `bar.open`.
- **Failure scenario.**
  Gap-down day where `bar.open == bar.low`. STOP_GAP fires at bar.open, then slippage subtracts more, producing a fill price strictly less than any level traded on the bar. With slippage_bps=25, a bar with open=low=$90 produces a stop-gap fill at $89.775, a price no participant could have transacted. Realized P/L and drawdown are biased pessimistic in a way not tied to any observed market state.
- **Impact.** Backtest results are systematically biased on gap-down/exit days; strategies compared across configurations are distorted by how often gaps occur, not by strategy design.
- **Recommended correction.** For STOP_GAP and MAXIMUM_HOLDING_PERIOD exits taken at bar.open, clamp the slipped price to `max(slipped, bar.low)`, or skip additional slippage on adverse-gap exits and rely on the bar.open concession alone.
- **Recommended regression tests.** Construct a bar where open==low, run STOP_GAP with slippage_bps>0; assert the SELL fill_price >= bar.low.
- **Capability blocked.** Realistic modelling of gap-down fills

### F-017 [Medium / CONFIRMED] Benchmark symbol not required in config.symbols causes late-stage failure after full run

- **Area / Dimension.** BACKTEST-CORRECTNESS
- **Affected workflow.** Backtest configuration
- **Affected files.** `src/trading_research/backtesting/engine.py`, `src/trading_research/backtesting/configuration.py`
- **Line ranges.** 141-150,372-377
- **Observed behavior.**
  `run_backtest` only fetches bars for `config.symbols` (lines 141-150). At the end it does `bars_by_symbol.get(config.benchmark_symbol)` and raises `BacktestError('benchmark dataset is unavailable')` when the benchmark was not in symbols (lines 373-376). `BacktestConfiguration.__post_init__` (configuration.py:38-49) does not enforce that `benchmark_symbol` is in `symbols`.
- **Failure scenario.**
  Operator sets `symbols=('AAPL',)` and `benchmark_symbol='SPY'`. The engine iterates every session, computes trades, daily states, and metrics, then raises after all work has been done, with a message that reads as a data-provider failure even though the true cause is a configuration omission.

### F-018 [Medium / CONFIRMED] CI runs only Python 3.11 despite pyproject `requires-python >= 3.10`

- **Area / Dimension.** CI-OPS
- **Affected files.** `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `pyproject.toml`, `paper_runtime/pyproject.toml`
- **Line ranges.** .github/workflows/ci.yml:18-20, 31-33, 44-48, 74-77, 96-98; pyproject.toml:9; paper_runtime/pyproject.toml:9
- **Observed behavior.**
  Every job hard-codes `python-version: "3.11"` (five occurrences in ci.yml). Both packages declare `requires-python = ">=3.10"` (pyproject.toml:9, paper_runtime/pyproject.toml:9). Neither Python 3.10 nor Python 3.12 is exercised in CI.
- **Failure scenario.**
  A contributor uses a 3.10-only construct (e.g., a `typing.Self` import from `typing` instead of `typing_extensions` — 3.11+ only, or a match-statement pattern that behaves differently pre-3.11) that runs fine in CI on 3.11 but crashes at import time on an operator's 3.10 workstation, or a 3.12 deprecation (e.g., `datetime.utcnow()` warnings escalating) breaks a downstream run. Since the schema-init path is executed on every process start (`storage/database.py::connect`), an import-time failure blocks all CLI commands.
- **Impact.** Support claims on `>=3.10` are unverified. Operators on 3.10 or 3.12 (Homebrew defaults today) can hit failures that CI cannot detect.
- **Why existing tests do not prevent it.** The suite itself does not enforce a Python version; the CI matrix is the sole enforcement point.
- **Recommended correction.** Add a `strategy.matrix.python-version: ["3.10", "3.11", "3.12"]` to the main-tests and paper-runtime-tests jobs; keep the type-check jobs at 3.11 if desired but drop 3.10/3.12 into main-tests at minimum.
- **Recommended regression tests.** Existing tests are sufficient; matrix expansion alone catches version-drift.
- **Capability blocked.** Multi-Python compatibility validation
- **Finder confidence.** high
