Downstream, each `ShortlistEntry` is passed to `research_budget.decide_research_action(entry, state, config)` at `src/trading_research/strategies/research_budget.py:62-90`. Config comes from `load_strategy_config()` at `src/trading_research/strategies/config.py:160-283`. In this repo no production entry point in `src/` invokes `select_shortlist` — only tests do (verified by Grep on `select_shortlist(` and on `MomentumBreakoutStrategy(` / `MeanReversionStrategy(` / `EventDrivenCatalystStrategy(`).
- **Authoritative validation.** Layered: - `StrategyConfig` loader in `strategies/config.py:160-283` fails closed on missing/invalid YAML keys and on `enabled=true` at STAGE_1/STAGE_2 (`config.py:185-191`); frozen dataclasses `MomentumBreakoutConfig` (`config.py:39-61`), `MeanReversionConfig` (`config.py:64-84`), `EventCatalystConfig` (`config.py:87-106`), `ShortlistConfig` (`config.py:109-140`) enforce bound checks in `__post_init__`. - `StrategySignal.__post_init__` (`contracts.py:68-104`) enforces tz-awareness, `[0.0,1.0]` strength, non-empty reason codes when ELIGIBLE, numeric-only `factor_values`, and — for ELIGIBLE — a positive entry/limit/stop/invalidation, `initial_stop_reference<entry_reference`, and `target_reference>entry_reference` if present. - `classify_safety_status` (`safety_gates.py:22-48`) requires sufficient bar history and every `screening_result.gate_results` passed; a STALENESS_GATE failure maps to `STRATE`, other gate failures to `NOT_ELIGIBLE`. - Per-strategy `evaluate()` re-checks INCOMPLETE conditions (missing factor history / relative strength / technical freshness, future timestamps) — e.g. `momentum_breakout.py:60-84`. - Portfolio-side authority in `selector._symbol_allocation_decision` (`selector.py:48-79`) requires a complete `PortfolioState.symbol_exposure_fraction` snapshot and non-null equity, else `symbol_exposure_unknown`. - Advisory only: `_symbol_allocation_decision`'s own docstring calls out "final risk validation remains authoritative" (`selector.py:57-58`, `config.py:114-117`). The authoritative execution-risk cap lives elsewhere (paper-book 10% cap, per config comment `config/strategies.yaml:65-72`).
- **Idempotency key.**  - `select_shortlist`: none (pure function; deterministic on inputs). - `StrategySignal` content identity: `derive_strategy_signal_content_id` returns `"strat-sig-" + sha256(content_payload)[:32]` — excludes `signal_timestamp`, so identical economic content across evaluations shares one ID (`contracts.py:155-190`). The evaluation-audit identity is `derive_strategy_evaluation_id` = `"strat-eval-" + sha256(payload_including_signal_timestamp)[:32]` (`contracts.py:193-204`). Used downstream by execution_boundary and backtest_adapter as `strategy_signal_id`. - Research token reservation idempotency key: `_research_token_idempotency_key` = `f"{RESEARCH_TOKEN_RESERVATION_PREFIX}:{research_run_id}:{symbol}:{provider}:{model_name or 'none'}:{research_attempt_identity}:{utc_date}"` (`research_budget.py:158-166`). Enforced via `load_budget_reservation_by_idempotency_key` inside a transaction (`research_budget.py:281-291`). 
- **Transaction boundary.**  - `select_shortlist` and `decide_research_action`: no DB writes, no transaction. - Token reservation: `with transaction(conn):` wraps `reserve_research_tokens` (`research_budget.py:280-340`), `_transition_reservation` (`research_budget.py:347-363`), `reconcile_ambiguous_research_tokens` (`research_budget.py:503-535`). Each mutation is one atomic UPDATE keyed on `(reservation_id, status, reservation_kind)` with `cursor.rowcount==1` acting as CAS. 
- **Lease or concurrency.**  - Shortlist selection has no lease; two concurrent callers would independently rank and could both hand off the same symbol. - Daily research counters (`DailyResearchBudgetState.symbols_sent_today`, `fresh_cycles_today`, `tokens_spent_today`) are caller-supplied advisory values, not persisted — no concurrency guard at the selector/budget-decision layer (`research_budget.py:40-53`). - Persistent concurrency guard exists only inside `reserve_research_tokens`: `_live_tokens_reserved_for_date` counts every RESERVED/SETTLED/AMBIGUOUS row for the UTC date (`research_budget.py:203-212`) so two concurrent reservations cannot both see full headroom; `_date_has_budget_breach` (`research_budget.py:215-221`) blocks the day if any prior row's `emergency_margin_breached=1`; per-attempt duplicate AMBIGUOUS rows for the same `(run,symbol,provider,model)` prefix (`research_budget.py:296-309`) rejects until reconciled. - Status transitions are single-row CAS via UPDATE with `WHERE status=? AND reservation_kind=?` — `_transition_reservation` (`research_budget.py:343-363`). 
- **State transitions.**  - Per-signal strategy status (deterministic classification, no persistence unless caller stores): ELIGIBLE / NOT_ELIGIBLE / INCOMPLETE / STALE (`contracts.py:34-38`, `safety_gates.py:22-48`). - Per shortlist entry (`ShortlistEntry.reason`, `selector.py:105-117`): "shortlisted" | "combined_shortlist_cap_reached" | "symbol_allocation_cap_reached" | "symbol_exposure_unknown". - Per research decision (`research_budget.py:30-38`): SEND_TO_RESEARCH → (after orchestrator invocation, via `record_research_outcome`, `research_budget.py:93-98`) REUSE_RESEARCH or RUN_FULL_RESEARCH; RUN_REDUCED_CONTEXT declared but with no trigger path. Skip-terminals: SKIP_LOW_SIGNAL, SKIP_DAILY_CANDIDATE_CAP, SKIP_DAILY_TOKEN_CAP. - Token reservation state machine (`research_budget.py:122-127`): RESERVED → SETTLED (via `settle_research_tokens`, `research_budget.py:366-410`); RESERVED → RELEASED (`release_research_tokens`, `research_budget.py:413-421`); RESERVED → AMBIGUOUS (`mark_research_tokens_ambiguous`, `research_budget.py:424-431`); AMBIGUOUS → SETTLED|RELEASED only via `reconcile_ambiguous_research_tokens` requiring operator+reason+source (`research_budget.py:434-535`). 
- **Persistent records.**  - The evaluation+shortlist step writes nothing (verified: `strategies/selector.py` and `strategies/research_budget.decide_research_action` never call `conn.execute` or a repo). No `strategy_signals` / `strategy_evaluations` table exists (Grep on `strategy_signal` and `insert.*signal` returned only in-code identifiers, no repository writes). - Only downstream token reservations touch storage: `shadow_budget_reservations` (INSERT at `research_budget.py:323-334`, UPDATE at `research_budget.py:347-363` and `research_budget.py:477-513`) and `token_budget_reconciliation` (insert via `insert_token_budget_reconciliation` at `research_budget.py:522-534`). - Downstream, when a signal actually seeds a paper intent, `execution_boundary.build_strategy_order_intent_context` (`execution_boundary.py:74-97`) and `backtest_adapter.strategy_signal_to_entry_signal` (`backtest_adapter.py:29-53`) both use `derive_strategy_signal_content_id` as the persisted ID. 
- **External side effects.** None during evaluation or shortlist selection: `test_zero_llm_calls_during_scanning` (`tests/unit/test_strategy_selector.py:182-193`) asserts the selector imports nothing from `research/` or `evidence_providers/`; the `strategies/` package as a whole is required to have zero LLM-touching imports (per `docs/strategy-candidate-selection.md:34-36`). The token-budget wrapper indirectly enables the LLM call that follows (`research/token_budget.py:66` calls `provider.generate_structured(request)`).
- **Failure behavior.** Fail-closed at every seam: strategies map missing inputs to INCOMPLETE/STALE/NOT_ELIGIBLE with `signal_strength=0.0` (never a favorable default); only ELIGIBLE signals with complete stop/invalidation/holding-period data survive selection (`contracts.py:88-104`, `selector.py:90-93`); portfolio uncertainty (`account_equity=None`, `symbol_exposure_complete=False`, held symbol missing from the exposure map) excludes the candidate with `symbol_exposure_unknown` (`selector.py:59-79`); config errors block loading entirely (`config.py:160-191`); token budget rejects propagate as non-retryable `ProviderUnavailableError` and ambiguous provider outcomes lock the reservation counted-but-unreconciled rather than releasing it back to the day's budget (`research/token_budget.py:70-97`).
- **Retry behavior.**  - Shortlist step: no retry logic (pure function). - Research decision: no retry (single-shot). - Reservation: idempotent by design — a repeat `reserve_research_tokens` with the same idempotency key reuses the existing row (`research_budget.py:281-291`); `settle_research_tokens` treats a re-settle as a no-op via the `_compatible` predicate at `research_budget.py:393-401` if the incoming actuals match, otherwise raises TOKEN_RESERVATION_STATE_CONFLICT (`research_budget.py:360-363`). Same idempotent no-op behavior for RELEASED (`research_budget.py:420`) and AMBIGUOUS (`research_budget.py:430`). - `research_attempt_identity` defaults to "attempt-1" (`research_budget.py:248`); the token-budget controller passes `f"{request.role}:{request.attempt_number}"` (`research/token_budget.py:49`), so each research retry attempt gets a fresh reservation. 
- **Recovery behavior.**  - AMBIGUOUS is the "unknown outcome" state — it stays counted against the daily cap and cannot be auto-cleared. Recovery requires an operator to call `reconcile_ambiguous_research_tokens` (`research_budget.py:434-535`) with `operator`, `reason`, `reconciliation_source`, and — for SETTLED — actual token usage and policy. An append-only `token_budget_reconciliation` row is inserted per resolution (`research_budget.py:522-534`). - A blocked day (any breached row) rejects further reservations with reason "daily token budget is blocked by a prior usage breach" (`research_budget.py:310-314`) — the operator must presumably resolve the offending row before the day can resume, though I did not see an explicit "unblock" API. - No recovery/retry logic exists for the strategy-evaluation or shortlist step itself; a fresh invocation just recomputes. 
- **Terminal states.**  - Shortlist output: `ShortlistResult(entries, excluded)` (`selector.py:27-34`) — in-memory, ephemeral. - Research decision terminals: SEND_TO_RESEARCH (further work), REUSE_RESEARCH, RUN_FULL_RESEARCH, SKIP_LOW_SIGNAL, SKIP_DAILY_CANDIDATE_CAP, SKIP_DAILY_TOKEN_CAP. - Reservation terminals: SETTLED, RELEASED. AMBIGUOUS is intentionally non-terminal until reconciled. 
- **Fail open or closed.** fail-closed
- **File refs.** `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/strategies/selector.py:1-119`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/strategies/config.py:109-283`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/strategies/contracts.py:34-204`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/strategies/safety_gates.py:1-48`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/strategies/momentum_breakout.py:24-194`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/strategies/mean_reversion.py:1-50`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/strategies/event_catalyst.py:24-320`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/strategies/research_budget.py:1-535`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/strategies/execution_boundary.py:1-140`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/strategies/backtest_adapter.py:1-89`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/research/token_budget.py:1-97`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/config/strategies.yaml:1-73`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/docs/strategy-candidate-selection.md:19-111`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/tests/unit/test_strategy_selector.py:1-194`
- **Suspected defects.**
  - No production orchestrator in `src/` currently invokes `select_shortlist` or constructs `MomentumBreakoutStrategy` / `MeanReversionStrategy` / `EventDrivenCatalystStrategy` (Grep on constructors and on `select_shortlist(` shows only tests). The end-to-end workflow described in `docs/strategy-candidate-selection.md:19-32` is not wired anywhere; only the individual pieces exist. This matches `activation_stage: STAGE_2_HISTORICAL_BACKTEST` in `config/strategies.yaml:20`, but the docstring in `strategies/research_budget.py:1-10` implies a caller that does not exist. NeedsVerification: whether a scheduled runner is intended to be added or whether a hidden wiring exists outside `src/`.
  - `ShortlistConfig.minimum_signal_strength_for_research` is enforced only in `decide_research_action` (`research_budget.py:67-72`), not in `select_shortlist`. A low-strength eligible signal can therefore consume a `maximum_combined_shortlist` slot (`selector.py:108-111`) that a higher-strength candidate above the minimum could otherwise have used, since selection ordering is done before budget filtering. Config currently defaults `minimum_signal_strength_for_research: 0.0` (`config/strategies.yaml:64`), masking this in practice. NeedsVerification: intended ordering of the strength filter vs. the combined-shortlist cap.
  - `DailyResearchBudgetState.symbols_sent_today` / `fresh_cycles_today` / `tokens_spent_today` are caller-supplied non-persistent counters (`research_budget.py:40-53`). Unlike `reserve_research_tokens`, they are not derived from `shadow_budget_reservations`, so two concurrent invocations of `decide_research_action` could both see room under `maximum_symbols_to_research_per_day` / `maximum_fresh_research_cycles_per_day`. Only the token reservation itself is atomic. NeedsVerification: whether the intended caller reads these from a persistent source.
  - `StrategyConfig.strategy_candidate_selection_enabled` is only consulted by `load_strategy_config` to reject `enabled=true` at STAGE_1/2 (`config.py:185-191`). It is not read inside `select_shortlist` or `decide_research_action`, so if a caller ever exists it must gate on this flag itself; otherwise the flag has no runtime effect. NeedsVerification.
  - In `_symbol_allocation_decision` (`selector.py:71-79`), when `symbol not in portfolio.symbol_exposure_fraction` and `symbol not in portfolio.existing_positions`, `current` is treated as `0.0` even if `symbol_exposure_complete=True`. Combined with the check `remaining >= config.minimum_candidate_allocation_fraction`, this behavior looks correct per docstring; but note that `symbol_exposure_fraction` is a float and float subtraction (`config.maximum_symbol_allocation_fraction - current`) can drift near the boundary — a symbol whose exposure equals the cap due to float rounding might be treated as still-having-room. NeedsVerification (low severity).
  - `reserve_research_tokens` treats an existing idempotent row of the wrong `reservation_kind` (i.e. someone reused a cost-reservation key for a token reservation) as `TOKEN_ACCOUNTING_POLICY_MISMATCH` (`research_budget.py:283-290`). The error code label is misleading — kind mismatch is a schema/collision problem, not an accounting-policy problem — but the fail-closed effect is correct. Minor.
  - The token controller's exception classification in `research/token_budget.py:66-77` catches `ProviderUnavailableError` and releases the reservation, but any other exception (including e.g. an `AssertionError` or user KeyboardInterrupt inside `provider.generate_structured`) marks AMBIGUOUS. If `generate_structured` raises before transmission for a non-provider reason (e.g. a bug in request serialization), the reservation is held pending operator reconciliation even though no request left the process. NeedsVerification: whether classification of `BaseException` here is intentionally conservative or should distinguish pre-transmission bugs from post-transmission ambiguity.
  - The activation-stage gate (`config.py:185-191`) forbids `enabled=true` only at STAGE_1/STAGE_2, but a caller at STAGE_3+ could still trigger the shortlist even if `enabled=false` (config permits `enabled=false` at any stage). Since `strategy_candidate_selection_enabled` is not consulted at runtime by the selector, the enabling switch is essentially decorative. NeedsVerification.

#### Two parallel submission stacks share the "ambiguous submission / crash recovery" pattern.

STACK A - Milestone 3/4 credentialed paper-broker submission (isolated runtime).
Entry: submit_credentialed_paper_order (src/trading_research/services/submit_credentialed_paper_order.py:58-179). Idempotency: `paper_broker_submissions` row written PENDING_SUBMISSION *before* the runtime is contacted (lines 97-98; storage/execution_repositories.py:313-331 is a no-op on replay). Client-order-id derived deterministically from intent_id (runtime/client/models.py:17-24). Intent uniqueness enforced by intent_id PK + UNIQUE(recommendation_id, execution_version) (storage/execution_schema.py:16-36). Pre-submit recovery lookup: lines 104-128 always call client.get_order(client_order_id) before ever calling submit_order; found -> UPSERT status and return SUBMITTED without resubmit. Ambiguous submit outcome: on RuntimeUnavailable/Timeout/RuntimeOperationError (lines 131-170), performs exactly one bounded recovery get_order lookup (line 143); recovered -> SUBMITTED; recovered=None or lookup raised -> submission_status set to "SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN" and paper_execution_failures appended (lines 159-166). Never blind-resubmits. Poll/recovery loop: services/sync_paper_orders.py::sync_paper_orders (lines 56-63) is a bounded single-pass sweep invoked by CLI (cli.py:483-513); reads non-terminal submissions (storage/execution_repositories.py:354-361 filters out FILLED/CANCELLED/REJECTED/ERROR) and calls get_order per row. Fills become PaperExecutionEvent (sync_paper_orders.py:107-123) with event_id = intent_id-sync-{event_type}-{cumulative_filled}; incremental delta = cumulative - sum(prior events) (lines 84-94). Terminal states persist PaperExecutionResult (lines 126-135). Ledger boundary: execution/ledger_events.py::apply_paper_execution_event first persists event row (save_event no-ops on duplicate event_id, storage/execution_repositories.py:130-149), then applies to PaperLedger inside DuplicateOrderError trap, then flips ledger_applied flag (execution/ledger_events.py:26-69) -- belt-and-suspenders idempotency across the ledger write and the flag update. Transport-level crash defenses in runtime/client/process_client.py: any request timeout marks the transport permanently unhealthy (lines 176-241, 282-297); a mutating operation is never automatically retried on a fresh restart, only allow-listed read-only follow-ups like get_order are (lines 38-44, 259-273); a process EOF during a request is never interpreted as an order outcome (lines 299-306).

STACK B - Milestone 11 external Alpaca paper order coordinator.
Entry: submit_external_paper_order (src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1319-1366) via CLI external_paper_submit_cli (cli.py:361-372). Local state machine STATE_NOT_SUBMITTED -> STATE_PREVIEWED -> STATE_SUBMISSION_REQUESTED -> {SUBMITTED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED, STATE_UNKNOWN=UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_RECONCILIATION} enforced by _TRANSITIONS (lines 64-86). Authoritative validation: _intent (lines 502-582) reverifies frozen intent, risk/exit approval, terminal-status refusal, execution-namespace claim, TIF DAY, LIMIT, quantity/side, oversell for SELL, stale/future timestamps. _safety_checks (lines 585-607) refuses if a shadow safety pause is active, any CRITICAL alert unresolved, or any critical reconciliation still outstanding (except the specific allow-listed retry evidence path). _require_reconciliation_baseline (lines 729-745) fails closed if no baseline is on file or fingerprint drifted. Preview: preview_external_paper_order (lines 918-985) claims the EXTERNAL_PAPER execution namespace (fenced write; ExecutionNamespaceConflictError => INTENT_NOT_ELIGIBLE_FOR_EXTERNAL), performs read-only runtime preview, writes preview row + PREVIEWED event under fenced write. Submit path: acquires order-scope lease keyed on (book_id, client_order_id) (lines 875-916 via storage/paper_books_repositories.py:2035-2062 acquire_external_order_lease; ttl per PaperBooksConfiguration.external_broker.order_lease_ttl_seconds, default 45s, heartbeat 10s). Fresh account fingerprint verify + baseline check + validated preview. _reserve_daily_notional (lines 230-344) atomically claims an attempt-scoped reservation keyed by (client_order_id, attempt_number, account_fingerprint, reservation_date) inside its own BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction, enforcing external_broker.maximum_daily_notional_usd. _submit_once (lines 1188-1286): under lease.fenced_write, reserves cash/shares (cash_ledger.reserve_for_order or positions.reserve_shares_for_sell) + appends SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event in one transaction (durable crash-checkpoint before runtime.submit_limit_order is called). Then calls runtime.submit_limit_order. On any exception -> _record_unknown appends STATE_UNKNOWN event and settles reservation to RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED (lines 1171-1185, 361-374). On success: _validate_order_response enforces shape/notional/timestamp invariants (lines 1118-1168), _state_from_order maps broker->local status (lines 1011-1022), fenced-writes success event + updates order status + settles reservation, then apply_external_fills sweeps normalized fills (lines 1369-1543) idempotently by pebf_ hashed external_fill_id (repo.fill_exists guard), applying buy/sell fills, cash settle, position release atomically per-fill under fenced_or_plain_write, then post-submit reconciliation via _reconcile_locked. Duplicate detection: _detect_duplicate_broker_order (lines 1028-1116) scans up to 100 recent broker orders and flags same-shape orders within a 300s window regardless of client-order-id prefix. Ambiguous / crash recovery: recover_stranded_submission (lines 1635-1696) is a dedicated, read-only recovery entry for an order stranded at SUBMISSION_REQUESTED (crash between the reservation+checkpoint commit and any broker response); delegates to _reconcile_locked, which calls _bridge_stranded_submission_requested to append a STATE_UNKNOWN event before persisting authoritative lookup evidence (lines 2014-2082) so retry_external_paper_order can eventually match on the correct event id. Retry: retry_external_paper_order (lines 1546-1632) requires current state STATE_UNKNOWN, requires a fresh, authoritative, unconsumed broker NOT_FOUND lookup whose ambiguous_event_id/attempt_number/payload_hash/account_fingerprint/book_id/client_order_id all match (lines 1561-1575), requires attempt_number < maximum_retry_attempts, re-checks account fingerprint, requires a matching APPROVED preview, transitions the prior reservation to SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY atomically with creating the new attempt reservation (lines 294-327), then calls _submit_once with the incremented attempt_number and consumes the lookup row (lines 1623-1626). Preview-refresh escape hatch: refresh_retry_preview (lines 1699-1768) is a read-only DB-only rewrite of a preview that has expired but for which fresh authoritative NOT_FOUND evidence still exists; never calls the broker/runtime; never consumes the lookup. Reconciliation: reconcile_external_paper_order (lines 1925-1956) is the public entry that resolves the target order and acquires the lease; _reconcile_locked (lines 1959-1986) wraps _run_reconciliation and, on any unexpected exception, persists a RECONCILIATION_INTERNAL_ERROR critical evidence row -- never exits silently. _run_reconciliation (lines 1989-2282) fetches broker order, saves authoritative peol_ lookup evidence, bridges SUBMISSION_REQUESTED->UNKNOWN before the lookup save so ambiguous_event_id matches the freshly appended event, transitions the local state if _TRANSITIONS allows, applies fills, checks account fingerprint history, symbol/side/quantity/price mismatch, fill quantity mismatch, cash-and-position baseline-delta comparison, and duplicate detection. _persist_reconciliation (lines 2285-...) stamps critical=1 when any of CRITICAL_RECONCILIATION_STATUSES (lines 53-62) applies.

Cross-cutting: PaperReconciliationReport account/position reconciliation lives in services/reconcile_paper.py + execution/account_reconciliation.py + execution/reconciliation.py; these are pure comparators that never repair mismatches, they only report and persist (paper_account_reconciliations, paper_position_reconciliations tables via storage/execution_repositories.py:367-424).

- **Entry point.** Two entry points share the ambiguous/crash-recovery pattern: (1) services/submit_credentialed_paper_order.py::submit_credentialed_paper_order (:58) and its poller services/sync_paper_orders.py::sync_paper_orders (:56); (2) paper_books/external_broker.py::submit_external_paper_order (:1319), the dedicated recovery hook recover_stranded_submission (:1635), retry_external_paper_order (:1546), refresh_retry_preview (:1699), and reconcile_external_paper_order (:1925). CLI wrappers in src/trading_research/cli.py:361-513 (external_paper_submit_cli, external_paper_reconcile_cli, external_paper_retry_cli, external_paper_refresh_retry_preview_cli, sync_paper_orders_cli, reconcile_paper_cli).
- **Authoritative validation.** Stack A: intent uniqueness enforced by intent_id PK + UNIQUE(recommendation_id, execution_version) in storage/execution_schema.py:16-36. PaperOrderIntent tz-awareness / policy checks in execution/models.py. runtime/client/process_client.py::start (:187-220) verifies protocol version, paper_endpoint_verified, broker_mode=='paper', real_money_disabled=true, capabilities.real_money=false, no short/options/margin, LIMIT-only, BUY/SELL only. runtime/client/models.py:17-24 stabilizes client_order_id. Stack B: paper_books/external_broker.py::_intent (:502) revalidates frozen intent (order type LIMIT, TIF DAY, positive whole quantity, side allow-list, oversell for SELL, stale/future timestamps, terminal-status refusal, execution-namespace claim). _safety_checks (:585) refuses when shadow pause or any unresolved CRITICAL alert or any critical reconciliation is active (allow-list only for the exact ambiguous-retry client_order_id whose latest reconciliation is ORDER_MISSING_AT_BROKER). _require_external_config (:486), _require_reconciliation_baseline (:729), _account_check (:610), _verify_fingerprint_history (:759), _validated_preview (:988), _validate_order_response (:1118), _validate_frozen_notional (:142). retry_external_paper_order (:1561-1575) requires fresh, unconsumed, authoritative broker NOT_FOUND lookup exactly matching ambiguous_event_id/attempt_number/payload_hash/account_fingerprint/book_id/client_order_id.
- **Idempotency key.** Stack A: client_order_id = intent_id (runtime/client/models.py:17-24). intent_id = "intent-" + 32-hex derived from (recommendation_id, execution_version) (see runtime/client/models.py comment and execution/models.py::derive_intent_id). paper_broker_submissions is PK by intent_id with UNIQUE(client_order_id) (storage/execution_schema.py:91-93). paper_execution_events keyed by event_id (typically intent_id-sync-{type}-{cumulative_filled}, sync_paper_orders.py:117-123). PaperLedger's simulated_orders.idempotency_key = event_id (ledger_events.py:52-59). Stack B: derive_external_order_identity (external_broker.py:431-444) builds a broker-safe client_order_id `epb-{book_id}-` + sha256 hex of frozen immutable payload; payload_hash is sha256 of the same. Attempt reservation id = derive_external_attempt_reservation_id(client_order_id, attempt_number, account_fingerprint, reservation_date) (:183-186 and storage/paper_books_schema.py). External event id = peoe_ digest of client_order_id/prev/new/now/request_id/attempt_number (:463-466). External broker fill id = pebf_ digest of book_id + fill_id (:1399-1400). Preview id = pepv_ digest.
- **Transaction boundary.** Stack A: every save_* helper (storage/execution_repositories.py) calls conn.commit() individually. save_intent (line 89), create_pending_submission (line 313), update_submission_status (line 334), save_event (line 130), mark_event_ledger_applied (line 152), save_result (line 194), save_reconciliation (line 234) — each is its own commit. There is no single wrapping transaction across create_pending_submission + get_order + update; each step is independently durable, which is how idempotency after a crash is preserved. Stack B: order-scope lease + fenced writes. `_order_lease` (external_broker.py:875-916) acquires a single-row conditional INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (paper_books_repositories.py:2035-2062) and releases in finally. `OrderLeaseHandle.fenced_write` (:846-862) issues BEGIN IMMEDIATE, verifies lease ownership+generation+expiry inside the write lock, yields, and commits on success or rollbacks on exception. `_reserve_daily_notional` (:230-344) intentionally runs its own top-level BEGIN IMMEDIATE / commit outside the caller's lease-transaction so cross-book/process races against the same account/day are serialized. `_submit_once` bundles the pre-submit reservation write + SUBMISSION_REQUESTED checkpoint into one fenced_write transaction (:1210-1226) — either both commit or neither, so no unaccompanied reservation orphan can result from a crash.
- **Lease or concurrency.** Stack A: no cross-process lease; concurrency depends on: (a) SQLite serializing writes; (b) client-order-id derived from intent_id so a re-invocation collides on the paper_broker_submissions.intent_id PK / client_order_id UNIQUE; (c) get_order-before-submit ensuring a prior attempt is detected. Also the RuntimeClient marks itself unhealthy after any timeout (process_client.py:176-241) to guarantee stdout-queue desync cannot poison a subsequent request. Stack B: renewable, fenced order-scope lease keyed on (book_id, client_order_id) — paper_external_order_leases table with acquire_external_order_lease (paper_books_repositories.py:2035-2062), heartbeat_external_order_lease (:2065-2078), verify_external_order_lease (:2081-2093), release_external_order_lease (:2103-2122). OrderLeaseHandle uses fresh clock reads for every heartbeat/verify (external_broker.py:816-844) so a stale timestamp cannot wrongly confirm ownership. fenced_write does BEGIN IMMEDIATE then re-verifies generation before yielding. TTL default 45s (config.external_broker.order_lease_ttl_seconds), heartbeat 10s, both selected so a single-request runtime call (default 30s) can't expire mid-request. Execution-namespace claim (repo.claim_execution_namespace, external_broker.py:940-947) enforces per-intent EXTERNAL_PAPER vs LOCAL_SIMULATED exclusivity.
- **State transitions.** Stack A submission_status: PENDING_SUBMISSION -> {SUBMITTED, ACCEPTED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, ERROR, SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN}; SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN can later be re-observed as any live state by the sync poller. TERMINAL_SUBMISSION_STATES = {FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, ERROR} (execution/broker_snapshots.py:37). Enum enforced by BrokerOrderSubmission.__post_init__ (:60-70). Stack B external order state chain: NOT_SUBMITTED -> PREVIEWED -> SUBMISSION_REQUESTED -> {SUBMITTED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED, STATE_UNKNOWN}; SUBMITTED/PARTIALLY_FILLED -> {SUBMITTED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, CANCEL_REQUESTED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED, UNKNOWN}; CANCEL_REQUESTED -> {CANCEL_REQUESTED, CANCELLED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, UNKNOWN}; STATE_UNKNOWN -> {UNKNOWN, SUBMITTED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, CANCEL_REQUESTED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED, SUBMISSION_REQUESTED}; TERMINAL_STATES = {FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED} - immutable (external_broker.py:64-86). Attempt reservation states: RESERVED -> {SUBMITTED, RELEASED, RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED, SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY}; RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED -> {SUBMITTED, RELEASED, SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY} (_settle_daily_reservation and _reconcile_attempt_reservation, :347-420).
- **Persistent records.** Stack A tables (storage/execution_schema.py): paper_execution_intents (intent_id PK, UNIQUE(recommendation_id, execution_version)), paper_execution_events (event_id PK, ledger_applied flag), paper_execution_results (intent_id PK), paper_execution_reconciliations (intent_id PK), paper_execution_failures (append-only audit), paper_broker_submissions (intent_id PK, UNIQUE client_order_id), paper_account_reconciliations (append-only history), paper_position_reconciliations (append-only history), plus PaperLedger's simulated_orders (idempotency_key UNIQUE via paper/ledger.py). Stack B tables (storage/paper_books_schema.py + paper_books_repositories.py): paper_external_order_events (append-only chain per (book, client_order_id)), paper_external_order_previews, paper_external_order_lookups (peol_ authoritative broker lookup evidence), paper_external_broker_fills, paper_external_order_reconciliations (with critical flag), paper_external_reconciliation_baselines, paper_external_attempt_reservations, paper_external_order_leases, execution_namespace_claims, plus the shared paper_book_orders and paper_book_fills, paper_external_position_reservation_events, cash_ledger tables.
- **External side effects.** Only via the isolated `paper_runtime` subprocess speaking paper-runtime.v2 JSON-Lines over stdin/stdout — the main process never imports LumiBot or a broker SDK (runtime/client/process_client.py:1-9). Startup gate refuses to talk to any runtime that has not proven paper_endpoint_verified, broker_mode='paper', real_money_disabled=true, no short/options/margin capabilities, LIMIT-only order type (:187-220). Mutating operations issued: submit_order (Stack A), SUBMIT_LIMIT_ORDER (Stack B), CANCEL_ORDER (Stack B cancel). Every operation is single-attempt and non-retried by the client (:330-335, 368-370). All responses go through schema validation (_validate_order_response external_broker.py:1118-1168; sync_paper_orders.py:81-99 for Stack A) before any local state advances. Recovery lookups (get_order / GET_ORDER_BY_CLIENT_ID) are read-only. reconcile_paper.reconcile_paper_account_and_positions also calls get_account and list_positions read-only; it never repairs mismatches (execution/account_reconciliation.py:1-89, services/reconcile_paper.py:1-63).
- **Failure behavior.** Stack A: ambiguous submit (runtime error) triggers one bounded get_order recovery lookup; if not found, the paper_broker_submissions row is set to SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN and paper_execution_failures is appended (submit_credentialed_paper_order.py:159-170). Never blind-resubmits. Transport failures mark the RuntimeClient permanently unhealthy (process_client.py:226-241, 285-297) so future mutating operations fail closed with RuntimeUnavailableError; only allow-listed read-only ops (`_RETRYABLE_ON_TIMEOUT`, :38-44) transparently start a clean child. Process EOF mid-request is never interpreted as a fill/reject (:299-306). Stack B: on any exception from runtime.submit_limit_order, _record_unknown appends STATE_UNKNOWN and settles the attempt reservation to RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED (external_broker.py:1231-1240, 1171-1185, 361-374). apply_external_fills failures inside _submit_once persist a critical MALFORMED_BROKER_FILL/FILL_APPLICATION_FAILED reconciliation and reraise (lines 1269-1283). _reconcile_locked (:1959-1986) is a fail-safe wrapper: any unexpected exception persists a RECONCILIATION_INTERNAL_ERROR (critical) evidence row before propagating. New submissions are blocked by _safety_checks whenever an unresolved critical reconciliation exists, so the system fails closed until a human clears it.
- **Retry behavior.** Stack A: no automatic retry. submit_order is never retried by the client (process_client.py:330-335). services/sync_paper_orders.py is a bounded single-pass sweep (:56-63) — CLI/orchestrator schedules any cadence. paper_execution_events idempotency via event_id (storage/execution_repositories.py:125-149); ledger idempotency via ledger_applied flag + PaperLedger's own idempotency_key (execution/ledger_events.py:26-69). Stack B: retries are exclusively operator-initiated via retry_external_paper_order (external_broker.py:1546). Preconditions: current state STATE_UNKNOWN, fresh authoritative unconsumed NOT_FOUND lookup with matching ambiguous_event_id/attempt_number/payload_hash/account_fingerprint/book_id/client_order_id, attempt_number < external_broker.maximum_retry_attempts, matching APPROVED preview (or a refresh_retry_preview replacement, :1699), matching account fingerprint. New attempt uses attempt_number+1 with a new attempt-scoped reservation, the prior reservation atomically transitioning to SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY (lines 294-327). Cross-day retries create a new date-scoped reservation. On retry success the lookup row is consumed (:1623-1626) so the same evidence cannot approve a second retry.
- **Recovery behavior.** Stack A: submit_credentialed_paper_order always calls get_order before submit (:104-128), so a crash between the PENDING_SUBMISSION insert and the runtime call is harmless. On ambiguous outcome the row moves to SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN; the poll sweep (sync_paper_orders) continues to try get_order each pass because list_unresolved_submissions selects everything not in FILLED/CANCELLED/REJECTED/ERROR (storage/execution_repositories.py:354-361). Stack B: recover_stranded_submission (external_broker.py:1635-1696) is the crash-recovery hook for an order stranded at SUBMISSION_REQUESTED. It only calls read-only get_order_by_client_order_id via _reconcile_locked and never submit_limit_order/preview_limit_order. If the broker still doesn't have the order (or the lookup itself errors), _bridge_stranded_submission_requested appends a STATE_UNKNOWN event (lines 2024-2038, 2263-2273) so that the fully-gated retry path becomes reachable. The bridging is emitted *before* the authoritative peol_ lookup row is written so lookup.ambiguous_event_id references the new STATE_UNKNOWN event, matching retry's later match criteria. Idempotent: a second call finds the chain already moved off SUBMISSION_REQUESTED and returns already_recovered.
- **Terminal states.** Stack A: paper_broker_submissions terminal set = {FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, ERROR} (execution/broker_snapshots.py:37); list_unresolved_submissions omits these (storage/execution_repositories.py:354-361). PaperExecutionResult with final_status in {FILLED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, ERROR} (execution/models.py:37). Stack B: TERMINAL_STATES = {STATE_FILLED, STATE_CANCELLED, STATE_REJECTED, STATE_EXPIRED} - immutable, cannot transition further per _TRANSITIONS (external_broker.py:64-86); STATE_UNKNOWN is explicitly non-terminal and retains the reservation (comments at 349-374). Queue display derives BLOCKED_BY_RECONCILIATION when a non-terminal chain still has a critical reconciliation outstanding (external_broker.py:1876-1897).
- **Fail open or closed.** fail-closed
- **File refs.** `src/trading_research/services/submit_credentialed_paper_order.py`, `src/trading_research/services/sync_paper_orders.py`, `src/trading_research/services/reconcile_paper.py`, `src/trading_research/execution/broker_snapshots.py`, `src/trading_research/execution/reconciliation.py`, `src/trading_research/execution/account_reconciliation.py`, `src/trading_research/execution/ledger_events.py`, `src/trading_research/execution/models.py`, `src/trading_research/runtime/client/process_client.py`, `src/trading_research/runtime/client/models.py`, `src/trading_research/storage/execution_repositories.py`, `src/trading_research/storage/execution_schema.py`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py`, `src/trading_research/cli.py`
- **Suspected defects.**
  - POSSIBLE (NeedsVerification): sync_paper_orders._sync_one does not check the fenced-write invariant used by the paper_books stack; apply_all_new_events -> save_event and mark_event_ledger_applied each conn.commit() independently (storage/execution_repositories.py:130-156) while update_submission_status is a separate commit (sync_paper_orders.py:96-99). A crash between save_event/apply and update_submission_status can leave the ledger correctly applied but the submission row still showing the previous status. Because the poller re-reads via get_order each pass this is likely self-healing, but there is no explicit transaction bracketing.
  - POSSIBLE (NeedsVerification): submit_credentialed_paper_order.py:159-166 sets submission_status='SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN' and increments attempt_count on a runtime error, but there is no maximum retry cap or backoff for a caller that re-invokes this service after a lookup returned None. Re-invocation triggers the pre-submit get_order branch (line 104) which finds nothing and proceeds to submit_order again — this is the intended 'never blindly resubmit' contract via the pre-lookup, but a persistently ambiguous broker (get_order timing out) will oscillate: each call is a fresh submit attempt, distinct from the Stack B retry gate that requires authoritative NOT_FOUND evidence. Verify whether operational cadence assumes the outer CLI is invoked only after a human confirms.
  - POSSIBLE (NeedsVerification): sync_paper_orders._sync_one line 82 raises RuntimeOperationError with retryable=False on any status outside EVENT_TYPES. Stack B's runtime status set is a superset (includes ACCEPTED, EXPIRED, CANCEL_REQUESTED); if the credentialed runtime ever emits one of those statuses, the poller aborts on that intent with no state update, leaving the paper_broker_submissions row in whatever non-terminal state it was in and no failure row (record_failure is only called for transport-level errors on lines 70-73). A stuck submission then depends entirely on human intervention.
  - POSSIBLE (NeedsVerification): external_broker.py:1230-1240 catches bare `Exception` around runtime.submit_limit_order and converts to STATE_UNKNOWN with error_code = getattr(exc, 'code', 'RUNTIME_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN'). Because RuntimeUnavailableError, RuntimeRequestTimeoutError, and RuntimeOperationError all derive from RuntimeError (not ExternalPaperError), they lack a .code attribute for the ones without it — falling back to RUNTIME_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN loses signal about credential-expired vs network-timeout. Also, an OrderLeaseLostError raised from lease.fenced_write() at line 1210 would be caught here and misclassified as STATE_UNKNOWN instead of surfacing lease-loss to the operator.
  - POSSIBLE (NeedsVerification): retry_external_paper_order.py:1600-1615 loads prior_reservation via load_active_attempt_reservation for current['attempt_number']. If a preceding _reconcile_attempt_reservation already transitioned that reservation to RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED, the prior reservation is 'active' in a state _reserve_daily_notional's supersede validation accepts (:316-318). But if the prior reservation has already been transitioned to SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY by an earlier retry that then rolled back, load_active_attempt_reservation would return None and this raises ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_MISSING. Verify recovery from a rolled-back retry that succeeded in transitioning the prior reservation but failed later.
  - POSSIBLE (NeedsVerification): recover_stranded_submission holds only the order-scope lease during _reconcile_locked (external_broker.py:1685-1696); it does not itself call _safety_checks or _require_reconciliation_baseline. If shadow safety pause or a critical alert is active, a stranded order can still be advanced from SUBMISSION_REQUESTED to STATE_UNKNOWN and lookups persisted. Intended? Downstream retry_external_paper_order does re-run _safety_checks, so exposure is not increased — but the local state chain still advances during a pause.
  - POSSIBLE (NeedsVerification): submit_credentialed_paper_order.py:80-86 records a failure for eligibility and returns REJECTED_INELIGIBLE, but does not create the paper_broker_submissions PENDING_SUBMISSION row. That is correct — no client_order_id has been generated. However, a re-invocation with a now-eligible policy would build an intent and derive the same client_order_id and pre-submit lookup; there is no evidence that the prior REJECTED_INELIGIBLE outcome persists as an intent row (save_intent runs only when eligible, line 88), so failure history for this recommendation is available in paper_execution_failures but not in intents. Ensure observability queries join failures explicitly.
  - POSSIBLE (NeedsVerification): external_broker.py:2189 uses `order.get('filled_quantity', local_fill_qty)` as the fallback when the broker order dict is missing the field. Because _validate_order_response above requires filled_quantity as part of the expected shape (:1121-1128), reaching this branch means order_valid was False. Silently substituting local_fill_qty then computes FILL_QUANTITY_MISMATCH=False even though the broker payload was malformed. Verify whether MALFORMED_BROKER_ORDER already covers this in statuses.
  - POSSIBLE (NeedsVerification): _release_terminal_reservation is skipped for STATE_FILLED (external_broker.py:1304-1305), leaving cash/share reservation intact until apply_external_fills sweeps and consumes it. If the fill sweep silently returns [] (e.g., runtime lacks list_order_fills capability, raises AttributeError at line 1376), the reservation remains held forever for a FILLED order. External reconciliation should catch this via RESERVATION_MISMATCH (:2168-2175), but this depends on `remaining` being negative — a fully-filled order where fills were not applied has remaining > 0, not < 0, so this check does not fire.
  - POSSIBLE (NeedsVerification): _reserve_daily_notional runs its own top-level BEGIN IMMEDIATE / commit outside the caller's lease-transaction (:265). A crash between _reserve_daily_notional's commit and _submit_once's fenced_write can leave an active RESERVED attempt reservation with no corresponding SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event. There is no periodic sweeper visible that reconciles orphan RESERVED reservations to RELEASED; the retry path assumes the prior attempt has an event chain to validate against. Recovery for this case depends on operator noticing the daily-notional cap silently reduced.

#### Account & reservation reconciliation runs in two distinct layers.

LAYER A — Milestone 4 simple paper account/position reconciliation
- Entry: `reconcile_paper_cli(db_path)` at src/trading_research/cli.py:516-550 (dispatched from the `legacy-paper-reconcile` command at src/trading_research/cli.py:3087-3092).
- Orchestrator: `reconcile_paper_account_and_positions(...)` at src/trading_research/services/reconcile_paper.py:30-62. Builds a runtime client (started/shutdown around a single `session(db_path)` block, cli.py:525-537), pulls `client.get_account()` and `client.list_positions()`, materializes `BrokerAccountSnapshot`/`BrokerPositionSnapshot`, compares to `PaperLedger.total_cash()`/`ledger.positions()` via pure comparators in src/trading_research/execution/account_reconciliation.py:22-88 (`reconcile_account`, `reconcile_all_positions`, `reconcile_position`).
- Persistence: `save_account_reconciliation` and `save_position_reconciliation` at src/trading_research/storage/execution_repositories.py:367-406 (append-only rows into `paper_account_reconciliations` / `paper_position_reconciliations`, defined at src/trading_research/storage/execution_schema.py:102-128).
- Terminal statuses: `MATCHED`, `CASH_MISMATCH`, `POSITION_MISMATCH`, `MISSING_BROKER_POSITION`, `MISSING_INTERNAL_POSITION` (account_reconciliation.py:26-61). Never repairs — only records mismatches.

LAYER B — External paper broker reservation-aware reconciliation (Milestone 11 / 11.3.1 / 23 / 24 / 25)
- Public entries:
  * `reconcile_external_paper_order(...)` at src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1925-1956 (CLI shim: `external_paper_reconcile_cli` at src/trading_research/cli.py:375-384).
  * `recover_stranded_submission(...)` at external_broker.py:1635-1696 for the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED crash-recovery path.
  * Inline reconciliation is also called by `submit_external_paper_order` (external_broker.py:1319-1366), `retry_external_paper_order` (external_broker.py:1546-1632) and `cancel_external_paper_order` (external_broker.py:1771-1864) after their own broker calls settle.
- Core state machine: `_reconcile_locked` (external_broker.py:1959-1987) → `_run_reconciliation` (external_broker.py:1989-2282). Reads current event, verifies lease, validates frozen intent notional (`_validate_frozen_notional`, external_broker.py:142-173), runs `_account_check` (external_broker.py:610-623), loads baseline (external_broker.py:2058-2063), does authoritative `get_order_by_client_order_id` broker lookup (external_broker.py:2066-2068), saves the lookup evidence (repo.save_external_lookup, external_broker.py:2083-2095), transitions attempt reservation to a target state (`_reconcile_attempt_reservation`, external_broker.py:377-420, called at 2097-2100, 2134-2137, 2141-2144), optionally sweeps fills (`apply_external_fills`, external_broker.py:1369-1543), performs delta-baseline cash/position comparison (external_broker.py:2225-2254), bridges stranded SUBMISSION_REQUESTED chains to STATE_UNKNOWN (external_broker.py:2024-2038, 2273), and finally persists a `paper_external_reconciliations` row via `_persist_reconciliation` (external_broker.py:2285-2303).
- Daily reservation life-cycle: `_reserve_daily_notional` (external_broker.py:230-344), `_settle_daily_reservation` (external_broker.py:347-374), `_reconcile_attempt_reservation` (external_broker.py:377-420) drive transitions RESERVED → SUBMITTED / RELEASED / RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED / SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY through `repo.transition_attempt_reservation_state` (storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1853-1883) with allowed-source predicates.

- **Entry point.** Layer A: `reconcile_paper_cli` (src/trading_research/cli.py:516-550) → `reconcile_paper_account_and_positions` (src/trading_research/services/reconcile_paper.py:30-62). Layer B (external paper): `external_paper_reconcile_cli` (src/trading_research/cli.py:375-384) → `reconcile_external_paper_order` (src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1925-1956) → `_reconcile_locked` → `_run_reconciliation` (external_broker.py:1959-2282). Recovery entry: `recover_stranded_submission` (external_broker.py:1635-1696). Reconciliation is also called after submit/retry/cancel at external_broker.py:1362-1365, 1628-1631, 1953-1955.
- **Authoritative validation.** - Layer A: pure comparators reconcile_account/reconcile_position in src/trading_research/execution/account_reconciliation.py:22-88 use `abs(broker - ledger) <= tolerance` (DEFAULT_TOLERANCE=Decimal("0.01") in services/reconcile_paper.py:21). - Layer B: multiple authoritative gates in `_run_reconciliation`:   * frozen-intent notional recomputation (`_validate_frozen_notional`, external_broker.py:142-173) checks quantity*limit_price == stored notional_usd, matches approved-risk-decision notional for BUY, and enforces min(risk.max_order_notional_usd, external_broker.maximum_order_notional_usd).   * `_account_check` (external_broker.py:610-623) requires provider=alpaca_paper, environment=paper, matching book_id, valid fingerprint prefix.   * fingerprint match vs current event (external_broker.py:2050-2053).   * `paper_external_reconciliation_baseline` must exist and match fingerprint (external_broker.py:2058-2063) — otherwise `RECONCILIATION_BASELINE_MISSING` and delta compare is skipped (external_broker.py:2225-2254).   * broker response shape validation (`_validate_order_response`, state derivation `_state_from_order`, and strict payload set checks in external_broker.py:2200-2216).   * Fill validation in `apply_external_fills` (external_broker.py:1369-1543) — payload shape, whole-share qty, positive price, book/side/symbol/fingerprint match, timestamp bounds (`_CLOCK_SKEW = 5s`, not_before), and cumulative-fill advancement.   * Delta-baseline compare (external_broker.py:2242-2254) computes expected broker qty and cash from local delta vs baseline.   * `_reconcile_attempt_reservation` (external_broker.py:377-420) enforces state-transition source predicates via `_ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_TRANSITION_SOURCES` in storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1853-1883.
- **Idempotency key.** - Layer A: none — every call appends a fresh row (rowid auto-increment) in paper_account_reconciliations / paper_position_reconciliations (execution_schema.py:102-128); `list_latest_position_reconciliations` (execution_repositories.py:409-424) picks the most recent per symbol. - Layer B:   * Reconciliation record: `reconciliation_id = _digest("per_", [book_id, client_order_id, statuses, details, now.isoformat()], 40)` (external_broker.py:2292-2294), inserted via `INSERT OR IGNORE` into `paper_external_reconciliations` (repo.save_external_reconciliation, storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1614-1628; table + immutability trigger at storage/paper_books_schema.py:683-696, 1368-1372).   * Attempt reservation: `derive_external_attempt_reservation_id(client_order_id, attempt_number, account_fingerprint, reservation_date)` (used at external_broker.py:183-186, 263).   * Order lease: `lease_key = f"{book_id}:{client_order_id}"` with per-owner UUID and monotonic `generation` (external_broker.py:897-908).   * Lookup: `_digest("peol_", [client_order_id, current["external_order_event_id"], lookup_result, request_id], 40)` (external_broker.py:2085-2088).   * External order events: `_digest("peoe_", [client_order_id, previous, new_state, now.isoformat(), runtime_request_id, attempt_number], 40)` (external_broker.py:463-476).   * Baseline: PRIMARY KEY (book_id) with INSERT OR IGNORE (paper_books_repositories.py:1649-1663) — captured exactly once per book.
- **Transaction boundary.** - Layer A: `session(db_path)` context in cli.py:526-537 wraps the read + save calls; each `save_*_reconciliation` runs a bare `conn.execute(...); conn.commit()` (execution_repositories.py:367-406), so each row commits independently. There is no BEGIN IMMEDIATE — position rows commit one at a time inside a single Python-level for-loop (services/reconcile_paper.py:59-60). - Layer B:   * Every mutation inside `_run_reconciliation` is gated via `lease.fenced_write()` which wraps a `begin_immediate(conn)` + `verify_or_raise` + commit/rollback (external_broker.py:846-862). All commit=False writes inside a fenced block commit atomically at block exit.   * `_reserve_daily_notional` deliberately opens its OWN `transaction(conn)` (BEGIN IMMEDIATE) at external_broker.py:265, NOT inside a `fenced_write` — the docstring at 254-259 explains this is required so the read-then-write budget check is atomic across books, and its superseding of a prior reservation is inside the same transaction as the new insert.   * `_persist_reconciliation` uses `_fenced_or_plain_write` (external_broker.py:2301-2302) — a plain `transaction(conn)` when called without a lease (recovery-time only) and fenced otherwise.   * `_reconcile_attempt_reservation` runs with `commit=False` inside fenced blocks (external_broker.py:2097-2100, 2134-2137, 2141-2144), and `transition_attempt_reservation_state` (paper_books_repositories.py:1865-1875) is a single conditional UPDATE with state predicate.
- **Lease or concurrency.** - Layer A: none. The `RuntimeClient.start()`/`shutdown()` pair is per-call; there is no cross-process locking on the reconciliation itself. Concurrent invocations would race and both append rows. - Layer B: order-scope lease keyed by `(book_id, client_order_id)` implemented via `_order_lease` (external_broker.py:875-915) and `OrderLeaseHandle` (external_broker.py:791-863).   * Acquisition: single conditional SQL write (`repo.acquire_external_order_lease` returning a generation), raises `ORDER_LEASE_HELD` if held.   * TTL: `_DEFAULT_ORDER_LEASE_TTL_SECONDS = 45` (external_broker.py:782) or `config.external_broker.order_lease_ttl_seconds`.   * Fencing: `fenced_write()` (external_broker.py:846-862) uses BEGIN IMMEDIATE + fresh clock verify of generation, so a stale owner cannot commit.   * Heartbeat/verify use fresh `_now(clock)` each call (external_broker.py:816-836).   * Recovery re-reads inside the lease to guard against a concurrent recovery/reconciliation call already advancing the chain (external_broker.py:1688-1692).   * Daily reservation lock is separate: its own `transaction(conn)` (BEGIN IMMEDIATE) at external_broker.py:265 serializes account/day budget check-and-reserve across books.
- **State transitions.** External event chain (`_TRANSITIONS`, external_broker.py:64-86): - NOT_SUBMITTED → PREVIEWED - PREVIEWED → PREVIEWED, SUBMISSION_REQUESTED - SUBMISSION_REQUESTED → SUBMITTED / PARTIALLY_FILLED / FILLED / CANCELLED / REJECTED / EXPIRED / UNKNOWN - SUBMITTED → SUBMITTED / PARTIALLY_FILLED / FILLED / CANCEL_REQUESTED / CANCELLED / REJECTED / EXPIRED / UNKNOWN - PARTIALLY_FILLED → PARTIALLY_FILLED / FILLED / CANCEL_REQUESTED / CANCELLED / UNKNOWN - CANCEL_REQUESTED → CANCEL_REQUESTED / CANCELLED / PARTIALLY_FILLED / FILLED / UNKNOWN - UNKNOWN → UNKNOWN / SUBMITTED / PARTIALLY_FILLED / FILLED / CANCEL_REQUESTED / CANCELLED / REJECTED / EXPIRED / SUBMISSION_REQUESTED - Terminal (empty): REJECTED / EXPIRED / CANCELLED / FILLED (external_broker.py:85).  Attempt reservation transitions (`_reservation_target_for_broker_state`, external_broker.py:423-428; `_settle_daily_reservation`, external_broker.py:347-374; `_reconcile_attempt_reservation`, external_broker.py:377-420): - RESERVED → SUBMITTED (any settled non-UNKNOWN, non-REJECTED broker state) - RESERVED → RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED (UNKNOWN/ambiguous or broker_state None) - RESERVED → RELEASED (REJECTED) - RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED → SUBMITTED or RELEASED (once broker outcome resolves) - RESERVED or RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED → SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY (cross-day retry with confirmed NOT_FOUND, atomic with new reservation insert; external_broker.py:294-327). - SUBMITTED is never downgraded to RELEASED/RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED by reconciliation (external_broker.py:405-408).  Bridging (crash recovery): SUBMISSION_REQUESTED → UNKNOWN via `_bridge_stranded_submission_requested` (external_broker.py:2024-2038), invoked both when broker lookup is NOT_FOUND/error (external_broker.py:2079-2081) and at the tail (external_broker.py:2273). Reservation stays RESERVED / RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED — exposure held.
- **Persistent records.** - Layer A: paper_account_reconciliations, paper_position_reconciliations (execution_schema.py:102-128). - Layer B: paper_external_order_events (immutable chain), paper_external_lookups (via repo.save_external_lookup, external_broker.py:2083-2095), paper_external_reconciliations (INSERT OR IGNORE + UPDATE/DELETE-blocking triggers, paper_books_schema.py:683-696, 1368-1372), paper_external_reconciliation_baseline (paper_books_schema.py:707-718), paper_external_order_leases (paper_books_schema.py:746-758, delete-blocking trigger at 1386-1387), paper_external_attempt_reservations (paper_books_schema.py:937 with core-field-immutability + no-delete triggers at 1434-1443), plus paper_book_fills / paper_book_positions / paper_book_cash_ledger side-effects from `apply_external_fills` (external_broker.py:1454-1543), lifecycle events (external_broker.py:1518-1542), legacy paper_external_daily_reservations mirror (_sync_legacy_attempt_zero_reservation, paper_books_repositories.py:1886-1904).
- **External side effects.** - Layer A: `client.get_account()` and `client.list_positions()` runtime-client calls (services/reconcile_paper.py:36-53); reconciliation itself never places orders. - Layer B: read-only broker calls only — `runtime.account_check` (external_broker.py:2049), `runtime.get_order_by_client_order_id` (external_broker.py:2066), `runtime.list_order_fills` (external_broker.py:1375), `runtime.get_external_positions` (external_broker.py:2198), `runtime.get_external_account_snapshot` (external_broker.py:2199), `_detect_duplicate_broker_order` (external_broker.py:2192). Reconciliation never calls `submit_limit_order`/`preview_limit_order` (docstring at external_broker.py:1648-1650). Recovery entry is explicitly read-only (external_broker.py:1648).
- **Failure behavior.** - Layer A: runtime start failure returns `{"error": "paper runtime unavailable: ..."}` and never records anything (cli.py:527-530). No fail-open path — mismatches are recorded as CASH_MISMATCH/POSITION_MISMATCH/MISSING_* without repair (execution/account_reconciliation.py:22-66). - Layer B: all defects fail-closed:   * `_reconcile_locked` (external_broker.py:1959-1986) wraps `_run_reconciliation` with a safety net that always persists a `RECONCILIATION_INTERNAL_ERROR` record on unexpected exceptions; on persist failure it raises `RECONCILIATION_PERSIST_FAILED` (external_broker.py:1983-1986). Only `OrderLeaseLostError` propagates without persistence (external_broker.py:1975-1976).   * ORDER_MISSING_LOCALLY, ORDER_MISSING_AT_BROKER, AMBIGUOUS_SUBMISSION, BROKER_ORDER_DUPLICATE, BOOK_NAMESPACE_MISMATCH, ACCOUNT_FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH, SYMBOL_MISMATCH, SIDE_MISMATCH, QUANTITY_MISMATCH, FILL_QUANTITY_MISMATCH, PRICE_MISMATCH, CASH_MISMATCH, POSITION_MISMATCH, UNKNOWN, FILL_APPLICATION_FAILED, MALFORMED_BROKER_ORDER, MALFORMED_BROKER_FILL, BROKER_STATE_UNKNOWN, RECONCILIATION_INTERNAL_ERROR, RESERVATION_MISMATCH, SHARE_RESERVATION_MISMATCH, FROZEN_INTENT_MISMATCH, EXTERNAL_NOTIONAL_LIMIT, RECONCILIATION_BASELINE_MISSING are all marked critical (external_broker.py:53-62). A critical latest reconciliation blocks future submits/retries via `_safety_checks` (external_broker.py:585-607) and surfaces the derived queue status as `BLOCKED_BY_RECONCILIATION` (external_broker.py:1876-1897).   * Missing baseline suppresses delta compare but records `RECONCILIATION_BASELINE_MISSING` (external_broker.py:2058-2063, 2225).   * A cancel post-fill sweep failure never releases the reservation (external_broker.py:1842-1860).   * Ambiguous submission retains the RESERVED/RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED hold so a concurrent submission for a different order cannot slip under the $150 daily cap (docstring external_broker.py:347-361; enforcement in `sum_external_daily_reserved_notional` at paper_books_repositories.py:1685-1832 counting RESERVED + SUBMITTED + RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED).
- **Retry behavior.** - Layer A: no built-in retry — each CLI invocation appends a fresh row. - Layer B: reconciliation itself is safe to call repeatedly (recovery docstring at external_broker.py:1651-1655 and idempotent lookup/reconciliation IDs). Retry of the ORDER, not reconciliation, is `retry_external_paper_order` (external_broker.py:1546-1632) and requires:   * current chain state == STATE_UNKNOWN (external_broker.py:1558-1560).   * A matching unconsumed authoritative NOT_FOUND lookup bound by ambiguous_event_id/attempt_number/payload_hash/account_fingerprint/client_order_id/book_id (external_broker.py:1561-1575).   * `_safety_checks` re-run with `allow_confirmed_not_found_retry` scoped to this client_order_id (external_broker.py:1576-1578, 585-607).   * `retries < config.external_broker.maximum_retry_attempts` (external_broker.py:1579-1583).   * fingerprint unchanged (external_broker.py:1584-1587).   * baseline present (external_broker.py:1588).   * a fresh APPROVED preview (external_broker.py:1589-1598).   * an active prior attempt reservation for the current attempt_number (external_broker.py:1600-1609).   * `_reserve_daily_notional` supersedes the prior reservation atomically with the new insert (external_broker.py:1610-1615, 294-327).   * Lookup row is atomically consumed once the new SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event is written (external_broker.py:1623-1626).
- **Recovery behavior.** - Crash between `_submit_once`'s reservation+SUBMISSION_REQUESTED commit and any broker response leaves the chain stranded at SUBMISSION_REQUESTED forever without the bridge; `recover_stranded_submission` (external_broker.py:1635-1696) is the operator entry point: acquires order lease, re-reads state under the lease, and delegates to `_reconcile_locked` which uses `_bridge_stranded_submission_requested` (external_broker.py:2024-2038, 2079-2081, 2273) to transition to STATE_UNKNOWN and mark the reservation RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED — retaining exposure until the operator retries or cancels. - The reservation itself is never released on trust: `_release_terminal_reservation` (external_broker.py:1294-1316) excludes FILLED so the release only happens inside `apply_external_fills` when the full approved quantity is durably applied (external_broker.py:1506-1517). - Recovery is idempotent — a second call sees the chain already moved off SUBMISSION_REQUESTED and returns `already_recovered` (external_broker.py:1690-1692). - Milestone 24 baseline is created only via `activate_external_reconciliation_baseline` preflight (docstring at external_broker.py:2054-2057) — reconciliation never invents a baseline; without one, delta compare is skipped and critical `RECONCILIATION_BASELINE_MISSING` is recorded (external_broker.py:2058-2063). - Cross-day retry: `_reserve_daily_notional` at external_broker.py:230-344 keeps prior-day reserved budget accounted for on either the old date (until superseded) or the new date, via atomic supersede-plus-new-insert within one BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction (external_broker.py:294-343).
- **Terminal states.** - Event chain terminal states: FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED (external_broker.py:52, 85). Once terminal, no further submit/preview/retry is allowed (external_broker.py:519-524). - Attempt reservation terminal states: RELEASED (definite non-executing outcome), SUBMITTED (settled ok), SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY (superseded by a cross-day retry). RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED is non-terminal (still counts against daily cap until resolved). Enforced by `_ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_TRANSITION_SOURCES` used by paper_books_repositories.py:1858-1863 and by `_reconcile_attempt_reservation` refusing to downgrade a SUBMITTED reservation (external_broker.py:405-408). - Layer A: no terminal state — each run persists an append-only row.
- **Fail open or closed.** fail-closed
- **File refs.** `src/trading_research/cli.py:340-412`, `src/trading_research/cli.py:516-550`, `src/trading_research/cli.py:3087-3092`, `src/trading_research/services/reconcile_paper.py:1-62`, `src/trading_research/execution/account_reconciliation.py:1-88`, `src/trading_research/execution/reconciliation.py:1-57`, `src/trading_research/storage/execution_repositories.py:360-424`, `src/trading_research/storage/execution_schema.py:90-143`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:40-90`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:142-180`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:183-344`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:347-428`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:431-500`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:585-623`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:778-916`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1170-1316`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1319-1543`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1546-1696`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1925-1987`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1989-2303`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/reconciliation.py:1-146`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1573-1704`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1810-1904`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:683-758`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:918-1103`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:1368-1443`
- **Suspected defects.**
  - Layer A `reconcile_paper_account_and_positions` (src/trading_research/services/reconcile_paper.py:30-62) has no concurrency protection: two overlapping runs of `legacy-paper-reconcile` would both call `client.get_account()`/`client.list_positions()` and `save_*_reconciliation` interleaved. Each `save_*_reconciliation` (execution_repositories.py:367-406) runs a bare `execute + commit` outside a BEGIN IMMEDIATE, so position rows commit one-at-a-time in a Python loop (reconcile_paper.py:59-60). Not evidently unsafe (rows are append-only) but the report result surfaced to the operator can silently reflect a mix of two snapshots. NeedsVerification for any operational impact.
  - `reconcile_paper_cli` (src/trading_research/cli.py:526-537) opens a DB `session(...)` and only *then* starts the runtime client; a `client.start()` failure returns `{'error': ...}` without persisting anything (cli.py:527-530). If a partial `_build_runtime_client()` allocates OS resources before start, they are only released by `client.shutdown()` inside the `finally` gated by successful start — verify no resource leak on start failure. NeedsVerification.
  - Layer B `_run_reconciliation`'s tail bridge (external_broker.py:2263-2273) runs after all mismatches are collected, but the tail bridge always runs regardless of whether an earlier `_bridge_stranded_submission_requested(...)` already ran at external_broker.py:2079-2081. The re-check inside the helper (external_broker.py:2027-2028) makes the second call a no-op *only* if the chain has already advanced off SUBMISSION_REQUESTED. In practice the first call advances the chain, but if a mid-flight step raised before appending its own event (e.g. a broker lookup returned a valid order and then `_append_event` at 2127 failed), stranded_at_submission_requested would remain True and the tail bridge would append an UNKNOWN event even after we saw a valid broker response — worth verifying that the two invocations cannot double-append. NeedsVerification.
  - `_run_reconciliation` broadens `except Exception` around the positions/account cross-check (external_broker.py:2255-2260) but classifies `ExternalPaperError` -> 'UNKNOWN' while `(InvalidOperation, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError)` -> 'RECONCILIATION_INTERNAL_ERROR' and everything else -> 'UNKNOWN'. A `MALFORMED_RUNTIME_RESPONSE` (raised at 2201/2206/2211/2216) becomes generic 'UNKNOWN' rather than a specific malformed-response status even though `CRITICAL_RECONCILIATION_STATUSES` treats both as critical. This masks the specific defect in `paper_external_reconciliations.statuses_json` and could obscure repeat root-cause analysis. NeedsVerification.
  - Legacy attempt-0 mirror in `_sync_legacy_attempt_zero_reservation` (storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1886-1904) updates the legacy row filtered by (state IN RESERVED/SUBMITTED/RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED) — but the mirror only fires when the mirrored attempt's `attempt_number == 0`. On a cross-day supersede where the *new* attempt is attempt-N (N>0) and the prior superseded row is attempt-0, `transition_attempt_reservation_state` for the prior row calls `_sync_legacy_attempt_zero_reservation` and correctly demotes the legacy row; verify the reverse case (a cross-day retry from a legacy row where the *legacy* row's attempt-0 counterpart is not the reservation being transitioned) doesn't leave the legacy row still counting in `sum_attempt_reservations_by_date` (paper_books_repositories.py:1810-1832) despite the attempt-N row being SUBMITTED. NeedsVerification.
  - Baseline (paper_external_reconciliation_baseline) uses INSERT OR IGNORE keyed by book_id (paper_books_repositories.py:1649-1663). If an operator switches paper accounts (new fingerprint) for the same book, the frozen baseline still carries the *original* fingerprint. Reconciliation then reports ACCOUNT_FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH (external_broker.py:2061-2063) — fail-closed, but the recovery path requires the baseline to be explicitly re-activated somewhere; verify that `activate_external_reconciliation_baseline` supports rotating fingerprint or whether the book must be re-created. NeedsVerification.

#### Cross-day retry & UTC-day reservation transitions
========================================================

WHAT THE WORKFLOW DOES
- Enforces a per-UTC-day, per-Alpaca-paper-account cap on external-paper notional using per-attempt reservations that live in `paper_external_attempt_reservations` (schema: `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:937-948`, immutability triggers `1434-1443`, unique-active index `1101-1105`).
- Reservation identity is deterministic: `derive_external_attempt_reservation_id(client_order_id, attempt_number, account_fingerprint, reservation_date)` (`paper_books_schema.py:1571-1585`).
- Legal state graph in `paper_books_repositories.py:28-33`:
    RESERVED -> SUBMITTED/RELEASED/RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED/SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY
    RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED -> SUBMITTED/RELEASED/SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY (never back to RESERVED)
  Terminal-for-budget: RELEASED, SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY. `sum_attempt_reservations_by_date` excludes those (repo `1799-1804`).

DAY 1 - INITIAL AMBIGUOUS SUBMISSION (attempt_number=0)
- `submit_external_paper_order` (`external_broker.py:1319-1366`):
   1. `_intent(...)` validates intent, config, risk, side, TIF, quantity, staleness, position (`502-582`).
   2. `_safety_checks` (`585-644`) blocks on shadow pause / CRITICAL alert / unresolved critical reconciliation.
   3. Acquires `_order_lease` (`876-915`) keyed by `(book_id, client_order_id)`, TTL from `external_broker.order_lease_ttl_seconds` (default 45s); every subsequent DB write is inside `lease.fenced_write()` which does `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` + fresh-clock generation verify before yielding (`846-862`).
   4. Fingerprint history verified (`_verify_fingerprint_history` `759-774`), baseline required (`_require_reconciliation_baseline`), preview validated.
   5. `_reserve_daily_notional` (`230-344`) computes `reservation_date = now.astimezone(UTC).date().isoformat()`, opens its OWN `transaction(conn)` (`begin_immediate`), does a sum-against-cap read, inserts the new RESERVED row, and commits. Uniqueness enforced by `idx_paper_external_attempt_one_active` (schema `1101-1105`).
   6. `_submit_once` (`1188-1286`) then, inside a SEPARATE `lease.fenced_write()`: writes cash/share reservation + SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event and commits.
   7. Calls broker via `runtime.submit_limit_order`. If that call raises, `_record_unknown` (`1171-1185`) appends STATE_UNKNOWN and settles the daily reservation to RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED via `_settle_daily_reservation` (`347-374`).

RECONCILIATION LOOKUP (may run on day 2)
- `reconcile_external_paper_order` -> `_reconcile_locked` -> `_run_reconciliation` (`1959-2199`), inside a fresh order-scope lease.
- Calls read-only `runtime.get_order_by_client_order_id`. Records evidence in `paper_external_order_lookups` including `ambiguous_event_id`, `attempt_number`, `payload_hash`, `account_fingerprint`, `authoritative` (only true when order is None AND no lookup errors) (`2083-2095`).
- If NOT_FOUND: `_reconcile_attempt_reservation(target=RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED)` (`2096-2100`, helper `377-420`). Because the row is already RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED, the transition is idempotent (`paper_books_repositories.py:1876-1880`).
- If FOUND: reservation target is derived from broker state (`_reservation_target_for_broker_state` `423-428`): REJECTED -> RELEASED, UNKNOWN -> RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED, else SUBMITTED. Applied inside `lease.fenced_write` alongside the state-transition event (`2122-2137`).
- Bridging: if the chain was stranded at SUBMISSION_REQUESTED after a crash, `_bridge_stranded_submission_requested` (`2024-2038`) appends a SYSTEM_RECOVERY UNKNOWN event BEFORE saving the lookup, so `retry_external_paper_order`'s later match on `ambiguous_event_id`/`attempt_number` sees the correct current event.

DAY 2 - REFRESH THE STALE PREVIEW (optional)
- `refresh_retry_preview` (`external_broker.py:1699-1770`) is a read-only DB operation: requires current chain UNKNOWN, requires fresh authoritative NOT_FOUND lookup (unconsumed), acquires order-scope lease, then inserts a new approved preview row with `expires_at = now + require_recent_preview_seconds`. Never touches the runtime.

DAY 2 - CROSS-DAY RETRY (attempt_number += 1)
- `retry_external_paper_order` (`1546-1632`):
   1. Same intent/config/safety validation as submit.
   2. Order-scope lease acquired (operation="RETRY").
   3. Current event must be STATE_UNKNOWN (`RETRY_NOT_ALLOWED` otherwise) (`1558-1560`).
   4. Lookup must be `authoritative=1`, `result="NOT_FOUND"`, unconsumed, and bound to current ambiguous event (matches `ambiguous_event_id`, `attempt_number`, `payload_hash`, `account_fingerprint`, `client_order_id`, `book_id`) (`1561-1575`).
   5. `_safety_checks` with the confirmed-NOT_FOUND allowance for the same client_order_id (`1576-1578`).
   6. Retry cap check: `retries = max event.attempt_number`; if >= `external_broker.maximum_retry_attempts` -> RETRY_LIMIT_REACHED (`1579-1583`).
   7. `_account_check` + fingerprint identity match against current event + baseline required (`1584-1588`).
   8. Preview lookup: any APPROVED preview row for this intent, then `_validated_preview` (still enforces `expires_at`, payload/fingerprint/config-hash match).
   9. `new_attempt_number = retries + 1`.
   10. `prior_reservation = load_active_attempt_reservation(client_order_id, current["attempt_number"], fingerprint, book_id)` (`1600-1609`) - must exist (else ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_MISSING); returns dup-detection error (ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_CONFLICT) if the unique-active index is bypassed and 2 lineage rows exist (`paper_books_repositories.py:1759-1775`).
   11. `_reserve_daily_notional(supersede_reservation_id=prior_reservation.reservation_id, supersede_attempt_number=current.attempt_number, attempt_number=new_attempt_number)`:
        - Opens its own `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction (`external_broker.py:265`).
        - Uses new reservation_id derived with today's UTC reservation_date + new attempt_number.
        - If that new reservation_id already exists (crash-replay), returns it after immutable-field validation (`266-273`).
        - Else, verifies the supersede-target reservation still holds `client_order_id`/`attempt_number`/fingerprint/`book_id`/notional and is in `{RESERVED, RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED}` (`294-322`).
        - Transitions prior to `SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY` with `commit=False` (`323-327`).
        - `sum_attempt_reservations_by_date` for TODAY only (superseded prior does not count) + new notional must fit `external_broker.maximum_daily_notional_usd`, else EXTERNAL_DAILY_NOTIONAL_LIMIT (`328-335`). Legacy-migration double-count guard in repo `1812-1831`.
        - Inserts new RESERVED row; single commit atomically supersedes prior + creates new (`336-344`).
   12. `_submit_once` runs (separate fenced_write) - broker submit is called only once; on ambiguous outcome the new reservation goes to RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED and STATE_UNKNOWN is appended; on success it goes to SUBMITTED and the broker-state event is appended.
   13. After success, `repo.consume_external_lookup` marks the NOT_FOUND evidence used (`1623-1626`), preventing a second retry from reusing it.
   14. If broker outcome != STATE_UNKNOWN, `_reconcile_locked` runs synchronously to sync state/fills.

TERMINAL RESERVATION STATES
- RELEASED (definite non-executing terminal: REJECTED) or after `_release_terminal_reservation` cash/share-side sweep (`1289-1316`).
- SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY (prior attempt after a valid retry).
- SUBMITTED (broker acknowledged - keeps the hold until fills clear via `apply_external_fills` `1369-1543`).
- RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED is NOT terminal - retains cap load until authoritative broker outcome is applied.
Immutability triggers (`paper_books_schema.py:1434-1443`) enforce only state/updated_at may change; DELETE is forbidden.

- **Entry point.** CLI: `external-paper-retry` -> `trading_research.cli.external_paper_retry_cli` -> `retry_external_paper_order` (`src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1546`). Companion entry points: `external-paper-reconcile` -> `reconcile_external_paper_order` (`external_broker.py:~1920`); `external-paper-refresh-retry-preview` -> `refresh_retry_preview` (`external_broker.py:1699`). Day-1 origin of a cross-day flow: `external-paper-submit` -> `submit_external_paper_order` (`external_broker.py:1319`).
