Crash-recovery entry: `recover_stranded_submission` (`external_broker.py:1635`) - not surfaced in the CLI.
- **Authoritative validation.** Two-layer authoritative validation: 1. Broker-evidence gate for retry (`external_broker.py:1558-1575`): current state STATE_UNKNOWN + latest `paper_external_order_lookups` row must be `authoritative=1`, `result=NOT_FOUND`, `consumed_by_retry_event_id IS NULL`, and match the current ambiguous event's `external_order_event_id`/`attempt_number`/`payload_hash`/`account_fingerprint`/`client_order_id`/`book_id`. `save_external_lookup` sets `authoritative` only when `order is None AND "UNKNOWN" not in statuses` (`external_broker.py:2090`), so a runtime timeout is NOT authoritative. 2. Reservation identity + payload gate (`_reserve_daily_notional._validate_attempt_reservation_payload` `193-215`, and `310-322` for the supersede target): every immutable field must match exactly, including `reservation_date` when applicable and notional recomputed as `quantity*limit_price`. Additional per-call authoritative checks: `_intent` (`502-582`) validates side/qty/tif/staleness/oversell; `_validate_frozen_notional` (`142-175`) recomputes notional and matches it to the risk decision and the strictest configured cap; `_verify_fingerprint_history` + `_account_check` + `current["account_fingerprint"]` equality (`1584-1587`); `_require_reconciliation_baseline` (`1588`); `_validated_preview` reverifies preview TTL and payload/fingerprint/config-hash.
- **Idempotency key.** `reservation_id = derive_external_attempt_reservation_id(client_order_id, attempt_number, account_fingerprint, reservation_date)` (`paper_books_schema.py:1571-1585`) - SHA-256 over the canonical JSON of those four fields. It is both the primary key and the deterministic idempotency key: a replay of `_reserve_daily_notional` with the same `(client_order_id, attempt_number, fingerprint, UTC-date)` recomputes the same `reservation_id`, sees `existing is not None`, revalidates immutable fields, and returns without re-inserting (`external_broker.py:266-273`). Secondary lineage-uniqueness key: `idx_paper_external_attempt_one_active` on `(client_order_id, attempt_number, account_fingerprint, book_id)` filtered `WHERE state IN (RESERVED, RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED, SUBMITTED)` (`paper_books_schema.py:1101-1105`). External-event chain idempotency: `uq_paper_external_events_scope_sequence(book_id, client_order_id, scope_sequence)` (`schema:1069-1070`). Authoritative-evidence one-shot consumption: `paper_external_order_lookups.consumed_by_retry_event_id` transitions NULL -> event_id exactly once via a versioned trigger (`_TRIGGER_VERSIONS` v2 in `schema:1526-1534`).
- **Transaction boundary.** Multiple, non-nested transaction boundaries within a single retry call: - T1 (order-scope lease acquisition): a single conditional UPDATE via `acquire_external_order_lease` (`external_broker.py:900-903`), autocommitted by SQLite (isolation_level=None per `storage/transactions.py:7-14`). - T2 (reservation transaction): `_reserve_daily_notional` opens its OWN `transaction(conn)` / `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` (`external_broker.py:265`) and commits after inserting the new reservation and transitioning the prior one to SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY. Explicitly documented as never nested inside a `lease.fenced_write` (`254-260`). - T3 (SUBMISSION_REQUESTED checkpoint): first `lease.fenced_write` inside `_submit_once` (`1210-1226`) covers cash/share hold + SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event. - T4 (broker success write): second `lease.fenced_write` (`1241-1253`) writes the broker-state event, updates order status, and settles the daily reservation to SUBMITTED or RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED. - T5 (lookup consumption): third `lease.fenced_write` (`1623-1626`) marks the authoritative NOT_FOUND lookup as consumed. - T6 (reconciliation): `_run_reconciliation` runs several sequential `lease.fenced_write` transactions (`2083-2137`), each atomic; state event + reservation transition are in one commit. `fenced_write` always does `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, then a fresh-clock lease-generation check, then yields, then commits (or rolls back on any BaseException) (`external_broker.py:846-862`). `TransactionAlreadyActiveError` (`storage/transactions.py:26-53`) prohibits nesting - inner participants pass `commit=False`.
- **Lease or concurrency.** Two layers: 1. Order-scope lease `paper_external_order_leases` keyed by `f"{book_id}:{client_order_id}"` (`external_broker.py:876-915`). TTL from `external_broker.order_lease_ttl_seconds` (default 45s). Generation counter incremented on every acquisition/reclaim (`_TRIGGER_VERSIONS`/schema column `paper_books_schema.py:1500-1502`). `heartbeat_or_raise`/`verify_or_raise` fail closed on generation mismatch (`OrderLeaseLostError`, `786-844`). `fenced_write` runs the verify inside SQLite's write lock, so a stale owner cannot commit after takeover. 2. SQLite `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` write lock inside `_reserve_daily_notional`'s own transaction serializes concurrent daily-notional sums against the same account/date even across books/processes (`external_broker.py:254-260`). 3. Uniqueness/serialization at the schema level: `idx_paper_external_attempt_one_active` prevents two live reservations for the same `(client_order_id, attempt_number, fingerprint, book_id)`; `uq_paper_external_events_scope_sequence` prevents forked event chains (`schema:1069-1070, 1101-1105`); immutability triggers block field-level tampering (`schema:1434-1443`). Additional coarse serialization: SQLite database write lock is single-writer; `TransactionAlreadyActiveError` prevents re-entrant `begin_immediate` mistakes (`transactions.py:38-53`).
- **State transitions.** Reservation-state graph (from `paper_books_repositories.py:28-33`): - RESERVED -> SUBMITTED  (broker acknowledged non-terminal state; from `_settle_daily_reservation`/`_reconcile_attempt_reservation`) - RESERVED -> RELEASED  (broker REJECTED; from `_settle_daily_reservation` or `_reconcile_attempt_reservation`) - RESERVED -> RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED  (runtime.submit raised; from `_record_unknown`/`_settle_daily_reservation` `347-374`) - RESERVED -> SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY  (cross-day/same-day retry with confirmed NOT_FOUND; from `_reserve_daily_notional` `323-327`) - RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED -> SUBMITTED  (later authoritative FOUND with executable state) - RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED -> RELEASED   (later authoritative FOUND with REJECTED) - RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED -> SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY  (retry with authoritative NOT_FOUND) - RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED !-> RESERVED  (never downgraded) - SUBMITTED, RELEASED, SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY are terminal (no outgoing transitions).  Idempotency of transitions: `transition_attempt_reservation_state` (repo `1853-1883`) does a conditional UPDATE and, if 0 rows changed, re-reads and returns the row when `state == new_state` (no-op replay).  Cross-day transition mechanics (`external_broker.py:261-344`): reservation_date is always the current UTC date at the moment `_reserve_daily_notional` runs. Attempt N's reservation stays tied to its day-1 date; retry allocates a new reservation_id at day-2's date and supersedes attempt N atomically. Same-day retry (same UTC date) also increments attempt_number to a new reservation_id, so day-1 total charges only the winning attempt's notional (superseded ones drop out of `sum_attempt_reservations_by_date`).  `_reserve_daily_notional` special "carry-forward" path (`285-292`): if there is already an ACTIVE reservation for the same `(client_order_id, attempt_number, fingerprint, book_id)` at a DIFFERENT reservation_date and its state is RESERVED, the function returns that prior-day row without opening a new one; if state is anything else it fails ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_CONFLICT ("only an unstarted reserved attempt may carry forward across dates").
- **Persistent records.** - `paper_external_attempt_reservations` (schema `paper_books_schema.py:937-948`) - append-plus-state-only row per attempt. - `paper_external_daily_reservations` (schema `919-928`) - deprecated legacy per-order table; kept for rolling-upgrade correctness. `_backfill_active_legacy_external_reservations` (`schema:1588-1632`) copies active legacy rows to attempt_number=0 in the new table; `_sync_legacy_attempt_zero_reservation` (`repo:1886-1904`) mirrors state changes back into the legacy row so `sum_attempt_reservations_by_date` cannot double-count (`repo:1812-1831`). - `paper_external_order_events` - append-only chain per (book, client_order_id), `scope_sequence` enforced unique (`schema:1069-1070`). Contains PREVIEWED, SUBMISSION_REQUESTED, SUBMITTED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED, UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_RECONCILIATION. - `paper_external_order_lookups` - authoritative broker lookup evidence with `authoritative`, `attempt_number`, `ambiguous_event_id`, `payload_hash`, `consumed_by_retry_event_id`. - `paper_external_order_previews` - preview rows with `expires_at`. - `paper_external_order_leases` - lease rows with `generation`, `expires_at`, `owner_id`, `operation`. - `paper_external_reconciliations` - critical/informational evidence written even on unexpected exceptions (`_persist_reconciliation`, unfailable path via `_reconcile_locked` `1959-1986`). - Downstream fill records: `paper_external_broker_fills`, `paper_book_fills`; cash/share reservation events in `paper_book_cash_ledger`, `paper_external_position_reservation_events`.
- **External side effects.** Only one kind of outbound broker interaction from this workflow, mediated by the `ExternalPaperRuntime` protocol (`external_broker.py:89-98`, implemented in the isolated `paper_runtime` subprocess): - `runtime.account_check(book_id)` (read-only) inside every submit/retry/reconcile. - `runtime.submit_limit_order(payload)` - the ONE write, guarded by the reservation + SUBMISSION_REQUESTED checkpoint being durably committed FIRST (`_submit_once` `1188-1226`). A single attempt per submit/retry call. - `runtime.get_order_by_client_order_id(...)` - read-only lookup used by reconciliation and by the duplicate-submit branch (`external_broker.py:1345`, `2066`). - `runtime.list_order_fills(...)` - read-only fill sweep in `apply_external_fills` (`1375`). No emails, no webhooks, no scheduled jobs from this path. `refresh_retry_preview` and `recover_stranded_submission` deliberately do not call `submit_limit_order`/`preview_limit_order` (`1699-1714`, `1648-1667`).
- **Failure behavior.** Every call site raises `ExternalPaperError(code, message)` with a specific machine-readable code and NEVER a bare exception string. Codes seen in this workflow: RETRY_NOT_ALLOWED, NOT_FOUND_NOT_CONFIRMED, RETRY_LIMIT_REACHED, ACCOUNT_FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH, PREVIEW_NOT_FOUND, ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_MISSING, ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_CONFLICT, ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_PAYLOAD_MISMATCH, EXTERNAL_DAILY_NOTIONAL_LIMIT, RESERVATION_STATE_CONFLICT, RUNTIME_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN, ORDER_LEASE_HELD, ORDER_LEASE_LOST, AMBIGUOUS_SUBMISSION, REFRESH_NOT_ALLOWED, RECOVERY_NOT_APPLICABLE, PAPER_BOOKS_DISABLED, EXTERNAL_BROKER_DISABLED, SUBMISSION_DISABLED, INTENT_NOT_ELIGIBLE_FOR_EXTERNAL, INTENT_NOT_APPROVED, SAFETY_PAUSE_ACTIVE, CRITICAL_ALERT_ACTIVE, RECONCILIATION_BASELINE_MISSING, RECONCILIATION_PERSIST_FAILED. A runtime.submit exception is converted into a STATE_UNKNOWN event + reservation state RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED (`_record_unknown` `1171-1185`) - it is not surfaced as an error. Unexpected exceptions inside reconciliation are trapped and persisted as RECONCILIATION_INTERNAL_ERROR through `_reconcile_locked` (`1975-1986`). Fill-sweep failures after successful submission persist MALFORMED_BROKER_FILL / FILL_APPLICATION_FAILED reconciliation evidence and re-raise (`_submit_once` `1263-1283`).
- **Retry behavior.** - One broker `submit_limit_order` per operator retry call (`_submit_once` `1188-1286`). No automatic in-process retries against the broker. - Retry cap: `external_broker.maximum_retry_attempts`; `retries = max(event.attempt_number)` gate at `external_broker.py:1579-1583`. Not reset per day - it counts total attempts on this `client_order_id`. - Each retry must satisfy: authoritative NOT_FOUND (unconsumed) bound to the current UNKNOWN event; matching approved preview inside its TTL; unchanged intent, fingerprint, config_hash, payload_hash; valid baseline; safety pause not active. - Idempotent replay of the same operator retry call: rerunning after commit is safe because (a) the authoritative lookup was marked `consumed_by_retry_event_id` and cannot satisfy the gate again (`retry_external_paper_order:1623-1626`, trigger v2 in `paper_books_schema.py:1526-1534`); (b) the reservation_id is deterministic so a replay hits the "existing is not None" fast path (`external_broker.py:266-273`). - No exponential backoff, no scheduled retry - retry is manual/operator-driven via CLI.
- **Recovery behavior.** - SUBMISSION_REQUESTED stranded chains (crash between the `_submit_once` fenced_write commit and any broker response commit): `recover_stranded_submission` (`external_broker.py:1635-1696`) re-acquires the order lease and delegates to `_reconcile_locked`. On authoritative FOUND: normalizes state, applies fills, releases the reservation appropriately. On NOT_FOUND / timeout / malformed response: `_bridge_stranded_submission_requested` (`2024-2038`) writes a SYSTEM_RECOVERY UNKNOWN event first (so the lookup evidence references the freshly appended event id), the lookup evidence is saved, and reservation transitions to RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED. That unblocks the ordinary retry path. - Preview TTL expired for a confirmed-safe-to-retry order: `refresh_retry_preview` writes a fresh approved preview row without touching the runtime (`1699-1770`). - Legacy reservation rows (pre-attempt schema): `_backfill_active_legacy_external_reservations` + `_enforce_one_active_external_attempt_reservation` at schema application time (`paper_books_schema.py:1588-1650, 1676-1697`); `_sync_legacy_attempt_zero_reservation` keeps the deprecated legacy row in sync so the daily sum cannot double-count during a rolling upgrade (`repo:1886-1904`). - Broken reservation lineage (multiple active rows for the same attempt tuple): `load_active_attempt_reservation` fails closed with `AttemptReservationIntegrityError`/`ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_CONFLICT` (`repo:1759-1775`), which surfaces as `ExternalPaperError` and blocks the retry. - Reconciliation catchall: `_reconcile_locked` guarantees a `paper_external_reconciliations` row is persisted even for unexpected exceptions (`1975-1986`).
- **Terminal states.** Reservation terminal states: RELEASED, SUBMITTED, SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY (no outgoing transitions per `_ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_TRANSITION_SOURCES` and the CHECK constraint at `paper_books_schema.py:945`). RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED is NON-terminal and continues to charge the daily cap (`repo:1799-1804`). Order-event terminal states: FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED (`external_broker.py:52`, `_TRANSITIONS` `64-86` empty sets); `_intent` refuses to preview/submit/retry once the shared `paper_book_orders.status` is terminal (`519-524`). For the whole cross-day workflow, "terminal happy path" = new attempt's reservation reaches SUBMITTED and the event chain reaches SUBMITTED / PARTIALLY_FILLED / FILLED, or a definite non-executing broker terminal (REJECTED etc.) with reservation RELEASED. "Terminal unhappy path" = RETRY_LIMIT_REACHED after which no further retries are permitted; the last reservation stays in whatever state it settled to (typically RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED) and continues to consume the day's cap until reconciliation or an operator escalation.
- **Fail open or closed.** fail-closed
- **File refs.** `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:100-113`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:180-345`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:347-428`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:585-644`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:786-915`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1171-1286`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1319-1366`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1546-1632`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1635-1696`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1699-1770`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1920-2199`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:730-948`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:1069-1105`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:1421-1443`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:1571-1697`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:19-33`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1733-1904`, `src/trading_research/storage/transactions.py:1-75`, `src/trading_research/cli.py:377-429`, `tests/unit/test_external_paper_broker.py:2126-2340`
- **Suspected defects.**
  - Suspected atomicity gap between the daily-notional reservation commit and the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event commit on retry. `_reserve_daily_notional` opens and commits its OWN `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction (`external_broker.py:254-260, 265`), so on retry the prior attempt-N reservation is atomically transitioned to SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY and the new attempt-(N+1) RESERVED row is inserted in T2, THEN a separate `_submit_once` fenced_write commits SUBMISSION_REQUESTED in T3 (`1610-1622`, first fenced_write at `1210-1226`). A process crash between T2 and T3 leaves the event chain still at STATE_UNKNOWN at attempt N while attempt N's reservation is already SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY and attempt (N+1)'s reservation exists in RESERVED. On restart `recover_stranded_submission` refuses (chain is not at SUBMISSION_REQUESTED, `1679-1684`), and a fresh `retry_external_paper_order` fails at `load_active_attempt_reservation(current["attempt_number"]=N, ...)` because the prior row is now SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY -> raises ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_MISSING (`1600-1609`). The stranded new reservation still counts against the day's cap. NeedsVerification via an integration test that crashes between the two commits; the in-code docstring's atomicity claim (`1199-1208`) refers only to the cash/share hold + event pair, not the daily-notional reservation.
  - Suspected UTC-date carry-forward gap in `_reserve_daily_notional` (`external_broker.py:280-293`). When an active RESERVED reservation exists for the same `(client_order_id, attempt_number, fingerprint, book_id)` but a DIFFERENT `reservation_date`, the function returns the prior-day row unchanged and never charges today's cap. Combined with the day-1 flow where an initial submit can create a RESERVED row but the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED commit that follows lives in a separate transaction (see previous finding), a partial write could leave attempt-0 RESERVED against yesterday's date. A same-attempt replay today would carry forward that reservation and not consume today's cap even though today's broker submission is the one that actually runs. NeedsVerification against tests to confirm whether attempt-0 can legitimately reach today without a prior SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event.
  - Suspected staleness of the retry-attempt counter after a crash-bridged UNKNOWN event. In `retry_external_paper_order` (`1579-1581`) `retries = max(event["attempt_number"] for event in list_external_order_events(...))`. If a crash before T3 leaves attempt (N+1) with no event but with an existing RESERVED reservation, a fresh retry recomputes `new_attempt_number = N+1` and hits the deterministic reservation_id in the existing-row fast path (`266-273`) - correct - but the prior-active check at `1600-1609` still targets attempt N whose reservation is now SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY, and the gate blocks (see first finding). Operators have no un-jam path short of manually mutating the reservation row, which the immutability triggers explicitly forbid (`paper_books_schema.py:1441-1443`). NeedsVerification.
  - The retry gate refuses when the ambiguous lookup was recorded against a stale attempt event but the reconciliation bridging path only backfills the reference `if order is None` (`external_broker.py:2079-2081`). If the reconciliation lookup returns FOUND but with a malformed response (`order_valid=False`), the lookup evidence is saved against the pre-recovery `current` event (`2082-2095`), yet the chain remains SUBMISSION_REQUESTED (bridging skipped) - the next retry can never match `ambiguous_event_id` (chain has no UNKNOWN event) and the operator is left with a `_reconcile_attempt_reservation` writing RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED against the reservation with no forward retry path except another reconciliation run. NeedsVerification.
  - `sum_attempt_reservations_by_date` legacy double-count guard uses a NOT EXISTS subquery keyed on immutable fields including `book_id` and `reserved_notional_usd` (`paper_books_repositories.py:1816-1828`). If a legacy row was ever inserted with a slightly different `reserved_notional_usd` string encoding (e.g. trailing zeros lost in earlier code paths), the guard could fail to match its migrated attempt-0 row and count the notional twice, causing spurious EXTERNAL_DAILY_NOTIONAL_LIMIT rejects. NeedsVerification by inspecting legacy Decimal serialization paths.

#### Local paper intent creation & approval workflow (isolated paper_books subsystem, Milestone 8/8.1/9). Sequence: (1) A scheduled research cycle previously froze one or two per-arm recommendations (baseline/enhanced) and persisted them in `recommendations` (see `recommendations/builder.py`; not entered again here). (2) `paper_books/scheduled_integration.py::integrate_scheduled_cycle_into_paper_books` is the primary entry point (also called from `paper_books/lifecycle.py::run_paper_book_lifecycle` when `integrate_cycle_ids` is supplied, and mirrored by `paper_books/cli_support.py::paper_book_run_cycle_cli` for fixture-mode CLI). (3) It enumerates each symbol row from `research_cycles`, precomputes deterministic `paper_order_intent_id`s for each arm (`derive_paper_order_intent_id(recommendation_id, book_id, EXECUTION_VERSION)` in `paper_books/models.py:98-103`), persists the experiment assignment (`paper_books/experiment_assignment.py::save_assignment`), then per (arm, book) calls `_process_arm` (scheduled_integration.py:220-411). (4) `_process_arm` runs a cascade of authoritative validations: `is_book_enabled`, `may_submit`, presence/shape of recommendation (`load_recommendation`), symbol/`as_of` alignment, active buy_candidate side, evidence-snapshot presence + symbol match + `screening_completeness == STATUS_COMPLETE_FOR_SCREENING`. (5) The book is lazily opened idempotently via `cash_ledger.open_book` (cash_ledger.py:71-88). (6) A per-`as_of` portfolio snapshot is built (`valuation.build_portfolio_snapshot`), a point-in-time-safe reference price is chosen (`valuation.select_valuation_price`), and if ATR sizing is enabled the requested quantity is capped by `compute_atr_position_plan`. (7) If authoritative daily-risk is enabled, `reconciliation.reconcile_book` + `daily_risk.calculate_and_persist_daily_risk_state` are computed and `save_economic_blackout_decision` is written. (8) `risk.evaluate_paper_risk` (risk.py:69-191) performs pure deterministic sizing, always producing a `PaperRiskDecision` (APPROVED/APPROVED_REDUCED or one of many REJECTED codes) — this is the authoritative approval step. (9) `order_intent.persist_risk_decision` (order_intent.py:24-39) writes the decision row (idempotent by hashed decision id — `sha256(book_id:cycle_id:recommendation_id:symbol)[:32]` — regardless of approval outcome, giving a queryable audit trail for rejects too), and if the reject code is DAILY_LOSS/DRAWDOWN a `safety_pause.pause_for_risk_state` is applied. (10) If decision is rejected (not in APPROVED_RISK_DECISIONS), the outcome is OUTCOME_REJECTED_BY_RISK and no intent is built. (11) If approved, `order_intent.build_order_intent` (order_intent.py:42-58) constructs a `PaperBookOrderIntent` — BUY-side LIMIT only, `limit_price = approved_notional_usd / approved_quantity`, notional derived from approved_notional_usd, deterministic `paper_order_intent_id`; frozen dataclass validates positivity/whole-share/tz-awareness/notional=qty*price (models.py:240-294). (12) Routing branch: (a) External-broker route (`cfg.external_broker.enabled` and this book is in `enabled_book_ids`) — `persist_order_intent` writes the intent as PENDING_SUBMISSION (no reservation, no claim), then `enqueue_external_submission` with deterministic queue_id `peqs_ + sha256(book_id:intent_id:scheduled)[:40]`; outcome is AWAITING_OPERATOR_EXTERNAL_SUBMISSION (out of scope for local approval). (b) Local route with no market-simulation input available — `persist_order_intent` writes intent as PENDING_SUBMISSION and outcome is INTENT_CREATED_PENDING_FILL (no reservation, no claim). (c) Local route with market input — `execution.submit_and_simulate` (paper_books/execution.py:99-232) atomically (`transaction(conn)` = BEGIN IMMEDIATE) claims LOCAL_SIMULATED namespace, inserts the intent, and (BUY only) inserts a `reserve:{intent_id}` cash-ledger row via `cash_ledger.reserve_for_order` (which itself takes a book-scoped BEGIN IMMEDIATE if not already inside a transaction); it then simulates a fill deterministically and commits fill + position + settlement + release + status=FILLED in a second atomic transaction. (13) Per-book reconciliations run at the end of `integrate_scheduled_cycle_into_paper_books` for every touched book. All persistence is SQLite; every save is idempotent by deterministic primary key or `IF EXISTS` guard.

- **Entry point.** Primary entry point: `paper_books.scheduled_integration.integrate_scheduled_cycle_into_paper_books` (src/trading_research/paper_books/scheduled_integration.py:414-523). Called by the lifecycle service `paper_books.lifecycle.run_paper_book_lifecycle` (src/trading_research/paper_books/lifecycle.py:536-663, iteration loop at 561-569) and by the recurring scheduler `paper_books.recurring_scheduler.run_recurring_paper_scheduler` (src/trading_research/paper_books/recurring_scheduler.py:872). CLI (fixture-mode) equivalent: `paper_books.cli_support.paper_book_run_cycle_cli` (src/trading_research/paper_books/cli_support.py:140-219), wired through `cli.py:3365-3369` under the `paper-book-run-cycle` subcommand. Note: the legacy `services.execute_paper_recommendation.execute_paper_recommendation` (src/trading_research/services/execute_paper_recommendation.py:64-116) is a separate Milestone-3 code path against the deprecated `paper/ledger.py` tables; the CLI has quarantined it behind `legacy-paper-execute` (cli.py:2572-2584 requires `--i-understand-this-is-the-legacy-ledger`) and it is NOT the paper_books local-intent workflow.
- **Authoritative validation.** Multi-layered, all deterministic: - Config-gate defense in depth: `paper_books_config.enabled` and `paper_books_config.scheduled_integration.enabled` both re-checked at the entry point (scheduled_integration.py:428-433). - Per-arm eligibility cascade in `_process_arm` (scheduled_integration.py:232-263): book enabled, `may_submit_*_to_paper_book` policy, recommendation exists + is frozen (via `load_recommendation`), symbol match, `rec_ts <= as_of` (no future data), `status==STATUS_ACTIVE`, `side==SIDE_BUY_CANDIDATE`, positive `risk_plan.shares`, evidence-snapshot presence + symbol match + `screening_completeness==STATUS_COMPLETE_FOR_SCREENING`. - Authoritative sizing/risk: `paper_books.risk.evaluate_paper_risk` (risk.py:69-191) checks arm alignment, book status ACTIVE, (optional) daily risk state present/fresh/reconciled/valuation-COMPLETE, daily-loss and drawdown caps, economic blackout, price present + point-in-time-safe + not-stale, requested-quantity positive, book valuation status COMPLETE-or-STALE, max-open-positions for new positions, then computes the min of `cash_capacity`, `remaining_position_capacity`, `remaining_symbol_capacity`, `remaining_daily_capacity`, `max_order_notional_usd`, floors quantity, and rejects to zero-share as INSUFFICIENT_CASH (or the binding cap's code). - ATR gate (scheduled_integration.py:286-313): rejects with ATR_UNAVAILABLE_OR_STALE / ATR_PERCENT_OUT_OF_BOUNDS / ATR_RISK_PLAN_REJECTED. - Domain-level fail-closed: `PaperRiskDecision.__post_init__` (models.py:223-237) and `PaperBookOrderIntent.__post_init__` (models.py:261-294) both enforce required fields, positive amounts, `notional == quantity*limit_price`, whole shares, tz-aware timestamps. - Namespace exclusivity: `claim_execution_namespace` in `submit_and_simulate` prevents a locally-simulated fill on an intent already claimed for EXTERNAL_PAPER (paper_books_repositories.py:1954-1988; execution.py:118-157).
- **Idempotency key.** Multiple deterministic idempotency keys, no random or wall-clock inputs: - `paper_order_intent_id`: `pb-intent-` + `sha256(recommendation_id:book_id:execution_version)[:32]` — `derive_paper_order_intent_id` (paper_books/models.py:98-103). Same recommendation submitted to two different books produces two different intent IDs. - `risk_decision_id`: `pb-risk-` + `sha256(book_id:cycle_id:recommendation_id:symbol)[:32]` (order_intent.py:31-34). - Cash-ledger idempotency: `reserve:{paper_order_intent_id}` (cash_ledger.py:124) and `release:{intent_id}:{reason}` (cash_ledger.py:159); enforced by `paper_book_cash_ledger` UNIQUE `(book_id, idempotency_key)` via `cash_ledger_entry_exists` (paper_books_repositories.py:107-131). - Order-intent row: primary-key uniqueness on `(book_id, paper_order_intent_id)` — `save_order_intent` returns False for a duplicate (paper_books_repositories.py:190-207). - External submission queue: `queue_id = "peqs_" + sha256(f"{book_id}:{intent_id}:scheduled")[:40]` (scheduled_integration.py:371-373); lifecycle mirrors it with `":lifecycle"`/`":exit"` suffixes. - Fill idempotency: `fill_id = "pb-fill-{paper_order_intent_id}"` (execution.py:91) with `fill_exists` guards on both sides of the fill transaction (execution.py:181-198).
- **Transaction boundary.** The approval step (`persist_risk_decision`) is its OWN top-level commit (paper_books_repositories.py:164 unconditionally calls `conn.commit()` after inserting the decision) — deliberately, so a rejected decision has a durable audit row even if downstream fails. The PENDING intent-create-only path (`persist_order_intent` on lines 370 and 387 of scheduled_integration.py, and lines 458 and 475 of lifecycle.py) is likewise a single-statement autocommit via `save_order_intent`'s default `commit=True` (paper_books_repositories.py:206). The atomic transactional boundary is inside `execution.submit_and_simulate`: - New-intent path: one `transaction(conn)` context (execution.py:119-133) commits {namespace claim, intent row, BUY reservation ledger row} atomically. This closes the pre-Item-6 gap where a crash between insert and reservation could leave an un-reserved BUY intent. - Fill path: a second explicit `begin_immediate` block (execution.py:194-231) commits {fill row, position/lot update, settlement ledger entry, fee/slippage entries, reservation release, order status=FILLED} atomically or rolls back on any exception. - Cash reservation `reserve_for_order` (cash_ledger.py:111-134) uses `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` book-scoped serialization when `commit=True` so concurrent BUYs can't both observe headroom. - `storage/transactions.py::begin_immediate` refuses to enter a nested transaction — instead raises `TransactionAlreadyActiveError` — so nested calls must use `commit=False` and participate in the caller's transaction.
- **Lease or concurrency.** Multiple layers: - SQLite `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` (a whole-database write lock in SQLite) is the concurrency primitive throughout. `storage/transactions.py::begin_immediate` requires connections opened with `isolation_level=None` and fails closed with `TransactionAlreadyActiveError` rather than silently coalescing (transactions.py:26-54). - `claim_execution_namespace` (paper_books_repositories.py:1954-1988) is a read-then-insert under an outer `begin_immediate` write lock; a second, mismatched claim raises `ExecutionNamespaceConflictError` — durable per-intent LOCAL vs EXTERNAL exclusivity. - `cash_ledger.reserve_for_order` (cash_ledger.py:111-134) holds a book-scoped write lock across the read of settled/reserved and the insert of the reservation, preventing double-reservation of the same cash. - Recurring-scheduler singleton lease: `acquire_recurring_lease` at `run_recurring_paper_scheduler` (recurring_scheduler.py:924-932) — a lease `LEASE_NAME` with TTL, returns `RecurringLeaseConflict` when held by another owner; skips as `STATUS_SKIPPED_LEASE_HELD`. The lease is intended to serialize a single scheduler slot per `intended_schedule_id`. - External path uses a separate `paper_external_order_leases` fencing-generation lease (paper_books_repositories.py:2035-2049), but that is the external workflow, not local approval.
- **State transitions.** Order intent lifecycle (KNOWN_INTENT_STATUSES in models.py:73-84): - Constructed → `PENDING_SUBMISSION` (default `PaperBookOrderIntent.status` at models.py:259; set at insert in scheduled_integration.py external branch, no-market branch, and inside `submit_and_simulate` new-intent branch). - `PENDING_SUBMISSION` → `FILLED` inside `submit_and_simulate` when the deterministic limit-order rule crosses (execution.py:227; commit=False, part of the atomic fill transaction). - `PENDING_SUBMISSION` → `REJECTED` for SELL orders when position not available (execution.py:174). - `PENDING_SUBMISSION` → `CANCELLED` via `cancel_pending_intent` (execution.py:235-247), only for local-namespace intents. - `PENDING_SUBMISSION` → `EXPIRED` via `expire_pending_intent` (execution.py:250-262) or by `_process_pending_orders` in lifecycle when age exceeds `expire_after_market_days` (lifecycle.py:205-215). Risk decision states: `RISK_APPROVED`, `RISK_APPROVED_REDUCED`, and many `RISK_REJECTED_*` codes (models.py:36-63); the risk-decision row is terminal and immutable once written.
- **Persistent records.** SQLite tables written on the approval workflow: - `paper_books` — via `cash_ledger.open_book` (idempotent) (paper_books_repositories.py:72-83). - `paper_book_cash_ledger` — INITIAL_CAPITAL on book open, BUY_RESERVATION on `reserve_for_order`, later BUY_SETTLEMENT / FEE / SLIPPAGE / ORDER_RELEASE on fill; keyed by `(book_id, idempotency_key)` (paper_books_repositories.py:107-131). - `paper_book_risk_decisions` — every decision (approved and rejected) via `save_risk_decision` (paper_books_repositories.py:145-165). - `paper_book_orders` — the `PaperBookOrderIntent` row via `save_order_intent` (paper_books_repositories.py:190-207). - `paper_order_execution_claims` — LOCAL_SIMULATED or EXTERNAL_PAPER exclusivity claim (paper_books_repositories.py:1954-1988). - `paper_book_portfolio_snapshots` (+ per-symbol rows) — via `valuation.build_portfolio_snapshot`. - `paper_book_experiment_assignments` — pre-write of arm→intent identity for both arms (`save_assignment`, scheduled_integration.py:481-491). - `paper_book_economic_blackout_decisions` — when authoritative daily risk is enabled (scheduled_integration.py:331-335). - `paper_book_daily_risk_state` — via `daily_risk.calculate_and_persist_daily_risk_state`. - `paper_book_safety_pauses` — on DAILY_LOSS/DRAWDOWN reject. - Fill-time additions (out of approval-only scope but same transactional block): `paper_book_fills`, `paper_book_positions`, `paper_book_position_lots`, `paper_book_position_lifecycle_states`. - External path only: `paper_external_submission_queue`, later `paper_external_order_previews`/`_events`/`_fills` (out of local-approval scope).
- **External side effects.** None on the local-simulated approval path. `paper_books/execution.py::simulate_fill` uses only the caller-supplied `MarketSimulationInput` (bid/ask) — no HTTP, no broker RPC, no live quote endpoint (execution.py:9-20 explicit contract; scheduled_integration.py:172-217 tier-1/2/3 bounded-source policy). `_build_market_simulation_input` labels the modeled bid/ask `SIMULATED`, never `OBSERVED`. The external-broker branch (`cfg.external_broker.enabled` + book in `enabled_book_ids`) never actually calls the broker either — it only writes an operator-review queue row and returns `AWAITING_OPERATOR_EXTERNAL_SUBMISSION` (scheduled_integration.py:369-383; lifecycle.py:189-204, 457-472). No orders are ever placed with an external paper broker without an explicit operator preview + submit CLI action, consistent with the repository's "explicit user approval" guardrail in CLAUDE.md.
- **Failure behavior.** Fail-closed everywhere with bounded, typed error surfaces: - Config-disabled/unknown-cycle: raises `ScheduledIntegrationError` at entry (scheduled_integration.py:429, 432, 438). - Any per-symbol/per-arm exception is caught around `_process_arm` and recorded as `SymbolArmOutcome(outcome=OUTCOME_FAILED)` (scheduled_integration.py:507-511) — one arm's failure never poisons the other arm or the rest of the cycle. - Every eligibility miss produces a bounded outcome code (SKIPPED_* / REJECTED_BY_RISK) with reasons — never silent success. - `risk.evaluate_paper_risk` never raises for ordinary rejects; a rejected decision is still persisted (`persist_risk_decision` runs before the reject-branch return, scheduled_integration.py:346-360). - `submit_and_simulate` raises `FillSimulationError` on namespace conflict, on invalid market input (bid/ask non-positive or crossed), or on a mismatched pre-existing reservation (execution.py:69-70, 126-128, 147-149, 155-157, 165-168). - Atomic write blocks (`transaction()`/`begin_immediate` + try/except with `conn.rollback()` in execution.py:194-231) roll back all of {fill, position, settlement, release, status} on any exception. - `TransactionAlreadyActiveError` refuses to silently discard a caller's open transaction (transactions.py:38-53). - `LifecycleError` is raised for `paper_books.enabled=false` / `lifecycle.enabled=false` / non-tz-aware `as_of` at lifecycle entry, and one book's caught exception is stored in `failure_reasons` without stopping the other book (lifecycle.py:549-554, 643-645). - Recurring scheduler: pre-run safety gates + activation state + due-slot check + singleton lease all fail closed to STATUS_SKIPPED_* or STATUS_BLOCKED_SAFETY (recurring_scheduler.py:889-978).
- **Retry behavior.** All entry points are safe to re-invoke: state is queried and existing rows are treated as "already done": - `save_risk_decision` short-circuits on the deterministic `risk_decision_id` (paper_books_repositories.py:148-152). - `save_order_intent` short-circuits on `order_exists(book_id, paper_order_intent_id)` (paper_books_repositories.py:190-192). - `open_book` short-circuits on `book_exists` and its `init:{book_id}` ledger key is unique. - `reserve_for_order` short-circuits if `cash_ledger_entry_exists(book_id, "reserve:{intent_id}")` — never inserts a second reservation for the same intent (cash_ledger.py:141-142). - `claim_execution_namespace` is a no-op on the same namespace and raises on the other (paper_books_repositories.py:1972-1988). - `submit_and_simulate` re-entry finds `existing is not None`, re-verifies namespace/reservation consistency, then `fill_exists` guards the fill insert on both sides of its transaction (execution.py:181, 196-198). - `run_recurring_paper_scheduler` refuses to re-run a scheduler slot in a terminal state (recurring_scheduler.py:909-922) and holds a singleton lease for the running slot. No automatic retry loop in this workflow itself — retries come from the operator re-invoking the CLI or from the launchd/recurring scheduler firing again for the next slot. There is no exponential backoff or retry counter here.
- **Recovery behavior.** Resumability is achieved through deterministic IDs and idempotent inserts: - A retried `_process_arm` will read the same recommendation, produce the same `risk_decision_id` and `paper_order_intent_id`, and inserts are no-ops. - Intent inserted but no reservation: `submit_and_simulate`'s "existing" branch attempts a lazy `claim_execution_namespace` (only if `has_external_execution_evidence` is false) and then verifies `remaining_buy_reservation == intent.notional_usd` — if the pre-existing BUY intent has status PENDING_SUBMISSION and no reservation, it FAILS CLOSED rather than fabricating one (execution.py:158-168). - Interrupted fill: on replay, `fill_exists` inside and outside the atomic block short-circuits to a `{status:FILLED, fill:None}` idempotent no-op (execution.py:181, 196-198). - Cash-ledger release keyed by fill_id (`release_settled_buy_reservation`, cash_ledger.py:186-200) never double-releases. - Lifecycle-level "one book's failure never mutates the other" (lifecycle.py:643-645) means an in-progress arm crash rolls back inside its own transaction while the other arm proceeds. - Recurring scheduler: `TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES` check returns cached results (recurring_scheduler.py:910-922), so a restart after termination doesn't re-run the slot.
- **Terminal states.** - Intent-level: `FILLED`, `REJECTED`, `CANCELLED`, `EXPIRED` (see KNOWN_INTENT_STATUSES models.py:73-84). Under this local-approval workflow the reachable terminals from the approval flow are FILLED (fill crossed) or PENDING_SUBMISSION (still open; not terminal). Later lifecycle runs may transition PENDING_SUBMISSION → FILLED or → EXPIRED via `_process_pending_orders`. - Risk-decision level: the row is written once and never updated — its `decision` field is the terminal state (APPROVED / APPROVED_REDUCED / any REJECTED_*). - `SymbolArmOutcome.outcome` terminals: `OUTCOME_EXECUTED`, `OUTCOME_INTENT_CREATED_PENDING_FILL`, `OUTCOME_AWAITING_OPERATOR_EXTERNAL_SUBMISSION`, `OUTCOME_SKIPPED_*`, `OUTCOME_REJECTED_BY_RISK`, `OUTCOME_FAILED` (scheduled_integration.py:80-98). - Recurring scheduler run: TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES (recurring_scheduler.py:910); e.g. STATUS_COMPLETED / STATUS_BLOCKED_SAFETY / STATUS_SKIPPED_*. - Namespace claim: durable and never modified — LOCAL_SIMULATED or EXTERNAL_PAPER (paper_books_repositories.py:1928-1930).
- **Fail open or closed.** fail-closed
- **File refs.** `src/trading_research/paper_books/scheduled_integration.py:220-411`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/scheduled_integration.py:414-523`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/order_intent.py:24-64`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/risk.py:69-191`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/execution.py:99-232`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/execution.py:235-262`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/models.py:98-103`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/models.py:214-294`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/cash_ledger.py:71-200`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/lifecycle.py:174-254`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/lifecycle.py:304-533`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/lifecycle.py:536-663`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/recurring_scheduler.py:872-978`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/cli_support.py:140-219`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:145-207`, `src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1928-2005`, `src/trading_research/storage/transactions.py:1-75`, `src/trading_research/cli.py:2765`, `src/trading_research/cli.py:3365-3369`
- **Suspected defects.**
  - NeedsVerification: `_process_arm` writes a BUY intent with **no** cash reservation on the `OUTCOME_INTENT_CREATED_PENDING_FILL` branch when the market-simulation input is unavailable (scheduled_integration.py:385-391, `persist_order_intent(conn, intent)` alone, no `reserve_for_order`). On a subsequent invocation (e.g. next lifecycle `_process_pending_orders` at lifecycle.py:187-232), `submit_and_simulate` takes the `existing is not None` branch (execution.py:117-168) and — because `existing['side']==BUY` and status==PENDING_SUBMISSION — evaluates `remaining_buy_reservation(...) != intent.notional_usd` (0 vs positive notional), which raises `FillSimulationError('existing reservation does not match the frozen intent notional -- refusing to proceed rather than fabricate a release or adjustment')`. That would strand any BUY intent that was ever persisted via the no-market-input path and later re-processed. This appears to contradict Milestone 11.3.1 Item 6 Part A's stated intent that a new intent's insert + BUY reservation be atomic — the guarantee only holds when `submit_and_simulate` created the intent, not when `persist_order_intent` did. Worth deeper investigation via tests to confirm reachability. Files: src/trading_research/paper_books/scheduled_integration.py:385-391 and lifecycle.py:458 (also mirrored at 475), vs src/trading_research/paper_books/execution.py:158-168.
  - NeedsVerification: `paper_books.risk.evaluate_paper_risk` accepts `context.valuation_status in (VALUATION_COMPLETE, VALUATION_PARTIAL_STALE_PRICE)` (risk.py:139-144) — i.e. it will approve sizing off a portfolio whose valuation is only 'partial stale'. Combined with the fact that `_process_arm`'s ATR gate demands a live entry-day bar (scheduled_integration.py:125 requires `bars[-1].session_date == as_of.date()`), the two staleness policies are slightly asymmetric: the same run can reject on ATR staleness but approve on stale portfolio valuation. Worth confirming this is intentional per ADR 0006.
  - NeedsVerification: `order_intent.build_order_intent` derives `limit_price = approved_notional_usd / approved_quantity` (order_intent.py:50) as an exact Decimal division. Because `risk.evaluate_paper_risk` computes `final_notional = approved_quantity * reference_price` (risk.py:183), the round-trip `final_notional / approved_quantity == reference_price` holds exactly; but `PaperBookOrderIntent.__post_init__` re-checks `notional_usd == quantity * limit_price` (models.py:277). This is only guaranteed if Decimal division of `(qty*reference_price)/qty` returns bit-identical `reference_price` — which it does for finite-quantity integer division. However, if any upstream ATR/policy path in the future substitutes a different `approved_notional_usd` that is not an exact multiple of `approved_quantity`, the constructor will fail closed. Not a bug today, but a brittleness worth noting as construction is one hop removed from the sizing math.
  - NeedsVerification: `persist_risk_decision` calls `save_risk_decision` which unconditionally commits after INSERT (paper_books_repositories.py:164 `conn.commit()`), then `_process_arm` proceeds to `build_order_intent` + `submit_and_simulate` in separate transactional units. If the process crashes between the risk-decision commit and the subsequent intent claim/insert, the decision row exists but no intent/claim/reservation does. On retry the deterministic IDs mean the flow re-runs cleanly (decision insert is a no-op, intent goes through), so this is not a correctness bug — but the risk decision is not part of the atomic 'claim+intent+reserve' window from Item 6 Part A. Confirm this is intentional (Item 6 Part A explicitly names only claim+intent+reservation).
  - NeedsVerification: On the `OUTCOME_AWAITING_OPERATOR_EXTERNAL_SUBMISSION` branch (scheduled_integration.py:369-383, and mirrors in lifecycle.py:189-204/457-472), the intent is persisted and the queue row is written, but **no** `claim_execution_namespace` for EXTERNAL_PAPER is issued at this point. The external-execution namespace claim presumably happens later in the external-broker preview path — need to confirm the external flow reliably claims before a concurrent local `submit_and_simulate` could hit `has_external_execution_evidence == False` and lazy-claim LOCAL, defeating the exclusivity guarantee for a brief window. See execution.py:139-149 lazy-claim branch.

#### Fresh research invocation flows: a CLI or scheduler builds an EvidenceSnapshot for one symbol and calls the only provider-invoking function analyze_with_research_committee (src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:691-924). (1) Fail-closed pre-check at orchestration.py:727-733: when require_decision=True and MANAGER_ROLE not in configuration.roles, raise ManagerNotConfiguredError before any provider call. (2) Compute deterministic research_run_id = 'run-' + sha256(snapshot_id, provider, model_name, roles, run_mode, config_hash, per-role prompt versions+hashes, system_prompt_hash)[:32] via compute_research_run_id (orchestration.py:214-236, invoked at 735-738). (3) Reuse gate at orchestration.py:740-762: if research_repository.get_run_status returns RUN_STATUS_COMPLETED, RUN_STATUS_ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE, or RUN_STATUS_ANALYST_REPORTS_COMPLETE_NO_MANAGER, return persisted decision/role_reports with reused_existing_run=True and no provider call is made. RUN_STATUS_RUNNING and RUN_STATUS_FAILED are NOT short-circuited. (4) Snapshot preflight validate_snapshot_preconditions (orchestration.py:764-767). (5) Persist run header only when get_run_status returned None: save_run_started INSERTs a research_committee_runs row with status='RUNNING' using INSERT OR IGNORE and commits (research_repositories.py:173-183; schema at research_schema.py:37-48). If preflight rejected, mark_run_finished(RUN_STATUS_ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE) and return with incomplete_reasons (orchestration.py:775-782). (6) For each analyst role (excluding manager) the orchestrator queries role reports already persisted for (research_run_id, role); if present it reuses that report; otherwise it calls _run_role_with_retries (orchestration.py:790-839). (7) _run_role_with_retries (orchestration.py:352-673) loops up to configuration.max_attempts_per_role. Each attempt: build system+user prompt via build_system_prompt/build_user_prompt with the JSON schema chosen by _schema_for_role, form a ResearchModelRequest, derive attempt_id via _attempt_id (research_run_id-role-attempt_number, with a scheduler_run_id suffix for scheduled correlation, orchestration.py:245-252). If an attempt_controller is supplied (shadow/attempt_controller.py adapter), invoke before_attempt; a not-allowed decision persists a STAGE_BUDGET_GATED / role-gated / paused-or-killed failure row, records a synthetic ResearchAttemptRecord with usage marked USAGE_NOT_RETURNED, breaks the retry loop, and never calls the provider (orchestration.py:395-442). Otherwise the provider call is provider.generate_structured(request) or, when a ResearchTokenBudgetController is injected, token_budget_controller.invoke(provider, request, provider_name=...) (orchestration.py:445-450). (8) Provider-side (e.g., AnthropicResearchProvider.generate_structured, anthropic_provider.py:102-233) forces a strict tool_use structured output, catches APITimeout/RateLimit/APIConnection/APIStatus into ProviderTimeout/RateLimit/Transient/Unavailable errors, validates that exactly one matching tool_use block was returned, extracts usage tokens, and returns a ResearchModelResponse. Deterministic and Claude-code/Codex providers implement the same protocol (research/provider_protocol.py:17-27). (9) Exception handling in the orchestrator: ProviderUnavailableError -> record failure, break (non-retryable termination, orchestration.py:451-474); ProviderTimeoutError/RateLimit/Transient/Malformed/SchemaValidation -> classify to structured failures via _failure_from_exc/schema_errors, decide retry eligibility from the structured failure's retryable flag (Milestone 12.1.1 change: not from the exception class), rebuild validation_feedback and continue, else break (orchestration.py:475-522). (10) Successful response is fed to build_decision/build_role_report + validate_decision/validate_role_report; EvidenceValidationError/SchemaValidationError produce classified retryable failures; claim-level failures produce STAGE_CLAIM_EVIDENCE_VALIDATION rows (orchestration.py:524-629, _claim_validation_failures at 314-349). Each attempt appends a ResearchAttemptRecord to the in-memory list; the outer analyze_with_research_committee then persists them one-by-one via save_attempt (research_repositories.py:185-219, orchestration.py:807-811). (11) A validated analyst report is persisted with save_role_report (research_repositories.py:248-258) and appended to valid_reports. If any required analyst role produced no valid report, the orchestrator appends a CODE_MISSING_REQUIRED_ROLE failure, records a manager-skipped failure (only when a manager was configured, orchestration.py:850-863), calls mark_run_finished(RUN_STATUS_ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE), and returns without ever calling the manager (orchestration.py:841-871). (12) When all analysts succeeded and no manager is configured (require_decision=False), the run finishes with RUN_STATUS_ANALYST_REPORTS_COMPLETE_NO_MANAGER (orchestration.py:873-887). Otherwise _run_role_with_retries is invoked for MANAGER_ROLE (orchestration.py:889-901) with valid_reports fed to the prompt. (13) On a valid ResearchDecision, save_decision persists a research_decisions row (research_repositories.py:260-270) and mark_run_finished writes RUN_STATUS_COMPLETED (or RUN_STATUS_ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE when decision.rating=='ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE'); the OrchestrationResult is returned with attempts, failures, role_reports, decision (orchestration.py:913-923). The scheduled cycle wrapper run_scheduled_research_cycle/_run_symbol (scheduled_cycle.py:318-412, 415-588) is the outermost orchestrator that computes cycle_id, persists evidence snapshot, computes evidence-completeness gate, calls analyze_with_research_committee when the gate does not block (scheduled_cycle.py:516-521), records provider provenance rows (scheduled_cycle.py:526-546), applies overlay/save_overlay_decision, saves frozen recommendations, and only then optionally submits a baseline paper order.

- **Entry point.** Programmatic entry: analyze_with_research_committee at src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:691. CLI entry: run_research_cli at src/trading_research/cli.py:775 (dispatched from the run-research subcommand). Scheduler/cycle entry: run_scheduled_research_cycle at src/trading_research/research/scheduled_cycle.py:318 which calls analyze_with_research_committee via _run_symbol at scheduled_cycle.py:516.
- **Authoritative validation.** Layered, all inside orchestration.py's retry loop. (a) Snapshot preconditions before any provider call: validate_snapshot_preconditions checks require_point_in_time_safe and fail_on_stale_required_evidence (orchestration.py:764-767 calling research/evidence_validation.py). (b) JSON-schema enforcement occurs at the provider boundary (e.g., Anthropic tool strict=true, anthropic_provider.py:110-115) and again on the parsed_json via build_decision/build_role_report + JSON schema (output_validation.py; orchestration.py:524-538). SchemaValidationError.schema_errors is unwound into per-field ResearchValidationFailure rows (orchestration.py:476-486, 547-557). (c) Manager/role-report semantic validation: validate_decision / validate_role_report (claim_validation.py) enforcing evidence-id whitelisting (allowed_evidence_ids from EvidenceSnapshot at orchestration.py:374) and material_claim_unsupported (orchestration.py:314-349, 600-629). (d) Manager configuration check fails closed before any call: ManagerNotConfiguredError at orchestration.py:727-733. (e) Attempt-control gate before every provider attempt (attempt_controller.before_attempt, orchestration.py:395-442) — a shadow-budget/kill-switch veto is treated as authoritative, non-retryable within this role invocation.
- **Idempotency key.** Deterministic research_run_id = 'run-' + sha256(snapshot_id, provider_name, model_name, roles, run_mode, config_hash, per-role {version,text_hash}, system_prompt_hash)[:32] (orchestration.py:214-236). Attempt idempotency key is _attempt_id(research_run_id, role, attempt_number[, scheduler_run_id]) (orchestration.py:245-252). Downstream storage keys: research_committee_runs.research_run_id PRIMARY KEY (research_schema.py:38); research_attempts.attempt_id PRIMARY KEY (research_schema.py:52); research_role_reports.report_id PK + UNIQUE(research_run_id, role) (research_schema.py:99-108); research_decisions.decision_id PK + UNIQUE research_run_id (research_schema.py:112-119); research_attempt_failures.failure_id deterministic via failure_taxonomy.new_failure and INSERT OR IGNORE (research_repositories.py:221-239). save_run_started uses INSERT OR IGNORE (research_repositories.py:177-182).
- **Transaction boundary.** No overarching transaction. SQLiteResearchRepository calls self._conn.commit() after every single write: save_run_started (research_repositories.py:183), save_attempt (:219), save_attempt_failure (:239), save_role_report (:258), save_decision (:270), mark_run_finished (:277). The orchestrator issues these commits one at a time as it progresses through analysts and manager (orchestration.py:807-811, 826-827, 838-839, 862-865, 897-901, 914-917). Consequences: (i) an attempt row is durably visible before the failure rows tied to it; (ii) if the process crashes between save_role_report and mark_run_finished the run stays at status='RUNNING' with no cleanup on resume. There is no BEGIN IMMEDIATE / lease / row-level lock in the research repository (verified by absence of any lease/lock/BEGIN/isolation_level references in research_repositories.py).
- **Lease or concurrency.** No lease. The only concurrency protection is the deterministic research_run_id + INSERT OR IGNORE on research_committee_runs (research_repositories.py:177-182): two concurrent runs with the same inputs will race to insert the header row, but subsequent writes (attempts, role reports, decision, mark_run_finished) are NOT guarded by a status-conditional UPDATE or a WHERE status='RUNNING' predicate. mark_run_finished (research_repositories.py:272-277) blindly overwrites status/completed_at. There is no reservation/heartbeat table, no attempt_control_check row-lock, no advisory lock, and no process-owner column. The scheduler-side idempotency key is per-symbol per-cycle f'{cycle_id}:{symbol}:baseline' (scheduled_cycle.py:426), used only to seed build_real_candidate_input.
- **State transitions.** research_committee_runs.status transitions (orchestration.py + research_repositories.py): (initial) NULL -> RUNNING (save_run_started at :773 via INSERT OR IGNORE); RUNNING -> ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE (preflight failure :776-777, required analyst role failure :864-865, manager-decision missing :904-905, decision.rating=='ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE' :916-917); RUNNING -> ANALYST_REPORTS_COMPLETE_NO_MANAGER (:880-881); RUNNING -> COMPLETED (:916-917). RUN_STATUS_FAILED constant exists (orchestration.py:60) but is never written by any code path. Per-attempt: ResearchAttemptRecord.success is set true only on the final validated attempt (orchestration.py:631-642), otherwise carries failure_reason + typed failure_code/stage/retryable/metadata (orchestration.py:459-505, 580-592, 610-621, 662-672). Failures follow STAGE_STRUCTURED_SCHEMA / STAGE_CLAIM_EVIDENCE_VALIDATION / STAGE_BUDGET_GATED / STAGE_REQUIRED_ROLE_FAILED / STAGE_MANAGER_SKIPPED / STAGE_RETRY_EXHAUSTED.
- **Persistent records.** Tables (research_schema.py): research_evidence_snapshots (:20), research_committee_runs (:37-48), research_attempts (:51-94, append-only with UPDATE/DELETE triggers at :222-228), research_role_reports (:98-109, append-only with triggers :214-220), research_decisions (:111-119, append-only with triggers :206-213), research_overlay_decisions (:121), research_experiment_assignments (:131), research_failures (:144), research_attempt_failures (:161, append-only with triggers :230-236). Writers: save_evidence_snapshot (invoked from scheduled_cycle.py:448), save_run_started, save_attempt, save_attempt_failure(s), save_role_report, save_decision, mark_run_finished (research_repositories.py:173-277). The scheduler also writes save_corporate_status_evidence, save_evidence_completeness_result, save_symbol_evidence_status, record_cycle_provider_provenance, save_overlay_decision, save_frozen_recommendation, save_experiment_assignment (scheduled_cycle.py:441-569).
- **External side effects.** Only outbound network calls happen inside ResearchModelProvider.generate_structured. AnthropicResearchProvider (anthropic_provider.py:125-132) POSTs to the Anthropic messages endpoint with tools=[strict tool] and tool_choice fixed to the structured-output tool. ClaudeCodeResearchProvider and CodexResearchProvider run bounded subprocess calls to their CLIs (claude_code_provider.py:279-361, codex_provider.py:342-459 via research/bounded_subprocess.py). DeterministicResearchProvider stays local. No external paper orders are issued from this workflow; paper submission is a separate branch of the cycle (scheduled_cycle.py:571-578) governed by configuration.submit_paper_orders and experiment_policy.may_submit_baseline. No SMS / email / third-party webhooks are called in the research path.
- **Failure behavior.** Fail-closed at every boundary. (a) ManagerNotConfiguredError raised before any provider call when require_decision=True and manager is missing (orchestration.py:727-733). (b) Snapshot preflight -> RUN_STATUS_ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE, no provider call (orchestration.py:775-782). (c) Attempt-control veto -> STAGE_BUDGET_GATED failure, ResearchAttemptRecord persisted with usage='USAGE_NOT_RETURNED', break, no retry (orchestration.py:408-442). (d) ProviderUnavailableError -> non-retryable termination, structured failure persisted (orchestration.py:451-474). (e) Retryable provider errors -> classified failure, validation_feedback rebuilt, next attempt; when structured failure.retryable is not True the loop breaks even inside _RETRYABLE_ERRORS (orchestration.py:508-520 — Milestone 12.1.1 Item 1 note). (f) Schema/evidence/claim validation failures -> retryable failures classified into codes (CODE_MISSING_BEAR_CASE, CODE_SCHEMA_REQUIRED_FIELD_MISSING, CODE_UNSUPPORTED_MATERIAL_CLAIM, ...) rather than crashing the run (orchestration.py:539-598, 600-629). (g) Retry exhaustion appends CODE_RETRY_EXHAUSTED failure (orchestration.py:651-672). (h) Required analyst role failure -> manager is not invoked; CODE_MISSING_REQUIRED_ROLE + CODE_MANAGER_NOT_INVOKED rows written; run marked ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE (orchestration.py:841-871). Per-symbol failure inside the scheduler is caught and, unless configuration.continue_on_symbol_failure is False, is wrapped as SymbolCycleResult with SYMBOL_STATUS_FAILED without terminating the whole cycle (scheduled_cycle.py:390-396).
- **Retry behavior.** Retries are per-role only. _run_role_with_retries loops attempt_number in range(1, configuration.max_attempts_per_role + 1) (orchestration.py:376). Retry eligibility is driven by the structured failure's retryable flag (orchestration.py:494-522), not by the exception class caught. Retry feedback built by build_retry_feedback(failures, allowed_evidence_ids) is threaded into the next prompt (orchestration.py:521, 597, 628 -> user_prompt at :381). No sleep/backoff is implemented in this file; if the provider raises RateLimit the retry is issued immediately. Non-retryable outcomes (ProviderUnavailableError, retryable=False structured failure, attempt-control veto) break out of the retry loop without incrementing further. The manager is invoked with its own independent retry budget (orchestration.py:889-894). Cross-run retry is achieved by re-invoking analyze_with_research_committee with the same inputs: the reuse gate short-circuits COMPLETED / ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE / ANALYST_REPORTS_COMPLETE_NO_MANAGER runs (orchestration.py:740-762), and inside a still-RUNNING or FAILED run any already-persisted role_report is skipped (orchestration.py:791-797).
- **Recovery behavior.** Resume behavior when a prior invocation crashed mid-run: (i) if the run reached COMPLETED / ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE / ANALYST_REPORTS_COMPLETE_NO_MANAGER, the reuse gate returns the persisted result with reused_existing_run=True and no provider call is made (orchestration.py:740-762). (ii) If it is still RUNNING or FAILED, the code re-enters preflight and, for each analyst role, checks research_repository.get_role_reports_for_run and reuses any (research_run_id, role) role report already persisted (orchestration.py:791-796); un-covered roles re-run through _run_role_with_retries. save_run_started uses INSERT OR IGNORE so re-entering does not overwrite the header. Attempt IDs remain deterministic per (run, role, attempt_number), so save_attempt on a re-entry will raise UniqueConstraint on the primary key (research_attempts.attempt_id) since save_attempt uses plain INSERT (research_repositories.py:187-197) rather than INSERT OR IGNORE — potential resume defect. There is no scavenger/janitor for orphan RUNNING rows.
- **Terminal states.** Persisted in research_committee_runs.status: COMPLETED (successful manager decision), ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE (preflight rejection, missing required role, manager retry exhaustion, or decision.rating=='ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE'), ANALYST_REPORTS_COMPLETE_NO_MANAGER (explicit require_decision=False success). Constant RUN_STATUS_FAILED='FAILED' (orchestration.py:60) is defined but never written — a partial/crashed run remains 'RUNNING' indefinitely. Returned OrchestrationResult mirrors these statuses (orchestration.py:745-923). At the scheduler layer, terminal cycle_status values are CYCLE_STATUS_COMPLETED / CYCLE_STATUS_PARTIALLY_COMPLETE / CYCLE_STATUS_FAILED (scheduled_cycle.py:78-81, 400-407).
- **Fail open or closed.** fail-closed
- **File refs.** `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:57-81`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:214-236`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:245-275`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:352-673`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:691-924`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/research/scheduled_cycle.py:318-412`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/research/scheduled_cycle.py:415-588`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/research/provider_protocol.py:17-27`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/research/anthropic_provider.py:91-233`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/storage/research_repositories.py:146-277`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/storage/research_schema.py:37-236`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/cli.py:775-846`
- **Suspected defects.**
  - Save_attempt uses plain INSERT (research_repositories.py:187) — a resume of a still-RUNNING run whose _attempt_id collides with an already-persisted attempt will raise sqlite3.IntegrityError inside analyze_with_research_committee. save_run_started (:178), save_attempt_failure (:226), save_role_report (:250), save_decision (:262) all use INSERT OR IGNORE, so save_attempt is the odd one out. Worth deeper investigation given the design intent that attempts be idempotently replayable on resume.
  - No lease or concurrency protection on research_committee_runs (research_repositories.py:152-277). Two processes with the same research_run_id can both pass the reuse gate (both see status NULL or RUNNING) and race to call the provider; save_run_started's INSERT OR IGNORE only deduplicates the header row, not the provider calls or the attempt inserts. mark_run_finished blindly overwrites status/completed_at with no WHERE status='RUNNING' predicate (research_repositories.py:272-277). No mitigation via SQLite BEGIN IMMEDIATE is visible.
  - RUN_STATUS_FAILED constant is declared (orchestration.py:60) but never written. A process crash between save_run_started and any terminal mark_run_finished leaves an orphan row at status='RUNNING' forever; there is no janitor and no code path that transitions RUNNING -> FAILED, so run bookkeeping is silently misleading after crashes. The reuse gate (orchestration.py:740-762) also does not act on RUNNING/FAILED, so a fresh caller will re-enter and attempt to re-insert attempts (interacts with finding #1).
  - Every SQLiteResearchRepository write commits immediately (research_repositories.py:183, 219, 239, 258, 270, 277). There is no atomic 'flush the whole run' transaction; a crash between save_role_report and save_decision leaves valid role reports without a decision, and a crash after save_decision but before mark_run_finished leaves an immutable decision row (append-only trigger, research_schema.py:206-213) tied to a run stuck in RUNNING. Consider a single BEGIN IMMEDIATE around per-attempt persistence.
  - attempt_control_check_id is required for CORRELATION_SCHEDULED (orchestration.py:118-125) but the field is populated from AttemptControlDecision (orchestration.py:262-275). If a shadow controller returns CORRELATION_SCHEDULED without setting attempt_control_check_id, ResearchAttemptRecord.__post_init__ will raise ValueError and the whole retry loop terminates uncaught inside _run_role_with_retries — potentially fail-closed but noisy. Confirm shadow adapter always populates these three fields.
  - Anthropic error mapping (anthropic_provider.py:151-166) classifies every non-transient APIStatusError (401/403/404, invalid_request, billing, model-not-found, etc.) as ProviderUnavailableError with retryable=False, which the orchestrator treats as terminal for the role (orchestration.py:472-474). This is correct for auth/billing but conflates transient 400 issues (e.g., a schema the model temporarily refuses) with permanent unavailability, and CODE_PROVIDER_CLIENT_ERROR carries no differentiation for downstream metrics. NeedsVerification.
  - The reuse gate (orchestration.py:740-762) does not verify that the persisted decision/reports pass the current claim_validation policy. A prior run persisted under an older validate_decision policy (e.g., pre-Milestone-6.1 material_claim_unsupported) will be replayed as-is on a rerun even after policy upgrades, because the reuse gate short-circuits before validate_decision runs. Mitigated in practice because config_hash and prompt hashes bump research_run_id, but a validator-only change without a config_hash bump would silently reuse.

#### Research-reuse (reused vs fresh, budget accounting) is implemented as two loosely-coupled layers, both anchored to the same SQLite database.

LAYER 1 - Pre-flight shortlist gate (strategies/research_budget.py)
- `decide_research_action(entry, state, config)` (src/trading_research/strategies/research_budget.py:62-90) is a pure deterministic function that returns one of SEND_TO_RESEARCH / SKIP_LOW_SIGNAL / SKIP_DAILY_CANDIDATE_CAP / SKIP_DAILY_TOKEN_CAP based on caller-supplied in-memory `DailyResearchBudgetState` (symbols_sent_today, fresh_cycles_today, tokens_spent_today, daily_token_budget) (:40-52) and `ShortlistConfig` (config/strategies.yaml:62-64 loaded by strategies/config.py:120-138,265-279).
- After the orchestrator is invoked, `record_research_outcome(symbol, reused_existing_run)` (:93-98) returns REUSE_RESEARCH vs RUN_FULL_RESEARCH, derived from `OrchestrationResult.reused_existing_run` (research/orchestration.py:193, 747/754/761 = True, 781/869/885/910/922 = False).
- These two functions are unreferenced in src/ (only tests/unit/test_research_budget.py imports them); the scheduled cycle does not call the pre-flight gate.

LAYER 2 - Orchestrator run-identity reuse (research/orchestration.py)
- `compute_research_run_id` (:214-236) hashes {snapshot_id, provider, model, roles, run_mode, config_hash, prompt versions/hashes, system_prompt_hash} to a stable `run-<sha256[:32]>` identity. Prompt-version bump or config-hash change forces a fresh run.
- `analyze_with_research_committee` (:691-924) queries `ResearchRepository.get_run_status(research_run_id)` at :741. If status is COMPLETED, ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE, or ANALYST_REPORTS_COMPLETE_NO_MANAGER, it returns the persisted decision/role_reports with `reused_existing_run=True` and never calls the provider, never invokes the token-budget controller, never invokes the attempt controller (:742-762).
- Otherwise it calls `save_run_started(...)` (:769-773) with INSERT OR IGNORE (storage/research_repositories.py:173-183) and runs analyst roles then the manager. Per-role reuse also happens at :791-797 - a role whose report was previously persisted is short-circuited before any provider call. Terminal state is written by `mark_run_finished` (:777, 865, 881, 905, 917; storage/research_repositories.py:272-277).

LAYER 3 - Persistent token budget (strategies/research_budget.py + research/token_budget.py)
- Wired via `ResearchTokenBudgetController` Protocol (research/orchestration.py:172-181) and only called inside `_run_role_with_retries` when the controller is provided (:445-449). `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` (research/token_budget.py:22-97) is the concrete implementation.
- Per attempt: `reserve_research_tokens` (strategies/research_budget.py:235-340) is called with `research_attempt_identity=f"{role}:{attempt_number}"` (token_budget.py:49). Idempotency key = `research_token_budget:{run_id}:{symbol}:{provider}:{model}:{attempt_identity}:{utc_date}` (:158-166).
- Inside a `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction (storage/transactions.py:38-74) it: (a) looks up existing key via `load_budget_reservation_by_idempotency_key` and returns the existing row if compatible (:281-291); (b) scans `list_budget_reservations(conn)` for any AMBIGUOUS row sharing the run/symbol/provider/model prefix and rejects with TOKEN_RESERVATION_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED (:292-309); (c) rejects if any RESEARCH_TOKENS row for the same budget_date has emergency_margin_breached=1 (:215-221, 310-314); (d) sums live counted tokens (RESERVED + SETTLED + AMBIGUOUS) for the UTC date (:203-212), rejects if requested_total > cap (:315-321); (e) INSERTs a new `shadow_budget_reservations` row with `reservation_kind='RESEARCH_TOKENS'`, `budget_date`, `token_accounting_policy`, status=RESERVED (:322-334).
- Then invokes `provider.generate_structured` (token_budget.py:66). `ProviderUnavailableError` -> `release_research_tokens` (:70-71 -> strategies/research_budget.py:413-421 RESERVED->RELEASED). Any other BaseException including timeouts, malformed output, process interruption -> `mark_research_tokens_ambiguous` (:72-77 -> strategies/research_budget.py:424-431 RESERVED->AMBIGUOUS). Missing authoritative usage -> mark AMBIGUOUS + raise ProviderUnavailableError TOKEN_USAGE_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED (:79-85). Otherwise `settle_research_tokens` (:86-93 -> strategies/research_budget.py:366-410 RESERVED->SETTLED with consumed_* columns and breached flag).
- Reused runs skip the controller entirely: `analyze_with_research_committee`'s early return path at :742-762 fires before `_run_role_with_retries` is ever entered, so a reused run consumes zero token budget.

- **Entry point.** Production entry: `research/scheduled_cycle.py:516` (`analyze_with_research_committee(...)` inside the per-symbol cycle loop) and `cli.py:833` (single-snapshot `analyze` command). Both eventually reach `research/orchestration.py::analyze_with_research_committee` (line 691). The pre-flight shortlist gate `strategies/research_budget.py::decide_research_action` has no production caller in src/. The persistent token-budget wrapper `research/token_budget.py::PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` has no production instantiation in src/ (only tests/unit/test_research_orchestration.py:401).
- **Authoritative validation.** Run-reuse is authoritative on `research_committee_runs.status` read via `SQLiteResearchRepository.get_run_status` (storage/research_repositories.py:152-156). Token-budget authoritative validation is done inside `reserve_research_tokens` (strategies/research_budget.py:257-321): non-negative-int checks (:169-172), policy allowlist (:175-181), clock timezone check (:265-266), caller/clock date-match (:268-272), non-empty attempt identity (:262-263), incompatible existing reservation guard (:283-290), AMBIGUOUS-prefix guard (:296-309), emergency_margin_breached-blocks-day guard (:310-314), and total-live-tokens-vs-cap check (:315-321). Reconciliation gates (:434-535) require operator+reason+source and mandate SETTLED-usage evidence.
- **Idempotency key.** Two independent keys operate at different layers. - Research-run identity (reuse key): `compute_research_run_id` = `run-<sha256[:32]>` over sorted-JSON of {snapshot_id, provider, model_name, roles, run_mode, config_hash, per-role prompt version+hash, system_prompt_hash} (research/orchestration.py:214-236). Any change (new snapshot, prompt bump, config change) creates a new run and defeats reuse. - Token-reservation idempotency_key: `research_token_budget:{research_run_id}:{symbol}:{provider}:{model_name|'none'}:{research_attempt_identity}:{utc_date}` (strategies/research_budget.py:158-166), where `research_attempt_identity = f"{role}:{attempt_number}"` (research/token_budget.py:49). Enforced by `UNIQUE (idempotency_key)` on `shadow_budget_reservations` (storage/shadow_operations_schema.py:84).
- **Transaction boundary.** Token-budget writes: `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` via `transaction(conn)` context manager (storage/transactions.py:57-74) wraps the read-check-write in `reserve_research_tokens` (strategies/research_budget.py:280-340), every `_transition_reservation` (settle/release/ambiguous) (:347-363), and `reconcile_ambiguous_research_tokens` (:503-534). SQLite `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` serializes concurrent writers so no other reserve/settle can interleave. `TransactionAlreadyActiveError` (storage/transactions.py:26-35) fails closed if a caller already holds a transaction. Research-run persistence writes are NOT wrapped in an explicit transaction; each `save_run_started`/`save_attempt`/`save_role_report`/`save_decision`/`mark_run_finished` (storage/research_repositories.py:173-277) does its own `self._conn.commit()`. Reuse dedup uses `INSERT OR IGNORE` on `research_committee_runs` (:178).
- **Lease or concurrency.** There is no explicit lease around research-run reuse: `get_run_status` (storage/research_repositories.py:152-156) is a plain SELECT outside any transaction, and `save_run_started` relies on INSERT OR IGNORE (:178) so two concurrent workers that both observe status=None can both proceed to run the same research_run_id. Concurrency protection for tokens is provided solely by `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` serialization on `shadow_budget_reservations` writes (storage/transactions.py:38-54) plus the `UNIQUE (idempotency_key)` constraint (storage/shadow_operations_schema.py:84). No advisory lock or lease row exists for the "one fresh cycle per (snapshot, provider, model, role, attempt) budget" invariant across separate processes.
- **State transitions.** Research run: absent -> RUNNING (`save_run_started`, storage/research_repositories.py:180) -> COMPLETED | ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE | ANALYST_REPORTS_COMPLETE_NO_MANAGER via `mark_run_finished` (research/orchestration.py:777, 865, 881, 905, 916-917). Reuse decision is made purely by inspecting the terminal status (:741-762). Token reservation: absent -> RESERVED (strategies/research_budget.py:322-334) -> SETTLED (:366-410) | RELEASED (:413-421) | AMBIGUOUS (:424-431). AMBIGUOUS -> SETTLED | RELEASED only via `reconcile_ambiguous_research_tokens` (:434-535) with operator evidence; other transitions from AMBIGUOUS are rejected. `_transition_reservation` (:343-363) enforces from-status conditionally and treats a compatible target-status row as an idempotent no-op (returns False).
