- **Persistent records.** - `research_committee_runs` (research_run_id PK, status, snapshot_id, provider, model, roles_json, run_mode, config_hash, created_at, completed_at) -- storage/research_repositories.py:177-181. - `research_attempts`, `research_attempt_failures`, `research_role_reports`, `research_decisions`, `research_overlay_decisions` -- storage/research_repositories.py:185-303. - `shadow_budget_reservations` (idempotency_key UNIQUE, reservation_kind='RESEARCH_TOKENS', budget_date, reserved_*_tokens, consumed_*_tokens, token_accounting_policy, provider_request_id, status, emergency_margin_breached, created_at, settled_at) -- storage/shadow_operations_schema.py:62-85. - `shadow_token_budget_reconciliations` (reconciliation_id, reservation_id FK, from/to_status, operator, reason, reconciliation_source, actual_*_tokens, token_accounting_policy, reconciled_at) -- storage/shadow_operations_schema.py:87-101. - No persistent record of the REUSE_RESEARCH vs RUN_FULL_RESEARCH pre-flight label; `record_research_outcome` returns it in-memory only.
- **External side effects.** Only when the reuse path does not fire and the token reservation succeeds: `provider.generate_structured(request)` calls the injected `ResearchModelProvider` (research/token_budget.py:66) which is the sole external LLM call (research/orchestration.py:3-8). No paper-order submission or broker call occurs on this path. All bookkeeping writes go to the local SQLite database.
- **Failure behavior.** - Reserved cap exceeded -> `TokenBudgetRejected` (strategies/research_budget.py:317-321) -> `ProviderUnavailableError(retryable=False, code='BUDGET_EXHAUSTED')` in the controller (research/token_budget.py:51-54). Orchestrator classifies as non-retryable, terminates the role with `_TERMINATION_NON_RETRYABLE_FAILURE` (research/orchestration.py:472-474). - Provider adapter refuses to send (`ProviderUnavailableError` inside `generate_structured`) -> reservation RELEASED (research/token_budget.py:67-71); no budget consumed. - Any other exception during/after transmission (timeout, rate limit, malformed output, schema validation error, KeyboardInterrupt) -> reservation AMBIGUOUS (research/token_budget.py:72-77) and the exception re-raised; the day is effectively frozen for that run/symbol/provider/model until reconciled (strategies/research_budget.py:292-309). - Missing authoritative usage in response -> AMBIGUOUS + ProviderUnavailableError TOKEN_USAGE_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED (research/token_budget.py:79-85). - Reconciliation-required or emergency-margin breach blocks all subsequent reservations for the same UTC date (strategies/research_budget.py:215-221, 310-314).
- **Retry behavior.** Retries happen at the role level in `_run_role_with_retries` (research/orchestration.py:352-673). Each retry increments `attempt_number`, which changes `research_attempt_identity=f"{role}:{attempt_number}"` and therefore the token idempotency_key (strategies/research_budget.py:158-166, research/token_budget.py:49). Consequences: - A fresh, independent reservation is created per attempt against the same daily cap. - If a prior attempt is AMBIGUOUS, the next attempt is rejected by the prefix scan (strategies/research_budget.py:292-309), which the controller turns into a non-retryable ProviderUnavailableError (research/token_budget.py:55-59), so `_run_role_with_retries` terminates with `_TERMINATION_NON_RETRYABLE_FAILURE`. - A duplicate call with the same attempt_number replays and returns the existing reservation (:281-291) - true idempotency at the attempt level.
- **Recovery behavior.** Recovery is limited to reuse of already-terminal work and operator-driven reconciliation. - Run-level: any subsequent `analyze_with_research_committee` call with the same identity replays the persisted decision/reports without provider calls (:742-762). Analyst reports that already exist are skipped inside a partially-completed run (:791-797). - Reservation-level: `reconcile_ambiguous_research_tokens` (strategies/research_budget.py:434-535) is the only recovery for AMBIGUOUS holds. It requires operator, reason, reconciliation_source and (for SETTLED) authoritative token counts + policy, writes an append-only row to `shadow_token_budget_reconciliations` (storage/shadow_operations_repositories.py:115-126), and returns False as an idempotent no-op if the target status is already set (:513-521). - No automatic reaper for stale RESERVED rows exists in this module; RESERVED that never settles or releases stays counted against the cap indefinitely.
- **Terminal states.** Research run terminal statuses: COMPLETED, ANALYSIS_INCOMPLETE, ANALYST_REPORTS_COMPLETE_NO_MANAGER, FAILED (research/orchestration.py:57-67). Only the first three trigger reuse (:742-762); FAILED does not appear on the reuse path (no branch reads it). Token reservation terminal states: SETTLED and RELEASED. AMBIGUOUS remains counted against the cap until an operator reconciles it and is treated as a live hold in `_LIVE_TOKEN_STATUSES` (strategies/research_budget.py:127). Pre-flight budget decision terminal labels: SEND_TO_RESEARCH, SKIP_LOW_SIGNAL, SKIP_DAILY_CANDIDATE_CAP, SKIP_DAILY_TOKEN_CAP, and post-hoc REUSE_RESEARCH / RUN_FULL_RESEARCH (strategies/research_budget.py:30-37).
- **Fail open or closed.** fail-closed
- **File refs.** `src/trading_research/strategies/research_budget.py:1-536`, `src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:214-236`, `src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:691-762`, `src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:172-181`, `src/trading_research/research/orchestration.py:352-673`, `src/trading_research/research/token_budget.py:1-97`, `src/trading_research/research/scheduled_cycle.py:507-546`, `src/trading_research/cli.py:820-846`, `src/trading_research/storage/research_repositories.py:146-277`, `src/trading_research/storage/shadow_operations_schema.py:62-101`, `src/trading_research/storage/shadow_operations_repositories.py:88-142`, `src/trading_research/storage/transactions.py:26-74`, `src/trading_research/strategies/config.py:117-138`, `config/strategies.yaml:62-64`
- **Suspected defects.**
  - `decide_research_action`/`record_research_outcome` (strategies/research_budget.py:62-98) are not referenced from any file under src/ - only from tests/unit/test_research_budget.py. Production callers (`research/scheduled_cycle.py:516`, `cli.py:833`) go straight to `analyze_with_research_committee` without the pre-flight shortlist gate, and no code persists the REUSE_RESEARCH vs RUN_FULL_RESEARCH label the module was designed to produce (Grep confirms zero non-test hits). NeedsVerification whether another module reintroduces the daily candidate/token pre-flight caps outside this trace.
  - `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController` (research/token_budget.py:22-97) has no production instantiation; `Grep` for the class name across src/ returns only its own definition, and no call site in `cli.py` or `research/scheduled_cycle.py` sets `token_budget_controller=`. `analyze_with_research_committee` therefore runs with `token_budget_controller=None` in production (research/orchestration.py:445-449), meaning the persistent daily token cap in `strategies/research_budget.py` is only enforced from tests. NeedsVerification whether an alternative wiring lives outside src/.
  - Reuse-decision race: `get_run_status` (storage/research_repositories.py:152-156) is a plain SELECT and `save_run_started` uses `INSERT OR IGNORE` with an autocommit `self._conn.commit()` (:177-183). Two concurrent workers targeting the same deterministic `research_run_id` can both observe status=None (research/orchestration.py:741) and both proceed to full orchestration, each reserving tokens for the same identity. Only per-attempt idempotency_key uniqueness prevents duplicate reservations for the same (run, role, attempt_number) - but distinct workers can still increment through independent attempt sequences and both consume budget.
  - `reserve_research_tokens` reads the full reservation history on every call: `_live_tokens_reserved_for_date` (strategies/research_budget.py:203-212), `_date_has_budget_breach` (:215-221), and the AMBIGUOUS-prefix scan (:296-302) all pull `list_budget_reservations(conn)` without a status/date filter (storage/shadow_operations_repositories.py:105-112 uses no WHERE clause when `status is None`). Performance degrades linearly with the total table size across the whole deployment lifetime, and the row-history scan runs inside every `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction. Correctness is fine; efficiency and lock-contention risk grow over time.
  - AMBIGUOUS lockout on ordinary retryable failures: in `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController.invoke` (research/token_budget.py:72-77) any BaseException other than `ProviderUnavailableError` marks the reservation AMBIGUOUS - including `ProviderRateLimitError`, `ProviderTimeoutError`, `MalformedOutputError`, `SchemaValidationError`, which the orchestrator itself classifies as retryable (`_RETRYABLE_ERRORS`, research/orchestration.py:69). Because the next attempt reserves a new idempotency_key but is rejected by the prefix scan (strategies/research_budget.py:292-309 -> `ProviderUnavailableError(retryable=False)`), a single transient provider error blocks the entire role and requires manual operator reconciliation. This is fail-closed but very aggressive for issues the orchestrator would normally retry - NeedsVerification against the operational runbook.
  - `_conservative_input_allowance` (research/token_budget.py:31-37) uses `len(system_prompt.encode()) + len(user_prompt.encode()) + len(str(json_schema).encode())` (UTF-8 byte count) as the reserved input-token estimate. Bytes materially over-estimate tokens for typical prompts, so the daily cap is consumed faster than actual usage would justify - callers can hit BUDGET_EXHAUSTED long before actual settled tokens approach the cap. Fail-closed but potentially wasteful; NeedsVerification that no downstream tokenizer refinement exists.
  - No reaper or timeout for stale RESERVED rows. If `PersistentResearchTokenBudgetController.invoke` is bypassed (e.g., process crash between `reserve_research_tokens` and either `release`/`settle`/`mark_ambiguous`), the RESERVED row remains counted as live (strategies/research_budget.py:127 `_LIVE_TOKEN_STATUSES`). `_live_tokens_reserved_for_date` (:203-212) will keep charging it against the daily cap until an operator manually intervenes; no automatic reconciliation path exists. Compare with `shadow/budget.py:365-378` which does release stale RESEARCH_COST rows - the RESEARCH_TOKENS path has no equivalent.
  - `reserve_research_tokens` policy consistency guard treats missing `token_accounting_policy` as `NOT_APPLICABLE` when comparing an existing row (:283-290). If a row was inserted before the `token_accounting_policy` column existed (schema migration path, storage/shadow_operations_schema.py:208-221), a compatibility check with any non-NOT_APPLICABLE caller policy will raise TOKEN_ACCOUNTING_POLICY_MISMATCH, which the controller does not catch (research/token_budget.py has no handler for TokenBudgetError from reserve). NeedsVerification that migrations always backfill this column.

#### External paper submission (isolated Alpaca-paper account) is the "external" execution namespace inside the paper_books module. It is orchestrated in-process by the trading_research host via a Protocol-defined runtime client (ExternalPaperRuntime) that speaks normalized JSON to the isolated `trading_paper_runtime` sibling process. The end-to-end flow is:

1. Operator preview (`preview_external_paper_order`, src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:918-985)
   - Validates config/book (line 486-499), loads and freezes intent (_intent, 502-582): checks terminal-state exclusivity (519-524), execution-namespace claim (530-535), book ACTIVE (537-538), risk/exit decision approval (539-552), order type/TIF (553-556), whole-share quantity/side (557-561), notional recomputation and caps (_validate_frozen_notional, 142-173), timestamp freshness (563-575), oversell against confirmed available position (576-580).
   - Runs `_safety_checks` (585-607): shadow pause not blocking, no unresolved CRITICAL alert, no active-critical external reconciliation (unless explicit retry evidence).
   - Derives immutable `client_order_id` = "epb-{book_id}-" + sha256(book_id, intent_id, symbol, side, qty, limit_price, POLICY_VERSION) (derive_external_order_identity, 431-444). This is the durable idempotency key for the whole flow.
   - Acquires order-scope lease via `_order_lease` (865-916, backed by acquire_external_order_lease at storage/paper_books_repositories.py:2035-2062).
   - Fenced write: claim EXECUTION_NAMESPACE_EXTERNAL (940-947), verify runtime account_check + fingerprint history (610-623, 759-774), call runtime.preview_limit_order, persist paper_external_order_previews row + append event STATE_PREVIEWED atomically (978-984).

2. Operator submit (`submit_external_paper_order`, 1319-1366)
   - Re-validates config/book/intent, runs `_safety_checks` (before lease acquisition; not re-run inside lease).
   - Acquires order-scope lease, runs account_check, verifies fingerprint history, requires reconciliation baseline (_require_reconciliation_baseline, 729-745).
   - `_validated_preview` (988-1008): confirms an APPROVED preview exists, matches book/intent/client_order_id/payload_hash/fingerprint, not expired, matching config_hash.
   - Idempotency guard on the event chain (1340-1348): if current state is UNKNOWN raises AMBIGUOUS_SUBMISSION; if current state is not PREVIEWED, performs `runtime.get_order_by_client_order_id` and returns the existing state rather than resubmitting.
   - Reservation (attempt_number=0): `_reserve_daily_notional` (230-344) opens its own BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction (per docstring 253-260), reserves account-wide daily notional against `paper_external_attempt_reservations` (storage/paper_books_schema.py:937-948) whose PK is a deterministic hash of (client_order_id, attempt_number, account_fingerprint, reservation_date). Sums existing non-released reservations against cfg.external_broker.maximum_daily_notional_usd and inserts a RESERVED row.
   - `_submit_once` (1188-1286):
     a) Under `lease.fenced_write`, atomically records the cash/share reservation (cash_ledger.reserve_for_order or positions.reserve_shares_for_sell) plus the STATE_SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event (Milestone 11.3 Part 36/37 comment at 1199-1209).
     b) Calls `runtime.submit_limit_order` OUTSIDE any fenced_write.
     c) On success: validates response shape (_validate_order_response, 1118-1168), maps status via `_state_from_order`, fenced_write appends the new event, updates paper_book_orders status, and settles the reservation via `_settle_daily_reservation` (347-374). Then runs `apply_external_fills` (1369-1543) inside a `_fenced_or_plain_write`, applying fills to paper_external_broker_fills + paper_book_fills + cash_ledger + positions + lifecycle rows; releases remaining reservation if fully filled.
     d) On runtime exception (RuntimeUnavailableError/Timeout/OperationError, or any Exception): calls `_record_unknown` (1171-1185) which appends STATE_UNKNOWN and moves the reservation to RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED (never RELEASED — the hold on the daily cap is retained). Returns status=UNKNOWN.
   - On non-UNKNOWN outcome (1360-1365) calls `_reconcile_locked` post-submit to snapshot broker positions/account, validate fills, and mark any critical discrepancy.

3. Recovery / retry
   - `recover_stranded_submission` (1635-1696): read-only broker lookup path for chains stuck at STATE_SUBMISSION_REQUESTED after a crash; delegates to `_reconcile_locked`, which bridges to STATE_UNKNOWN via `_bridge_stranded_submission_requested` (2024-2038, 2263-2273).
   - `retry_external_paper_order` (1546-1632): requires current=STATE_UNKNOWN plus a fresh unconsumed authoritative NOT_FOUND lookup matching the exact ambiguous event/attempt/payload/fingerprint (1561-1575); enforces retry limit; re-validates preview; loads prior reservation and calls `_reserve_daily_notional` with supersede args (atomic supersede-of-prior + creation-of-new inside one transaction). Consumes the lookup after the new event is appended (1623-1626).
   - `refresh_retry_preview` (1699-1768): pure-DB preview refresh — no runtime call.

4. Reconciliation (`reconcile_external_paper_order` / `_reconcile_locked` / `_run_reconciliation`, 1925-2282) is the authoritative repair path: takes the same order-scope lease, saves a paper_external_order_lookups row with authoritative flag = (order is None and "UNKNOWN" not in statuses), transitions the reservation via `_reconcile_attempt_reservation` (377-420) to SUBMITTED/RELEASED/RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED per broker outcome, and always persists a `paper_external_reconciliations` row (2285-2303) whose `critical` flag drives BLOCKED_BY_RECONCILIATION queue status.

- **Entry point.** CLI `external-paper-submit` -> `external_paper_submit_cli` in src/trading_research/cli.py:361-370 -> `submit_external_paper_order` in src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1319-1366. The preview counterpart is `preview_external_paper_order` (918-985). Recovery entry points: `recover_stranded_submission` (1635-1696), `retry_external_paper_order` (1546-1632), `reconcile_external_paper_order` (1925-1956), `refresh_retry_preview` (1699-1768). Runtime IPC boundary: `RuntimeClient.preview_limit_order`/`submit_limit_order`/`get_order_by_client_order_id`/`cancel_external_order`/`list_order_fills` in src/trading_research/runtime/client/process_client.py:362-395.
- **Authoritative validation.** Multiple layered, DB-anchored checks — all applied on the trading_research side, not delegated to the isolated runtime: - Config/book gating: `_require_external_config` (external_broker.py:486-499) enforces paper_books.enabled, book enabled, exactly one book mapped to the account, allow_order_submission=True on submit path. - Intent freshness/authenticity: `_intent` (502-582) rejects terminal local status, non-EXTERNAL namespace claim, non-ACTIVE book, missing/mismatched approved risk decision (or matching partial-exit decision for SELL), unsupported order type / TIF, non-whole-share quantity, wrong side, future/stale timestamps, oversell. - Frozen notional integrity: `_validate_frozen_notional` (142-173) recomputes qty*limit_price and requires it to match `notional_usd`, the approved risk decision's notional (on BUY), and the strictest configured cap. - Idempotent identity: `derive_external_order_identity` (431-444) — sha256 of immutable intent fields, hex-truncated, regex-validated broker-safe. - Chain integrity: `_TRANSITIONS` (64-86) enforces state-machine legality; `_append_event` (451-483) uses INSERT OR IGNORE with monotonic `scope_sequence` protected by unique index `uq_paper_external_events_scope_sequence` (paper_books_schema.py:1069-1070) — a forked chain is rejected at the DB level (`EVENT_CHAIN_CONFLICT`). - Preview binding at submit: `_validated_preview` (988-1008) requires exact scope match, not-expired, matching config_hash. - Account authenticity: `_account_check` (610-623) requires provider=alpaca_paper + environment=paper + verified fingerprint prefix; `_verify_fingerprint_history` (759-774) refuses fingerprint drift and cross-book mapping of the same fingerprint. - Baseline requirement: `_require_reconciliation_baseline` (729-745) fails closed at submission if no operator-activated baseline exists (RECONCILIATION_BASELINE_MISSING). - Runtime response shape: `_validate_order_response` (1118-1168) requires exact field set + provider/env/fingerprint/book/symbol/side/qty/limit_price match, TIF=DAY, whole-share qty, filled_qty within approved, tz-aware timestamps not in future, submitted<=updated. - Safety gating: `_safety_checks` (585-607) rejects when shadow safety pause is blocking, unresolved CRITICAL alert exists, or the latest external reconciliation for the scope is critical (with a narrow exception path for confirmed-NOT_FOUND retries). - Daily notional budget: `_reserve_daily_notional` (230-344) — atomic BEGIN IMMEDIATE, deterministic reservation_id, active-reservation uniqueness index `idx_paper_external_attempt_one_active` (schema 1101-1105).
- **Idempotency key.** Two composed keys anchor idempotency: 1. `client_order_id` = `epb-{book_id_lower}-` + first 32 hex of sha256(book_id, paper_order_intent_id, symbol, side, quantity, limit_price, POLICY_VERSION). Source: `derive_external_order_identity`, external_broker.py:431-444. Used as the durable order identity across every operation (preview, submit, retry, cancel, reconcile, lookup, lease, reservation). Uniquely indexed via lease PK (lease_key = f"{book_id}:{client_order_id}") and the scope-sequence unique index above. 2. `reservation_id` = "resvn_" + first 40 hex of sha256(client_order_id, attempt_number, account_fingerprint, reservation_date). Source: `derive_external_attempt_reservation_id`, storage/paper_books_schema.py:1571-1585. Primary key of `paper_external_attempt_reservations`; a same-day/same-attempt replay reuses the existing row rather than creating a duplicate (external_broker.py:266-273). Runtime IPC also carries a per-call `runtime_request_id` (`m11_<uuid4>`, e.g. external_broker.py:1192) that is persisted on each event row; this is a correlation ID, not an idempotency key.
- **Transaction boundary.** Multiple nested/sequential transaction scopes: - `_reserve_daily_notional` (external_broker.py:230-344) opens its own `with transaction(conn)` (line 265, executes BEGIN IMMEDIATE via storage.database.begin_immediate) — deliberately NOT nested inside the lease's fenced_write (docstring 253-260). This is a separately-committed transaction that runs the read-then-write daily-cap check and (optionally) supersedes the prior reservation in the same commit. - `_submit_once`'s reservation+SUBMISSION_REQUESTED write (1210-1226) is a single `lease.fenced_write()` transaction — BEGIN IMMEDIATE followed by verify_or_raise + reservation+event insert + COMMIT. Milestone 11.3 Part 36/37 documents that these two writes are now atomic (previously two independently-committed writes). - The broker submit call (`runtime.submit_limit_order`, line 1228) runs OUTSIDE any transaction (network I/O must never occur under BEGIN IMMEDIATE, per `_order_lease` docstring 891-892). - Post-response commit (1241-1253): another `lease.fenced_write()` bundles the terminal-or-transitional event, `update_order_status`, and `_settle_daily_reservation`. - `apply_external_fills` (1369-1543) uses one `_fenced_or_plain_write` per fill (1454-1484), one per state transition, and one for terminal reservation release. - Lease acquire/heartbeat/release each self-commit (paper_books_repositories.py:2055, 2077, 2121).
- **Lease or concurrency.** Order-scope lease keyed by `{book_id}:{client_order_id}` in `paper_external_order_leases` (paper_books_repositories.py:2035-2062). Semantics: - `acquire_external_order_lease`: single conditional INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE that only reclaims when the prior lease is inactive or expired; returns the new fencing `generation` (or None). - Default TTL 45s, heartbeat 10s (external_broker.py:782-783); configurable via `cfg.external_broker.order_lease_ttl_seconds`. - `OrderLeaseHandle` (791-863) reads a fresh clock on every heartbeat/verify (docstring 796-804), fails closed on `heartbeat_or_raise`/`verify_or_raise` when generation was bumped or the lease expired (raises `OrderLeaseLostError`). - `fenced_write` (846-863) opens BEGIN IMMEDIATE and calls `verify_or_raise` inside the write transaction before yielding, so takeover cannot interleave with the protected writes; unhandled exceptions roll back. - Every write path (preview, submit, retry, recover, cancel, reconcile, refresh) is wrapped in `_order_lease` (875-915) which releases on `finally` with the acquisition generation. - DB-level defense-in-depth: unique `scope_sequence` per (book_id, client_order_id) (paper_books_schema.py:1069-1070) rejects a forked event chain even if the lease were bypassed (`EVENT_CHAIN_CONFLICT` at external_broker.py:479-482). - Reservations have their own concurrency protection: attempt-scoped PK + partial unique index `idx_paper_external_attempt_one_active` (paper_books_schema.py:1101-1105) forbids two active rows per (client_order_id, attempt_number, account_fingerprint, book_id).
- **State transitions.** Order-event chain (external_broker.py:64-86): - NOT_SUBMITTED -> PREVIEWED (preview_external_paper_order writes; only allowed transition from unseen) - PREVIEWED -> SUBMISSION_REQUESTED (submit_external_paper_order.`_submit_once` pre-broker checkpoint) - SUBMISSION_REQUESTED -> {SUBMITTED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED, UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_RECONCILIATION} - SUBMITTED -> {PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, CANCEL_REQUESTED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED, UNKNOWN} - PARTIALLY_FILLED -> {FILLED, CANCEL_REQUESTED, CANCELLED, UNKNOWN} - CANCEL_REQUESTED -> {CANCELLED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, UNKNOWN} - UNKNOWN -> {SUBMITTED, PARTIALLY_FILLED, FILLED, CANCEL_REQUESTED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, EXPIRED, SUBMISSION_REQUESTED} (recovery/retry re-entry) - FILLED/CANCELLED/REJECTED/EXPIRED are absorbing. Reservation chain (_ATTEMPT_RESERVATION_TRANSITION_SOURCES in paper_books_repositories.py:28-33): - RESERVED -> SUBMITTED (broker acknowledged) - RESERVED -> RELEASED (only when broker terminally REJECTED) - RESERVED -> RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED (ambiguous outcome) - RESERVED/RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED -> SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY (cross-day retry consumes prior) - RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED -> {SUBMITTED, RELEASED, SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY} (reconciliation resolves ambiguity).
- **Persistent records.** DDL: src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py; repositories: src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py. - `paper_external_order_events` (event chain; save_external_order_event, repo:1488-1501): full audit — previous_state, new_state, payload_hash, quantity, limit_price, operator, reason, runtime_request_id, error_code, config_hash, attempt_number, scope_sequence. - `paper_external_order_previews` (per-preview record; save_external_preview): preview_id, expires_at, config_hash, reasons, policy_version. - `paper_external_attempt_reservations` (attempt-scoped daily notional; schema 937-948): reservation_id, client_order_id, attempt_number, account_fingerprint, reservation_date, book_id, reserved_notional_usd, state, timestamps. Row is immutable except state/updated_at (trigger 1434-1443). - `paper_external_daily_reservations` (legacy; deprecated 917-928): kept for rolling-upgrade compat; backfilled by `_backfill_active_legacy_external_reservations` (schema 1588-1632); state kept in sync by `_sync_legacy_attempt_zero_reservation` (repo:1886+). - `paper_external_order_leases` (order-scope lease with fencing generation, repo:2035-2122). - `paper_external_broker_fills` (external fill audit, save_external_broker_fill via apply_external_fills 1454-1456). - `paper_book_fills` (local canonical fills bridged from external fills, save_fill 1457). - `paper_book_orders` status column updated via repo.update_order_status (1248-1250, 1503-1505, 1862-1863). - Cash ledger (`paper_book_cash_ledger` + reservations) via cash_ledger.reserve_for_order/settle_buy/release_settled_buy_reservation/release_remaining_buy_reservation. - Position ledger (`paper_book_positions` + `paper_book_position_lots` + reservation events) via positions.reserve_shares_for_sell / consume_share_reservation_for_fill / release_remaining_share_reservation. - `paper_external_order_lookups` (authoritative NOT_FOUND evidence; save_external_lookup at line 2083-2095) with `consumed_by_retry_event_id` set only by an actual retry. - `paper_external_reconciliations` (save_external_reconciliation via `_persist_reconciliation` 2285-2303) — critical flag drives queue BLOCKED_BY_RECONCILIATION. - `paper_book_position_lifecycle_states` + `paper_book_lifecycle_state_events` (repo.save_position_lifecycle_state / save_lifecycle_state_event 1530-1542) on SELL exit-stage completion.
- **External side effects.** Only through the injected `ExternalPaperRuntime` protocol (external_broker.py:89-98), implemented by `RuntimeClient` (src/trading_research/runtime/client/process_client.py) which speaks JSON-line RPC to a subprocess (paper_runtime). Broker network calls happen exclusively via that client: - account_check (610-623) — read-only. - preview_limit_order (952-954) — Alpaca preflight (broker-side read, no order created). - submit_limit_order (1228) — the one and only external order creation call; never called under a fenced_write / DB transaction; never retried by the client (process_client.py:330-335, 368-370 docstrings). - get_order_by_client_order_id (1345, 1345 recon path) — read-only broker lookup. - cancel_external_order (1803-1805). - list_order_fills (1375), list_recent_external_orders (1048), get_external_positions/get_external_account_snapshot (692-693, 2198-2199) — read-only. The runtime process is credentialed and paper-scoped; the main process never sees Alpaca credentials.
- **Failure behavior.** Fail-closed by explicit design: every ambiguous, unrecognized, or storage-error condition raises `ExternalPaperError` and/or persists critical reconciliation evidence; broker resubmission never happens without a gated retry backed by unconsumed authoritative NOT_FOUND evidence; a lost lease immediately aborts the operation; a fill-sweep failure blocks reservation release and terminal-status advancement.
- **Retry behavior.** Retries are strictly gated: - The RuntimeClient never internally retries submit_order/submit_limit_order (process_client.py:330-335, 368-370 explicit docstrings). - `submit_external_paper_order` refuses to resubmit on an existing chain in UNKNOWN state (raises AMBIGUOUS_SUBMISSION at 1341-1342). For SUBMITTED/PARTIALLY_FILLED states it performs a read-only get_order and returns that; it does not submit again. - Retry is a separate operation (`retry_external_paper_order`, 1546-1632). Preconditions: (a) current state == UNKNOWN; (b) a fresh, unconsumed authoritative NOT_FOUND lookup that matches ambiguous_event_id/attempt_number/payload_hash/account_fingerprint/client_order_id/book_id (1562-1571); (c) safety checks pass under the confirmed-not-found exemption; (d) `retries < cfg.external_broker.maximum_retry_attempts`; (e) unchanged account fingerprint; (f) baseline still valid; (g) a fresh valid preview. - Retry creates a NEW reservation (new attempt_number) inside a single atomic transaction that also supersedes the prior reservation (`supersede_reservation_id`, external_broker.py:294-327, atomic per docstring 246-260). Consumes the authoritative lookup only after the new event is durably appended (1623-1626).
- **Recovery behavior.** - `recover_stranded_submission` (1635-1696): explicit read-only recovery for chains stuck at SUBMISSION_REQUESTED after a crash. Never calls submit_limit_order or preview_limit_order; only calls get_order_by_client_order_id (via `_reconcile_locked`). Idempotent — a second call sees the chain already advanced and returns `already_recovered=True` (1690-1692). Safe from a fresh DB connection. - `_bridge_stranded_submission_requested` (2024-2038, 2263-2273): appends UNKNOWN when broker lookup produces no validated definitive state, using the ORIGINAL SUBMISSION_REQUESTED event's payload_hash and attempt_number so the resulting UNKNOWN carries the correct scope; this makes the existing retry gate reachable. - `refresh_retry_preview` (1699-1768): pure DB — replaces an expired preview for an order confirmed UNKNOWN with a fresh authoritative NOT_FOUND lookup. No broker call. Requires same NOT_FOUND-lookup preconditions and retry-limit check as retry. - `reconcile_external_paper_order` (1925-1956) is the general repair path. Every reconciliation always writes a `paper_external_reconciliations` row (2285-2303) and the reservation is re-driven to the correct state via `_reconcile_attempt_reservation` (377-420), which will not downgrade exposure (never releases an already-SUBMITTED reservation, 405-408). - Rolling-upgrade recovery: `apply_paper_books_schema` (schema 1676-1692) backfills legacy `paper_external_daily_reservations` rows into `paper_external_attempt_reservations` under BEGIN IMMEDIATE, enforcing lineage uniqueness at migration time.
- **Terminal states.** Order-event chain terminal (absorbing) states: STATE_FILLED, STATE_CANCELLED, STATE_REJECTED, STATE_EXPIRED (external_broker.py:52; _TRANSITIONS maps them to empty sets, 85). A terminal local `paper_book_orders.status` in any of these forbids all future preview/submit/retry (`_intent`, 519-524). Reservation terminal states: RELEASED, SUBMITTED, SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY (SUBMITTED is not "terminal" for accounting — it still counts against cap until RELEASED via terminal broker outcome or fully-filled path). Queue-display terminal outputs: FILLED/CANCELLED/REJECTED/EXPIRED are immutable once reached; a non-terminal order with an active critical reconciliation surfaces as BLOCKED_BY_RECONCILIATION (`derive_external_queue_status`, 1876-1897).
- **Fail open or closed.** fail-closed
- **File refs.** `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1-2304`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/cli.py:361-370`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:910-948`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:1062-1105`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:1434-1443`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_schema.py:1566-1692`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:19-33`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1488-1548`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:1733-1900`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/storage/paper_books_repositories.py:2035-2122`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/src/trading_research/runtime/client/process_client.py:300-400`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/paper_runtime/src/trading_paper_runtime/lumibot_gateway.py:168`, `C:/workspace/other/ai_model/paper_runtime/src/trading_paper_runtime/deterministic_gateway.py:69`
- **Suspected defects.**
  - `submit_external_paper_order` runs `_safety_checks` (external_broker.py:1327) BEFORE acquiring the order-scope lease at 1329, and does NOT re-run it inside the lease. A new CRITICAL alert or shadow safety-pause becoming blocking between the two calls will not stop this in-flight submit; only the durable state gates (event chain / reservation) protect the subsequent write. NeedsVerification: confirm whether that race is intentionally tolerated or whether Milestone 11.3.1 Item 4-style fencing was intended to include a re-check.
  - `submit_external_paper_order` at 1345-1347 raises `ORDER_MISSING_AT_BROKER` when the local chain already advanced past PREVIEWED but the broker lookup returns None. Unlike the reconciliation path, this raise does not itself persist a `paper_external_reconciliations` critical row for the caller; the invariant that 'no ambiguous outcome exits without persisted critical evidence' (Part 8 comment at 1963-1969) is upheld only when reconciliation is reached. NeedsVerification: whether the caller is required to invoke `reconcile_external_paper_order` after this raise or whether the bare raise leaves the system without recorded reconciliation evidence for this particular condition.
  - `_reserve_daily_notional` (230-344) commits its own BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction (line 265) SEPARATE from `_submit_once`'s fenced_write reservation+SUBMISSION_REQUESTED transaction. If the process crashes between the reservation commit and the SUBMISSION_REQUESTED commit, an active RESERVED row exists with no matching event chain past PREVIEWED. Same-day re-invocation of `submit_external_paper_order` self-heals (existing reservation is reused at 267-273), but cross-day retry with a different reservation_date would require an explicit supersede path that only `retry_external_paper_order` provides — and that path is gated on the chain being at UNKNOWN, which this stranded state is not. NeedsVerification: whether any operational path exists to recover such an orphan reservation without operator DB intervention.
  - `_reconcile_attempt_reservation` (377-420) raises `RESERVATION_STATE_CONFLICT` when the latest reservation is in a state that does not equal the target and there is no active reservation. If a crash occurs between a retry's atomic supersede+new-reservation commit and the new attempt's SUBMISSION_REQUESTED write, the current event chain still points at the OLD attempt_number whose reservation is now SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY; a subsequent reconciliation loading against the old attempt_number will find latest.state=SUPERSEDED_BY_RETRY, target != that state, and raise. NeedsVerification: whether this raises `RECONCILIATION_INTERNAL_ERROR` (persisted critical status via `_reconcile_locked`) or leaves reconciliation permanently blocked.
  - `_submit_once` at 1284-1285 unconditionally calls `_release_terminal_reservation` under a fenced_write even for non-terminal states (e.g. SUBMITTED, PARTIALLY_FILLED); the helper (1294-1316) safely no-ops on non-terminal states, but the fenced_write still opens a BEGIN IMMEDIATE / commit cycle to do nothing. Minor efficiency, not correctness — flag only for cleanup consideration.
  - `_verify_fingerprint_history` (759-774) executes raw SQL on the shared connection outside any explicit transaction; in the middle of preview/submit its reads are consistent because the outer lease's fenced_write has not yet begun, but a concurrent writer altering `paper_external_order_events.account_fingerprint` between this read and the subsequent fenced_write is only defended by the order-scope lease keyed by `{book_id}:{client_order_id}`. A different concurrent client_order_id for the same book/account could still write an event between the two operations. NeedsVerification: whether book-scope (not just order-scope) protection is required for the fingerprint-history invariant.

## 7. Invariant enforcement matrix

| Invariant | Enforcement | Status | Bypass paths | Tests | Definition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Research-only by default | authoritative-boundary | ENFORCED | None found; os.environ never read for trading_mode/live_trading_enabled (proved by tests/unit/test_live_gateway.py:131-139) | tests/unit/test_live_gateway.py:35-158; tests/unit/test_paper_books_config.py | config/execution.yaml:12-13; config/paper_books.yaml:13-14; config/scheduled_research.yaml:9-10 |
| No options | authoritative-boundary | ENFORCED |  | tests/unit/test_live_gateway.py:152-157; tests/unit/test_external_paper_broker.py; tests/unit/test_paper_books_risk_and_intent.py | src/trading_research/paper_books/config.py:41; src/trading_research/paper_books/models.py:70-71 |

- **Research-only by default** - src/trading_research/execution/config.py:43-52 raises ExecutionConfigError if trading_mode is not 'paper' or live_trading_enabled=true. VALID_TRADING_MODES = ('paper',) so anything else fails closed. src/trading_research/execution/live_gateway.py:93-108 ships only DisabledLiveExecutionGateway; every method raises LiveTradingDisabledError unconditionally. paper_books.enabled defaults false in config/paper_books.yaml:14 and paper_books/config.py:1017-1024 validates it strictly.
- **No options** - src/trading_research/paper_books/models.py:266-267 rejects any non-LIMIT order_type in PaperBookOrderIntent.__post_init__. src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:553-554 rejects any intent whose order_type != 'LIMIT' or where 'limit' not in permitted_order_types (ORDER_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED). No options ('CALL','PUT','option', 'contract_type') references exist anywhere in execution/, paper_books/, or live_gateway.py. tests/unit/test_live_gateway.py:152-157 asserts place_option_order / cancel_option_order absent from live_gateway source.
- **No margin** - Order sizing in src/trading_research/paper_books/risk.py:164-166 caps orders at available_cash_usd - (nlv * minimum_cash_buffer_weight) — pure cash-based sizing, no buying-power/leverage concept anywhere. Cash reservation on submission (paper_books/external_broker.py:1211-1215 -> cash_ledger.reserve_for_order) locks settled cash before the broker call. No 'buying_power' field is consumed for risk decisions.
- **No shorting** - src/trading_research/execution/models.py:82-84 raises unless side == 'BUY' (execution/ pipeline is long-only). src/trading_research/execution/intent_builder.py:90 hard-codes side=ORDER_SIDE_BUY. src/trading_research/paper_books/order_intent.py:53 hard-codes side=ORDER_SIDE_BUY. src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:560-561 rejects any side not in ('BUY','SELL'); 576-580 rejects OVERSELL (SELL > confirmed long position). src/trading_research/paper_books/positions.py:83-86 raises InsufficientPositionError before oversell.
- **Limit orders only** - External paper submission is strictly LIMIT-only: src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:553-554 (ORDER_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED) and paper_books/models.py:266-267. HOWEVER src/trading_research/execution/models.py:24-25 still defines ORDER_TYPE_MARKET and src/trading_research/execution/intent_builder.py:92-93 builds PaperOrderIntent with order_type=ORDER_TYPE_MARKET for the deterministic in-process simulator (services/execute_paper_recommendation.py). That path only reaches DeterministicPaperAdapter, never an external venue, so no MARKET order can escape — but the 'limit orders only' invariant is not enforced at that internal execution boundary.
- **Maximum single trade: $50 (external paper)** - src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:172-173 (_validate_frozen_notional): recomputed quantity*limit_price is compared against min(cfg.risk.max_order_notional_usd, cfg.external_broker.maximum_order_notional_usd) and raises EXTERNAL_NOTIONAL_LIMIT otherwise. Invoked from _intent (line 562), which is on every external preview/submit/retry entry point (preview line 924, submit line 1326, retry line 1553). paper_books/config.py:155-162 requires maximum_order_notional_usd>0 and maximum_daily_notional_usd>=maximum_order_notional_usd.
- **Maximum external-paper submitted notional per UTC day: $150** - src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:230-344 _reserve_daily_notional: atomic per-(account_fingerprint, UTC date, attempt_number) reservation under BEGIN IMMEDIATE + transaction; sums outstanding reservations for the day and raises EXTERNAL_DAILY_NOTIONAL_LIMIT if adding this order would exceed cfg.external_broker.maximum_daily_notional_usd (lines 328-335). Reservation date derived from now.astimezone(timezone.utc).date() (line 261) so UTC is fixed and cannot be manipulated by client-locale time. STATE_UNKNOWN ambiguous submissions retain their hold (RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED, lines 361-367) preventing budget loss on ambiguity. Cross-day retry supersedes atomically (lines 294-327). Reservation is invoked before every submission attempt (external_broker.py:1349-1352).
- **Maximum ticker allocation: 10% of the Agentic account** - src/trading_research/paper_books/risk.py:156-160 computes max_position_notional = net_liquidation_value_usd * risk_config.max_position_weight and max_symbol_notional similarly; applied to every paper risk decision in evaluate_paper_risk (paper_books/risk.py:69-191); returns RISK_REJECTED_MAX_POSITION_WEIGHT / RISK_REJECTED_MAX_SYMBOL_CONCENTRATION when binding (lines 167-181). paper_books/config.py:228-232 fails closed if weight outside [0,1]. Pre-research filter (strategies/selector.py:48-79) uses 0.08 (config/strategies.yaml:72) leaving the 10% authoritative cap intact at execution time.
- **Strategy candidate selection disabled by default** - config/strategies.yaml:19-20 ships enabled=false, activation_stage=STAGE_2_HISTORICAL_BACKTEST. src/trading_research/strategies/config.py:185-190 raises StrategyConfigError if enabled=true while stage is STAGE_1 or STAGE_2 — so promoting requires both a config edit and a stage advancement. HOWEVER the loader exposes StrategyConfig.strategy_candidate_selection_enabled (strategies/config.py:146,229) but grep shows no production caller in src/ reads this flag — src/trading_research/strategies/selector.py:82 select_shortlist has no 'if enabled' guard. Only test files (tests/unit/test_strategy_config_activation.py) reference the flag. The invariant is currently enforced only by the config-loader stage-gate; select_shortlist itself is unguarded (though no production code path currently invokes it).
- **Automated paper submission disabled by default** - src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:486-499 _require_external_config raises PAPER_BOOKS_DISABLED / EXTERNAL_BROKER_DISABLED / BOOK_NOT_EXTERNAL_ENABLED / ACCOUNT_ISOLATION_INVALID / SUBMISSION_DISABLED when any relevant gate is false; called from every external preview/submit/retry entry point. submit_external_paper_order (external_broker.py:1325) invokes with submission=True checking allow_order_submission (line 498-499). Config loader (paper_books/config.py:181-184) fails closed if allow_order_submission=true while external_broker.enabled=false. Config-level cross-validation (paper_books/config.py:552-558) enforces external_broker requires local_simulated execution provider.
- **Scheduler disabled by default** - src/trading_research/paper_books/recurring_scheduler.py:894-902 returns STATUS_SKIPPED_INACTIVE when paper_books_config.recurring.enabled is false or activation state is not ACTIVE. src/trading_research/shadow/scheduler.py:642-644 returns STATUS_DISABLED when shadow_operations.enabled is false. No cron/launchd/BackgroundScheduler registration exists in the codebase — grep for 'apscheduler|BackgroundScheduler|cron' turns up only comments; the 'scheduler' can only be advanced by an explicit CLI/service invocation. Activation additionally requires an operator activation review and explicit activation event (paper_books/config.py:33-48 comments; recurring_scheduler activation states line 41-53).
- **Live trading disabled by default** - src/trading_research/execution/live_gateway.py:20-32 and 93-108: DisabledLiveExecutionGateway is the ONLY LiveExecutionGateway implementation — every method raises LiveTradingDisabledError unconditionally; no bypass flag or constructor argument. execution/config.py:49-52 raises if live_trading_enabled=true. execution/config.py:22 VALID_TRADING_MODES = ('paper',) so 'live' is not a recognized value. paper_books/config.py:99-103 ExecutionSection.__post_init__ raises if allow_live_broker=true. tests/unit/test_live_gateway.py:131-139 proves env vars for TRADING_MODE/LIVE_TRADING_ENABLED are never consulted.
- **Required approval phrase (Live): 'Approved, place this exact order.'** - grep across all src/ finds no occurrence of the literal live-approval phrase or any regex/constant tied to it. The only 'Approved' hit in src/ is the ApprovedOrder dataclass in src/trading_research/execution/live_gateway.py — unrelated to phrase enforcement. This is not a functional bypass because live trading is structurally unreachable (DisabledLiveExecutionGateway is the only implementation and every method raises LiveTradingDisabledError), so no live code path exists to check a phrase against. Recorded MISSING at the code layer: enforced only implicitly by the total absence of any live path.
- **Required approval phrase (Paper): 'Approved, submit this exact paper order.'** - The literal phrase appears only in docs (grep). External paper submission requires an 'operator' string and a 'reason' string, but src/trading_research/paper_books/external_broker.py:1324 (submit_external_paper_order) validates them only by length via _bounded() (128 and 512 characters) — no phrase match. src/trading_research/cli.py:361-372 external_paper_submit_cli passes any operator/reason through unchanged. Enforcement of 'no automated paper submission' relies on the external_broker.enabled/allow_order_submission gates (which default false, ENFORCED) and on procedural discipline that the human operator supply the phrase — the phrase itself is not verified by code. If external_broker.enabled and allow_order_submission are both flipped true (a documented human decision), any 'operator' string would pass.

## 8. Migration-risk summary

The Find phase (dimension `db-migration`) produced dedicated findings; see F-* entries tagged `db-migration` above. Cross-check with `tests/fixtures/schema_history/*` which contains fixtures for milestone-11, milestone-11-1, and pre-milestone-11 schemas.

Key migration observations captured in findings:
- Semantic backfills for external-paper reservations (recent commits `03c8a24`, `5b92da1`, `47c4cbf`).
- Cross-day retry budget transfer atomicity (recent commit `ceb75fc`).
- Point-in-time source-timestamp inclusion in strategy signal identity (recent commit `09aba30`, `88be592`).

## 9. Test-gap plan (prioritized)

Gaps consolidated from the `testing-quality`, `crash-recovery`, `concurrency-tx`, and `db-migration` dimensions of the Find phase. See the corresponding F-* entries for the specific test files or missing coverage each recommendation points to. Highest-leverage additions:

1. Crash-point tests around reservation / submission / settlement for external paper (before-reservation, after-reservation-before-request, after-request-before-response, after-response-before-persistence).
2. Restart tests that verify no lost budget accounting across process boundary.
3. Cross-day tests: reservation transitioning across UTC day, cross-day retry budget transfer.
4. Multi-connection SQLite concurrency tests (avoid single-connection fixtures for concurrency claims).
5. Migration tests that begin from each historical schema and validate upgrade + rerun idempotency.
6. Explicit "provider was NOT called" / "broker was NOT called" assertions in unit tests that mock adapters.
7. Property-based tests for the $50 / $150 / 10%-per-ticker invariants.

## 10. Recommended remediation sequence (isolated PRs)

Group findings by severity + subsystem to keep PRs small. Concrete PR shells are derived from finding clusters:

- **PR-1 (High):** Close reservation/settlement race and INSERT OR IGNORE conflict-hiding cases. Finding IDs: (all High-severity items scoped to `execution/` and `paper_books/`; see F-001..F-011). Files: `src/trading_research/execution/*.py`, `src/trading_research/paper_books/*.py`.
- **PR-2 (High):** Enforce pre-research portfolio filter parity with final execution risk check. Files: `src/trading_research/execution/eligibility.py`, `src/trading_research/execution/config.py`, related pre-filter modules.
- **PR-3 (Medium):** Migration-safety hardening. Files: schema files under `src/trading_research/**` and fixtures under `tests/fixtures/schema_history/`.
- **PR-4 (Medium):** Point-in-time timestamp completeness in signal identity (extend beyond recently merged fix). Files: `src/trading_research/hashing.py`, strategy signal modules.
- **PR-5 (Medium):** Token / cost reservation atomicity across research paths; ensure no fresh-research bypass exists.
- **PR-6 (Medium):** CI + security hardening. Files: `.github/workflows/*`, add pinned actions, required lint/type/security jobs.
- **PR-7 (Low):** Documentation reconciliation: adjust README + milestone claims to reflect current code (Doc dimension findings).

Each PR should ship with the regression tests listed in the corresponding finding blocks.

| Deterministic offline strategies | READY | none blocking; watch F-* Medium items in `strategy-correctness` and `backtest-correctness` |
| Historical backtesting | READY | none blocking; watch F-* items in `backtest-correctness` |
| Daily read-only candidates | READY | none blocking |
| Fresh automated research | READY (supervised) | address F-* items in `model-token-cost` before broadening |
| Local paper book | READY | address F-* Medium items in `concurrency-tx` |
| Supervised external paper | READY | address High-severity items in `financial-safety` and `crash-recovery` (see F-001..F-011) |
| Multi-day paper soak | BLOCKED | pending resolution of crash-recovery Highs and reservation invariants |
| Automated paper scheduling | KEEP_DISABLED | requires closure of all High severity items; scheduler currently disabled by default |
| Live trading | KEEP_DISABLED | structurally unavailable; DO NOT enable until all High and reservation-tx Medium items closed |
